# v2026.10.10

> **Draft for review**
>
> This is the proposed full release page, not a release announcement or a merge-ready page. Complete source and credit checks, release-wide accounting and independent final review remain pending. No final release totals are claimed here.
>
> The proposed code cutoff is [06d598c818155a5ac6ad00c9611bbb4b6ed57d45](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/06d598c818155a5ac6ad00c9611bbb4b6ed57d45), a release-branch snapshot rather than a claimed final tag.

OpenClaw 2026.10.10 helps you follow your agent’s work, offers an optional second opinion while it runs, and lets supported interrupted conversations pick up where they left off.

## Keep track of your agent’s work

See progress, background tasks and project changes beside your chat, so you can follow the work while continuing the conversation. The Details panel brings that information together on wide screens and stays available through Panels on mobile.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and details</summary>

- Details gathers existing session information and progress in one panel. [#161674](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161674), closing [#161650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161650). Contributions from [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski). See [chat Details](/web/control-ui/chat) and [progress cards](/tools/progress-card).
- The session inspector groups existing information and actions into clearer sections. [#172436](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172436) addresses [#172429](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172429). Requested and design-reviewed by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). See [Control UI](/web/control-ui).
- Details docks beside wide conversations and stays available as an overlay in narrower panes. [#170707](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170707), co-authored by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Automation hovercards provide read-only inspection and a link to the selected job. [#172240](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172240), closing [#172236](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172236). Contribution from [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Show running and finished swarm workers in the Subagents panel, with view-only transcripts and automatic loading of older children. [#168162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168162). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The Layout menu collects pane controls, and a wait indicator links to active subagents. [PR #168845](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168845). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session Details moves into the chat header to avoid covering messages and their actions. [PR #168494](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168494). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Expanded activity shows nested tool calls again, and parked image and video previews release and reconnect their resources. [#169468](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169468), thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Personal conversation resume and agent activity indicators ([#170134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170134)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Removes the duplicate working indicator from the composer while retaining transcript progress and Stop ([#170211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170211)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the co-authorship.
- Total request time beside subagent activity and removal of the extra composer wait line ([#170325](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170325)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Wait time after a parent update or reload counts from the latest completed parent turn ([#170397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170397)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Show saved commentary and the Interrupted label for inactive runs that end after tool use without a final reply. [#170705](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170705) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Related changes cover [saved streamed answers](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170697) and [recovery markers](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170699).
- Retry and fallback progress, settled failure notices, and recovery actions ([#170820](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170820), [reported issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170798)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restore a saved failure notice and its actions after a reload or reconnect during a run ([#170971](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170971)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep Subagents closed until opened, while retaining inline child activity ([#171167](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171167)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Details opens populated pull request and automation sections, hides confirmed-empty sections and preserves manual collapse. [#171533](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171533) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep activity summaries consistent when expanded and make their source links available while collapsed. [#172573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172573). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Keep saved agent progress visible during tool runs and after reloading an active session. [#172585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172585). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Put token counts before elapsed time in chat status and the completed-turn recap. [#172549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172549). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Keep sessions awaiting approval on one line in the sidebar while retaining their approval indicator. [#172618](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172618). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Show a workspace label or directory name in session Details while preserving explicit full-path copying. [#172657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172657). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Adjust the Details layout around transcript actions and the landscape question footer, followed by removal of the extra transcript gap ([commit 320b088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/320b0884c23e40c8765e5417c42b9636d7133a4b), [commit dc0ef48](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/dc0ef48154f58877a50b29d2f9bc575420fccbb7), [commit 7e256f7](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7e256f7302d401bcbfa41347bf3fe2b88a729090)).
- Preserve authored task titles in opt-in failure narration for OpenClaw-prefixed MCP exec calls, [#168132](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168132). Thanks to [ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559).
- Hide raw exec completion notices from Control UI chat while retaining them for agent continuation, [#168236](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168236). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Avoid a false Claude CLI tool-start event when interrupted input is replaced by text, [#168578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168578). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Include background title, recap and plugin model completions in request counts, outcomes, traces and durations, [#168411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168411). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Keep completed global-scope commands visible to their owner in Processes in [#169302](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169302). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Control UI keeps subagent wait indicators and controls accurate while overlapping child-list reads finish. [#167672](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167672). Thanks to [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Claude CLI clients can show counts-only edit progress during streamed tool input, before execution starts. [#167789](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167789), [#167742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167742). Thanks to [ZengWen-DT](https://github.com/ZengWen-DT), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [umeranjum17](https://github.com/umeranjum17).
- Show current tool activity and exact or lower-bound call counts in running subagent rows. [PR #169719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169719). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Show unfinished tool calls as pending in built-in session forks and retain completed results in later forks. [#171580](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171580), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

</details>

## Get a second opinion while your agent works

The optional Advisor can notice when an agent drifts from your request, repeats unproductive work or claims a result it has not established, then suggest a correction while the task is still underway. Enable it in Settings → Agents & Tools → Labs. It is experimental and off by default, adds model usage and can give mistaken advice; it cannot grant permissions or guarantee that every problem will be caught.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and details</summary>

- Experimental Advisor can review ongoing agent work and offer checked corrective advice. Enable it in Settings → Agents & Tools → Labs. It is off by default, uses additional model calls and can make model-dependent mistakes. [#169564](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169564) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

</details>

## Pick up work after an interruption

A restart or brief device disconnection no longer has to mean starting the whole task again. Supported conversations can recover saved progress, keep partial answers visible and return completed work from helper agents to the original conversation. Cancelled work stays cancelled, and tools whose outcome is uncertain are not blindly run again. Recovery still depends on the original conversation, available records and current access.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and details</summary>

- One-time chat continuation after node loss, with saved transcript and workspace recovery. Direct agent RPC turns are excluded. [#171867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171867) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Clearer node-disconnection errors and guidance to check possible side effects before retrying. [#171695](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171695) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Kept node workspace retries within their elapsed-time budget after disconnects and clock changes ([#171977](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171977)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Preserved SSH worker and workspace operation timeouts across clock changes ([#172256](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172256)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Kept remote attachment transfer timeouts from growing after a backward clock change ([#173142](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173142)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Kept node invocation progress heartbeats on schedule across clock changes ([#182481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182481)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Kept capacity-blocked node worker launches within their existing wait budget across clock changes ([#184042](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184042)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Waited for native worker attachment before the first session turn ([#177184](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177184)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prevented remote node commands from inheriting misleading service identity ([#173304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173304), [#171542](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171542)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini).
- Preserved node worker failure causes in redacted local warnings ([#174750](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174750), [#173351](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/173351)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@unknown7751](https://github.com/unknown7751) and [@Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot).
- After repeated restarts, ready parent continuations no longer wait behind certain interrupted subagent deliveries. [#184100](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184100). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Settled failed subagent deliveries stop appearing as outstanding work in parent context, while pending work remains visible. [PR #155019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155019), [issue #154834](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/154834). Thanks to [linhongyu510](https://github.com/linhongyu510) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), with the report from [jerryliang122](https://github.com/jerryliang122), investigation by [ifyouhavethisemail-afk](https://github.com/ifyouhavethisemail-afk), [assimetria-ai](https://github.com/assimetria-ai), and [waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault), and related reports from [itanyplus](https://github.com/itanyplus) and [rozenpl](https://github.com/rozenpl).
- Authorized Claude CLI resumes carry bounded recorded subagent completions into follow-up turns. Account boundaries that cannot authorize replay receive metadata only. [#164175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164175), [#171195](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171195), [#159512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159512), [#154716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/154716). Thanks to [etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [potterdigital](https://github.com/potterdigital), [synapse-homeroot](https://github.com/synapse-homeroot), [cassthebandit](https://github.com/cassthebandit), [holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen), [oldrich333](https://github.com/oldrich333) and [xdom-jr](https://github.com/xdom-jr).
- Final replies from opted-in plugin tools wait for sibling tool calls to finish, keeping errors and unfinished work visible. [#165291](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165291), [#164558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164558). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Codex sessions recognize still-pending child agents started on earlier turns. [#167368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167368), [#167354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167354), [#160737](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160737), [#167277](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167277). Thanks to [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Subagent cancellation, recovery and cleanup check recorded ownership and session identity. Ambiguous watches and incomplete visible spawns report an error. [#167966](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167966). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Let visible subagents started during a parent turn reach their configured model and fallback sequence. [PR #169984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169984). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prevent independently launched `sessions_send` and subagent turns from inheriting the caller's transcript writer. [PR #170077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170077), [issue #166734](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166734). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [mariorivas1](https://github.com/mariorivas1) and [ghbalf](https://github.com/ghbalf).
- Deliver blocking [agent questions](/tools/ask-user) to the originating routed conversation. Credential questions retain their existing handling. [PR #170039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170039). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prevent successful session completions from being rejected after their own writes and keep queued replies eligible for retry. [PR #169646](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169646). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Let Codex plugins track outer tool calls until their recorded outputs arrive, helping them avoid treating an active run as idle. [#171661](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171661), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Continue protected conversations and private completions while child work is active, with current tool permissions preserved. [#174900](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174900) Thanks to [@enki](https://github.com/enki), [@Ricardodpalmeida](https://github.com/Ricardodpalmeida), [@Stronggenetics](https://github.com/Stronggenetics), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stop permanently undeliverable Codex child completions from returning after registration or restart. `openclaw doctor --fix` can repair proven duplicate or obsolete assignments. [#173018](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173018) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jekmanis](https://github.com/jekmanis), and [@gisk0](https://github.com/gisk0).
- Retry cleanup of completed Codex subagent subscriptions after a temporary failure while protecting active follow-ups. [#173547](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173547) Thanks to [@Kimiyu-186](https://github.com/Kimiyu-186).
- A parent can resume after all native children finish while their results await delivery. [#175246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175246) Thanks [@quqi1599](https://github.com/quqi1599), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Stronggenetics](https://github.com/Stronggenetics).
- After a restart interrupts a quiet subagent, affected parents can receive a turn to reconcile unfinished work. [#175278](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175278) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Codex native children can make model requests after startup without inheriting an expired startup context. [#176311](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176311), [#162119](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162119), [#165739](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/165739) Thanks [@enki](https://github.com/enki).
- Codex sessions can reply after recovering completed native children without conflicting with their own pending recovery writes. [#177564](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177564) Thanks [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen).
- Parents can resume after a native child's follow-up finishes while its result awaits acknowledgment. [#177577](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177577), [#172837](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172837) Thanks [@linancn](https://github.com/linancn) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Completed nested Codex work no longer leaves a stale assignment that repeats a warning after restart. [#179050](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179050), [#178395](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/178395) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@mmartoccia](https://github.com/mmartoccia).
- An interrupted native child can remain resumable without blocking an idle parent from replying. [#179099](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179099) Thanks [@wtfichigo](https://github.com/wtfichigo) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- When a configuration or policy change encounters a busy Codex thread, model fallback stops and the existing binding and configuration remain available. [#181204](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181204), [#181046](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181046) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@stronggenetics](https://github.com/stronggenetics) and [@jason-allen-oneal](https://github.com/jason-allen-oneal).
- A recovered subagent continuation can finish silently when its results have already been delivered, avoiding a duplicate final answer in that restart scenario. [#181260](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181260) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep a completed child result for an authorized in-place Resume after restart. Replacing the parent session does not deliver the old result to the new conversation. [#182587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182587) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Finish cleanup when a native child ends while its parent is waiting for an inline reply, allowing descendant settlement to complete. [#181933](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181933) Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Let Claude CLI parent replies finish when nested work belongs to a subagent, while the parent's own background work still holds the reply open. [#182267](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182267) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@goslingmanagment](https://github.com/goslingmanagment).
- Route nested Claude CLI background-agent results according to whether the launcher consumed or forwarded them, preserving the final answer in both cases. [#182516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182516) Thanks [@goslingmanagment](https://github.com/goslingmanagment), [@dmsrg399](https://github.com/dmsrg399) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduce unnecessary idle maintenance work when completed subagent runs are retained. [#181560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181560) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Queue new turns and side questions at the existing 64-root inference limit. Waiting adds latency, and parents holding all roots while waiting for children can still stall until a root is freed. [#181895](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181895) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve ACP input and completion notifications during session and dispatch races in [af03aa0](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/af03aa072fc53ae697dcc2baba7772c6e6fe4eba). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep discarded Claude subagent records from exhausting the parent reply budget in [#184352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184352). Retained output and the existing line and frame limits still count. Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [jaxonparrott](https://github.com/jaxonparrott).
- Report conflicting subagent continuations and observe detached cleanup failures in [#185386](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185386). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retain recovery notifications while a main session is awaiting preparation ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2251880c0f1df0cc22c56d73c7b6f8a57046ec59)).
- Preserve eligible nested Claude CLI background task answers across snapshot orderings ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8bf0e03aa413f53694def4fad3b2d4e79e2c726d)). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Contain quota suspension lookup and marker-write failures so later work can proceed ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2fdae7f27e20089ed7f3ef62a800be934f965a28)).
- Subagent completion can report `tokens unknown` without waiting for late usage statistics. If a parent resets during spawn and the child is denied memory access because of stale lineage, spawn it again from the current parent session. [#170400](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170400), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restart recovery leaves active subagent result delivery and cleanup to finish. [#170486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170486), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- After a restart, a replaced child no longer leaves its old task unresolved. The original parent receives a private notice, and parent reset leaves the replacement child alone. [#170513](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170513), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Startup combines legacy subagent authority warnings into a single summary with a count and sample IDs. [#170506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170506), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Completed subagent launches release retained authorization resources and parent context. [#170855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170855), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Subagent failure logs retain useful errors, and progress-only output is no longer counted as a visible final reply. [#170959](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170959). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Subagent list and wait avoid scanning unrelated retained runs. [#170998](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170998). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restart recovery selects the relevant subagent children without repeatedly scanning the full registry. [#171067](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171067), with related startup work in [#171046](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171046). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Completed interrupted subagent cleanup is not repeated on restart. [#171091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171091). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Completed child-result batches do not rerun the requester, and interrupted work resumes only when its original authority and child batch are available. [#171158](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171158). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Recover eligible accepted agent-to-agent updates after a restart, in order, without rerunning the sender. Cancelled updates and updates from reset, archived, replaced or access-revoked sessions are not automatically delivered. [#171717](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171717), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Return visible child completions and failures to a yielded guest session. [#171796](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171796), thanks to [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) for the fix and [skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223) for investigating the [reported handoff problem](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171647).
- Avoid retrying a subagent result that was already delivered when receipt recording fails, and retain useful errors for failed handoffs. [#171657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171657), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gave human browser-chat follow-ups in retained child sessions their own recovery record. [#172017](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172017). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Bounded large subagent completion batches and marked truncated findings. [#172068](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172068). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171864).
- Kept stopped requester turns cancelled when a subagent-result settlement is retried. [#172286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172286). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Continues eligible interrupted turns after session initialization or workspace preparation finishes. [#168650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168650) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Recovers eligible interrupted main chats when a shutdown marker was missed and reports archived recovery entries more clearly. [#168651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168651) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Return interrupted delegated work to its original parent, including quiet children and nested parents, while preserving pending final replies and respecting cancellation and reset. [#169144](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169144), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve the sender label on replayed inter-session messages, including forwarded messages. [#169130](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169130), thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Restart recovery retains eligible queued or preparing turns. Delegated work still needs reconstructable sender authority. If recovery stops, WebChat offers **Resume in new session** and other channels can use `/new` or `/reset`. [PR #170384](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170384). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retain restart recovery for a canceled main-session run whose cleanup finishes before its recovery record is saved ([#170699](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170699)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Continue eligible main dashboard turns interrupted by a backend restart drain ([#170858](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170858)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep unread watcher context for an ordinary turn after restart without creating replay turns or outbound replies ([#170781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170781)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Wait for pending worker placement before admitting a chat, retain interrupted-run recovery information, and allow selected trace diagnostics after a native reply. Session and worktree handling also avoid repeated work ([#170860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170860), [#170845](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170845)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Recovered turns use a background lane so they do not fill the interactive lane. [#171242](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171242), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve partial output and continue eligible replies interrupted by a Gateway restart. [#172234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172234). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Record accepted unfinished replies for recovery before restart shutdown cancels them. [#172412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172412). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Recover eligible authenticated CLI and API tasks after steering and repeated restarts while rechecking current authority. [#172356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172356). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep the reason for permanent restart-event admission failures and stop retrying those failures indefinitely. [#172322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172322). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Return the established authority error when a recovery continuation is rejected after its authority closes. [#172195](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172195), related to [#172183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172183). Thanks to [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Reuse plugin-managed grant authority during recovered operator runs while continuing policy and revocation checks. [#172482](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172482). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Delegated work remains eligible to resume when its completion check meets a Gateway shutdown window. [#172852](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172852), thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- After an unresumable delegated turn is retired during restart recovery, a conversation can accept a fresh authenticated message. The interrupted work is not replayed, and outstanding delivery or execution work can still block recovery. [#176682](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176682). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@enki](https://github.com/enki), and [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333). [@SamuelThornburg](https://github.com/SamuelThornburg) supplied an additional lab reproduction.
- Recover eligible delegated child work and inline follow-ups after restart without duplicate completion notices. Missing execution records or grants cannot be recreated ([#181373](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181373)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restored child and descendant work waits until it is ready to continue instead of blocking later user messages. [#184022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184022) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@borclaw](https://github.com/borclaw) for the report.
- Let later messages proceed after restart while a restored child remains paused ([#185999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185999)). Thanks to [@vacinc](https://github.com/vacinc).
- Preserve the restart cancellation reason when a reply finishes during a session restart ([45e2bced](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/45e2bcedec8f0561a409faeeff4034327f4dba71)).

</details>

## Speak to the same agent you use in chat

Browser Talk can use your current chat agent with your chosen speech and transcription services, including local transcription. You can talk instead of type while keeping that agent’s tools and ordinary approvals. This split mode works alongside existing realtime Talk, but does not support realtime voice switching or camera input.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and details</summary>

- Control UI speech uses configured services and split Talk uses the current agent. [#170343](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170343), [#45508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/45508), [#113049](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/113049). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@mcfex](https://github.com/mcfex) for the self-hosted speech report and [@mike-nott](https://github.com/mike-nott) for the browser Talk report.
- Accepted Talk work can finish after a client failure. [#171377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171377), [#171310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171310). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the fix and [@jonathanlindsay](https://github.com/jonathanlindsay) for the report.
- Authenticated Talk uses ordinary permissions and approvals. [#159697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159697). Thanks [@brokemac79](https://github.com/brokemac79).
- Spoken Talk requests can reach the agent after voice connection setup finishes. [#170083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170083). Thanks [@brokemac79](https://github.com/brokemac79).
- Speech-only heartbeat replies can reach synthesis and delivery, and later OpenAI Completions turns can see the spoken answer. [#170357](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170357), [#170027](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170027), [#170062](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170062). Thanks [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Twilight-Networks](https://github.com/Twilight-Networks) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for the replay probe.
- Repeated Talk questions after tool use keep valid message ordering and instruction updates on affected Anthropic routes. [#171267](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171267), [#171227](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171227). Thanks [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@marleyman5000-boop](https://github.com/marleyman5000-boop).
- Browser Talk saves Gemini 3.8 Live and Extended Thinking spoken questions in conversation history after Stop and reload. The fix does not restore previously discarded speech. [#171506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171506), [#171505](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171505). Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Empty audio transcripts are treated as silence instead of a provider failure, allowing configured fallbacks to continue. [#171514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171514), [#171512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171512). Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Captioned final speech text avoids duplication when paragraph separators differ. [#181161](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181161), fixing [#181070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181070). Thanks [@mushuiyu886](https://github.com/mushuiyu886), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@julianloperar](https://github.com/julianloperar).

</details>

## Work with files where they already live

Start a conversation in a folder on a compatible paired computer without copying its files to the Gateway or turning the folder into a Git repository. Choose Existing folder on device in New Session. The device controls which folders are allowed, and files stay there when the session ends. These conversations stay on that device and do not provide cloud dispatch, Gateway Files or attachments.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and details</summary>

- Device-folder access follows `nodeHost.workerRuns.folders`. Leaving it unset permits existing directories, configured roots restrict access, and an empty list disables the option. [#169722](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169722) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

</details>

## Find earlier notes without their exact wording

Memory search can find notes from conversational questions when exact text and filename searches miss them. Relevant older notes stay in consideration, and notes closer to your meaning can rank above weak keyword matches. If you have tuned a minimum search score, you may need to lower it after the ranking change. These improvements do not guarantee exact recall.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and details</summary>

- Natural-language questions can recover notes through a fallback when strict body and filename searches find nothing, and hybrid search can score keyword matches outside the initial vector window. [#161165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161165), with thanks to [wangjx-xydt](https://github.com/wangjx-xydt), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini) and [AXEG0](https://github.com/AXEG0). See [#160839](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160839) and [#162764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162764).
- Relevant older dated notes remain eligible before recency is applied to their ranking. [#168692](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168692), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [DrDanielSingleton](https://github.com/DrDanielSingleton), [S1gv4rd](https://github.com/S1gv4rd), [davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini), [rkcollins](https://github.com/rkcollins) and [h6y3](https://github.com/h6y3). See [#157392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157392).
- Keyword relevance remains distinct when dated notes decay, with changed hybrid scores for tuned thresholds. [#167004](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167004), with thanks to [MonkeyLeeT](https://github.com/MonkeyLeeT), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [AXEG0](https://github.com/AXEG0) and [n0mad-ai](https://github.com/n0mad-ai). See [#162764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162764).
- Eligible standalone headings stay with following content in built-in memory search. [#168867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168867), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [TomSchuman](https://github.com/TomSchuman) and [maximilliangrand](https://github.com/maximilliangrand). See [#149758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/149758).
- Agents can answer from sufficient supplied context and retrieve memory when facts are missing. [#121115](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/121115), with thanks to [arhxam](https://github.com/arhxam), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [shannon0430](https://github.com/shannon0430) and [petergaultney](https://github.com/petergaultney). See [#120968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/120968).
- In eligible private chats, French questions about past discussions can trigger Active Memory escalation when the initial lookup is weak. [#171370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171370), thanks to [RaphazZze](https://github.com/RaphazZze). See the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171369).
- Hybrid memory search gives weaker keyword matches less weight against semantic relevance. Tuned minimum scores can exclude lower-scoring results, and some rare identifiers or filename matches can change order ([#177450](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177450)). The related [exact-recall report #162764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162764) remains open. Thanks [@serg0x](https://github.com/serg0x), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [@axeg0](https://github.com/axeg0).

</details>

## Before updating

- If you manage Node yourself, use Node 24.22.1 or newer within Node 24, or Node 26.12.1 or newer, for the certificate-security updates. See [Node requirements](/install/node-compatibility).
- Keep a verified backup before database upgrades if you may need to roll back. Restore the matching backup and older OpenClaw build together; rolling back the program alone does not roll back your data. See [backup and recovery](/cli/backup).
- Review conversations and raw local logs before sharing them because they can contain credentials. Incognito keeps OpenClaw conversation content in memory, but Codex-runtime submissions may still be saved locally; use the OpenClaw runtime if that logging is unacceptable. Providers, tools and plugins may retain their own data.
- Some older Mac pnpm installations need a [one-time manual upgrade](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171797). Restore any truncated multiline environment values before reinstalling the service.
- Matrix shared-account installations should stop older writers and upgrade together. The [unsafe-save recovery procedure](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160707) can lose keys received since the last successful snapshot.

  - Personal navigation rail, shortcuts and Mine and All session scopes, with access still governed by existing permissions. [PR #168325](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168325), [design issue #168053](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168053). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Personalize the rail with draggable shortcuts and a single Sessions owner filter. [#168997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168997) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - The personal navigation rail starts clean using a separate shortcut store. Older stored values and unrelated settings remain in place. [#169447](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169447), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Saved pin labels stay visible while details refresh, and busy sidebar updates are coalesced. Missing or failed pins remain saved but are hidden. [#169680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169680), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - Find matching conversations when search words are separated by standalone punctuation or non-indexed symbols. [#168117](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168117). Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  - Match equivalent Unicode forms in session title search and command-palette ranking ([#184374](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184374)). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- File searches tell you when nothing matches instead of leaving a blank panel.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Show an empty-search message in filtered Files views instead of a blank panel. [#169114](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169114) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Copy link is easier to reach in session menus, and New conversation stays available across sidebar views and on mobile.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Session menus bring Copy link and eligible owner assignment to the top and group less-used actions under Advanced. [PR #168616](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168616), [broader menu issue #132846](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/132846). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the new conversation action available across sidebar views and on mobile. [#170771](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170771), [request #170744](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170744). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the design direction and request.

  </details>

- Conversations receive automatic names earlier without overwriting your chosen name.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Start automatic session naming earlier while preserving explicit names and manual renames. [#170794](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170794) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Open the command palette with Cmd+P or Ctrl+P, alongside the existing shortcut.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - The command palette accepts platform-specific P and Shift+P shortcuts alongside the existing K shortcut. [#169943](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169943), thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).

  </details>

- Short conversation views automatically load some earlier messages, with manual loading still available for more history.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Earlier messages load automatically when a visible conversation is underfilled. [#171529](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171529) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Automatic history filling stops after three older pages per opening, with manual loading still available. [#171593](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171593) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce history-read work on long, marker-heavy conversations. [#172566](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172566). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Switch the Users sidebar between people who are online and all users.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Switch the Users sidebar between Online and All users. [#172243](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172243), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Unsent drafts, attachments and queued follow-ups stay with the intended conversation through more navigation and connection changes. Check history before resending a message whose delivery is uncertain.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Change saved model and reasoning preferences during a reply for unstarted queued work. The picker shows the saved preference, and explicit per-message reasoning choices take precedence. [#160440](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160440), [#160428](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160428). Thanks [@hxy91819](https://github.com/hxy91819) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Type, attach files, and queue follow-ups while a new chat starts. They follow the initial prompt in order, and failed creation keeps the input for retry. [#165091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165091). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - In a connected Control UI chat, typing `/stop` cancels work and clears queued follow-ups in the selected non-global session. The Stop button and offline typed stops remain specific to the active run. [#167860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167860), [#167859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167859). Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep files attached to `/steer` and `/redirect` messages, including for retry after a rejected submission. [#165105](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165105), [#165104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/165104). Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Confirmed queued messages clear a false delivery warning while uncertain messages continue to warn. [PR #168284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168284). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Large WebChat images are staged so workspace file tools can open them, including in sandboxes. [PR #168537](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168537), [issue #103198](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/103198). Thanks to [@synthalorian](https://github.com/synthalorian), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [@woohahahaaa](https://github.com/woohahahaaa).
  - Messages with surrounding whitespace use the same normalized text on first inference and later replay. [PR #168725](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168725). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Chat preparation reuses fresh session facts to reduce repeated database and worker reads. [PR #168316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168316). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid false media errors and duplicate file cards when a WebChat attachment was already committed. Previously cached duplicates are not rewritten. [#169093](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169093) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prevent duplicate history entries for prompts with inline directives. Existing duplicate transcripts are not rewritten. The related [issue #168843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168843) covers a separate problem. [#169434](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169434) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Retain prepared model catalogs and pending Settings edits during preference changes. RPC clients without editable drafts can request a compact configuration patch acknowledgement. [#169253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169253) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show an error after repeated preference-save conflicts while retaining the edit for a later retry or reconnect. [#169367](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169367) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Browser connections wait for a valid Gateway challenge, and pending session creation and dictation stay tied to the correct draft. [#169692](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169692), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An accepted message without a confirmation ID reports unknown on the first response and replay without sending again. [#169650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169650), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Offline queued messages retain individual reconnect badges without a duplicate count band ([#170352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170352)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Accepted chat input and its interaction time are saved together before acknowledgment ([#170250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170250)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Removes an extra writer-queue wait when acquiring chat input for an already-admitted native database ([#170342](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170342)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Durable acknowledgment for eligible fresh idle sends after one input commit ([#170376](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170376)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep an original request above a later steering message while a new conversation is being set up. [#170723](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170723) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Acknowledge chat sends sooner when unrelated database writes are in progress ([#170816](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170816)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject edited retries using the same request key for newly fingerprinted messages, including after in-memory state is lost ([#171174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171174), [reported issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171173)). Older records and records created without an available device identity retain the earlier comparison. If the identity changes and a retry conflicts, check the conversation history before sending with a new key. Thanks [@SeanLiu326](https://github.com/SeanLiu326) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Warm conversations reuse session information to reduce database work. [#171337](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171337) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show current queue labels after reconnecting while an earlier unconfirmed send remains paused for review. [#171866](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171866), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Remove an empty second composer after an interrupted session start while keeping any actual follow-up draft. [#172318](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172318), thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Refresh applicable filtered sidebars promptly after session creation and clear accepted prompts and attachments from the draft. Failed creation and newer unsent drafts remain available. [#171768](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171768), thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) for reporting and directing the fix.
  - Keep a prompt sent immediately after stopping a preparing turn in the queue so it can run after the stopped turn settles. Recovering a queued reply also keeps its chat identity. [#172685](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172685), fixing [#172658](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172658). Thanks to [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) for the report and fix.
  - Avoid false storage warnings, unwanted retries and restored drafts after a queued message has already been retired ([#186171](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186171)). Thanks [@syxscott](https://github.com/syxscott) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Close stale command and skill menus after a submitted draft clears, while keeping menus for retained or newer drafts ([#184869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184869)). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
  - Restored pasted-text chips, attachment previews and capability-menu interactions in the composer. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0c8464cf802c2f7ca62f2bc0def9303f98d0dde1)
  - Settled accepted terminal-state writes before releasing canceled turns. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/04cb1cbfab59e5dda6db7ee56ab0ab1be4500f75)
  - Loaded persisted transcript state when an append needs a retry and the session has no in-memory snapshot. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e65bd3be4134f1453bb4ab26e68704c678086b58)
  - The chat integration includes timing information for slow sends and a shortcut for empty standing-intent matches. [Merge](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/210340a16a91aa77b4b1cb61a4d8a18a80ae6ba9)
  - [Queued follow-up recovery](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/333b2e303bee777b8882f9139e32629953ee79d1), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Fixes affected replies appearing twice, losing finished text or arriving out of order during streaming and recovery.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Deliver eligible background completions to their original WebChat conversation without an external messaging target. [#158067](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158067), [#147387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/147387). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and reporter [@oywino](https://github.com/oywino).
  - Avoid a false WebChat error when a Claude CLI turn has already delivered its final answer through the message tool. [#167736](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167736), [#167709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167709); related separate report [#169614](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169614). Thanks [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Hide recognized serialized tool-call markup from streamed and final replies while keeping surrounding text and literal code examples. [#138204](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138204), [#138198](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138198), [#153594](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153594). Thanks [@ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), [@ruiling-smartbear](https://github.com/ruiling-smartbear), and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep an authorized channel message in history when required preflight compaction fails, so a later turn can see it. The compaction error still appears. [#160462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160462), [#86592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/86592). Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and reporter [@aacodex401](https://github.com/aacodex401).
  - Retain the original message or tool result when a plugin returns a non-message replacement and normal plugin error handling applies. Configured fail-closed handling still applies. [#161098](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161098), [#160641](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160641). Thanks [@xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and reporter [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80).
  - Let implicit group thread follow-ups remain silent when group silence is allowed, without a misleading missing-response retry. Explicit mentions and commands still require replies. [#164877](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164877), [#141556](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141556); related separate report [#160750](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160750). Thanks [@shannon0430](https://github.com/shannon0430), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [@zabealbe](https://github.com/zabealbe).
  - Live replies and saved history stay in chronological order around worker updates. [PR #168250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168250). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A remaining chat observer continues receiving updates and narration after another observer unsubscribes. [#169693](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169693), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Newly committed message previews appear promptly in resident session lists ([#170105](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170105)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session branch operations during transcript reconciliation ([#170079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170079)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restart recovery for eligible human conversations with inter-session steering ([#170252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170252)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the request and co-authorship.
  - Preserve a main-session recovery claim when a follow-up arrives after interrupted work, so the earlier request can resume first. [#170647](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170647) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Load earlier messages when the recent history page contains only hidden activity. [#170731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170731) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for reporting the issue.
  - Focus the folder path and keep the save action reachable on short screens in group defaults. [#170566](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170566) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep new and previously saved failure notices out of later model requests ([#171327](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171327)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Observe late worker errors after cancellation and limit the raw size of default history pages. Oversized pages ask for a smaller limit, while an oversized message returns an error ([#170898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170898)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show a saved completed reply when its message event was missed. [#171766](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171766), thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) for reporting and co-authoring the fix.
  - Keep images with their headings in live web chat and reloaded history. [#172438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172438). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for raising the problem.
  - Hide stray reply actions on pre-tool progress messages while retaining actions for eligible answers. [#172620](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172620). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
  - Include confirmed delivered final replies when session history rebuilds a conversation, addressing one cause of repeated Telegram responses. [#179399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179399), related to [#166482](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166482). Thanks to [@sobolevbel](https://github.com/sobolevbel), [@gkoch02](https://github.com/gkoch02), and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep side-question replies when different questions have the same answer and suppress a complete reply already sent by a messaging tool. [#174869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174869). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep CLI-backed group conversation context across room events and mentions when automatic replies are enabled. Visible room-event output still requires an explicit message-tool send ([#182273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182273), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182177)).   - Prevent a stray separator at the start of a reply after an empty native thinking-block segment ([commit 9931e6e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9931e6efb02e24b2541194f14b72a0354e838cfb)).
  - Preserved history paging, retry controls, parked content state and focus across chat updates. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/84fb3196ed56a5a74e389563b287770e041909db)
  - Prevented an empty linked-session preview from freezing navigation in web and native conversation views. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0407fd597c0d8f7e1905c3a78a6e2260fdde14ea) Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Removed managed media directives from assistant session previews while preserving literal and user-authored text and fallback-model attribution. Media-only replies have no text preview. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/47ba0ae081320861c3c032c3a57f2a24489e5588)
