[#171293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171293), reported in [#171197](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171197). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@arquiteto-raiz](https://github.com/arquiteto-raiz).
  - Interactive turns and manual compaction receive higher priority than background work when the per-model option is enabled. [PR #129434](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/129434). Thanks to [m888m](https://github.com/m888m) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The Neon guide covers existing custom-provider setup for its Chat Completions, Responses, and Anthropic Messages endpoints. [PR #113734](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/113734). Thanks to [mezotv](https://github.com/mezotv), [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), and [clarkbw](https://github.com/clarkbw) for review advice.
  - Status, context and session reports use the selected model and runtime capacity instead of a stale or guessed limit. [#165991](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165991). Thanks to [snotty](https://github.com/snotty) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore turn preparation for pinned native Codex sessions after their writer is claimed ([#169101](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169101)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Existing agent databases migrate to schema 26. Upgrade time grows with retained data and the number of agent databases. [#169315](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169315), [issue #169254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169254). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Shows labeled provider request time per credential in model connection checks and avoids creating an agent session for the check. [#169554](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169554), [issue #169066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169066). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@dabase](https://github.com/dabase).
  - Gives reasoning-capable utility models room to produce visible titles, observer digests and recaps within the configured and model limits. [#169331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169331), [issue #153391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153391). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@dwalthour](https://github.com/dwalthour), [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Adds paired request and response metadata to prompt-cache drop diagnostics without including prompt content or raw keys. Available fields depend on the transport. [#169630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169630) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Give response callbacks the HTTP status and headers of rejected OpenAI and Azure SDK requests. [PR #169688](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169688). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Processing and estimated wait are available in model-call diagnostics and Gateway stability output when the provider supplies valid final statistics. [#170369](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170369), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Uraroga](https://github.com/Uraroga) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137557).
  - OpenAI tier downgrade logs include response and account-route details that can be matched with cache-drop warnings. [#170949](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170949). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - History-free runs stop at an output limit without replaying their request, and model-call logs carry correlated timing and completion information. [#170981](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170981), following [#161082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161082). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Distinguish steering and queued follow-up waits from startup in slow `chat.send` logs and Prometheus metrics. [#169006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169006), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Worktree preparation reports why template reuse falls back. The first template still needs to be built. [#169239](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169239). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stall warnings no longer attribute later callbacks to completed startup, and channel account starts log their reason. [#169221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169221). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include a bounded, redacted error tail when spawn-broker shutdown fails ([#170829](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170829)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Slow pre-acknowledgement chat requests log admission and persistence subphase timings to help operators locate waits. [#171250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171250), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Add turn input and final model output to opted-in message, harness and run spans. Gemini CLI run-level composed prompts are excluded. [#156603](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156603), [related request #144294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/144294). Thanks to [ryan-dyer-sp](https://github.com/ryan-dyer-sp).
  - Extend OpenTelemetry message-duration histogram buckets through one hour. [#128785](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128785). Thanks to [patrick-erichsen](https://github.com/patrick-erichsen).
  - Report local worker deadlines as local timeouts instead of fabricating a provider HTTP 408, while retaining configured fallback. [#156650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156650), [reported in #155627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155627). Thanks to [YangManBOBO](https://github.com/YangManBOBO), [EndeavorPioneer](https://github.com/EndeavorPioneer), and [altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - Identify a confirmed foreign process holding the Gateway port in status and channel diagnostics, with guidance for resolving the conflict. [#161106](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161106), [reported in #161024](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161024). Thanks to [yashas-13](https://github.com/yashas-13), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - Direct automatic repair to check Gateway RPC before reporting recovery. [#167080](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167080). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Doctor prepares its runtime configuration before plugin checks, avoiding a false Codex registration error in non-interactive runs. [#170445](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170445). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Doctor's guide clarifies that standalone checks can run after an update before starting the Gateway. [#170550](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170550). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Routine successful Gateway activity moves to debug logs, and Prometheus gains egress and tool-catalog counters. [#170509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170509). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway stall captures handle quoted or escaped script names so later captures retain redacted stack attribution. [#170505](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170505). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway logs identify sidebar session-list requests as sidebar requests instead of full rows. [#170512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170512). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The Gateway startup banner shows the resolved thinking level when the default is off. This corrects the displayed setting. [#170627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170627). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for the fix and [chughtapan](https://github.com/chughtapan) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170180).
  - Logbook warns about a missing vision model when analysis work is waiting, rather than while its queue is empty. [#170608](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170608). Thanks to [duyufansh2008](https://github.com/duyufansh2008).
  - Avoid another Gateway-blocking heap scan when starting an operator CPU capture on Node 26 with the stall flight recorder already active. Other captures can still scan, and Bun uses the inspector backend. [#170932](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170932) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Add finer Prometheus histogram buckets for Gateway RPC latency estimates. The additional series increase scrape and render cost. [#171062](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171062) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Expose SQLite writer request phases in Prometheus, including time spent transferring framed requests and results. [#171082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171082) by [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Attribute more Git launches and count streaming workspace inventory launches in the child-process metric. [#171465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171465) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Identify more child-process launch owners, including agent execution and helpers. [#171582](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171582) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include collected objects in a Gateway heap profile with `includeCollected`. [#171686](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171686) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse validation results for unchanged plugin settings across configuration revisions. [#171592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171592) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Garbage collection metrics include collection kind and main-thread or instrumented worker labels. Workers without the telemetry connection and other unregistered isolates are outside this coverage. [#171923](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171923) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include underlying causes and storage codes in post-ready Gateway maintenance warnings. [#172138](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172138). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include queue wait and available holder information in slow agent database reservation warnings. [#172459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172459). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway and node service stop and uninstall errors include status and recovery hints in text and JSON output. [#176335](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176335). Thanks [@555aaditya](https://github.com/555aaditya) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Stale Gateway cleanup warnings include the reason cleanup failed. [#174590](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174590). Thanks [@shoaibulhaque](https://github.com/shoaibulhaque) and [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - Optional cache-trace and Anthropic payload diagnostic writers warn once when they cannot write their files, without stopping agent work. [#174622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174622). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jamie8johnson](https://github.com/jamie8johnson) and [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80).
  - Saved shutdown diagnostics retain process details and signaling errors when service-child cleanup times out. [#175625](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175625). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Linux service inspection surfaces an error when it cannot confirm that a systemd connection probe stopped. [#175689](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175689). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev) for the earlier inspection work.
  - Linux service inspection preserves systemd command cleanup failures so Gateway maintenance and update recovery can recognize unfinished work. [#175703](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175703). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway startup recovery preserves cleanup and ownership errors instead of reporting a missing service. [#175726](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175726). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Usage and session lists can refresh without waiting for as much unrelated background work.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Cold Usage partial reports leave uncached sessions pending without waiting for transcript scans ([#170093](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170093)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use less temporary memory for fresh ordered session pages. [#171968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171968), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Kept an empty usage refresh from creating an unused agent database and disturbing a session event handoff. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/927c8b25d016f4fb54ff55c9d6fa3fc004c23716) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Usage history rebuilds use compact monthly projections and yield to foreground writes. [#169816](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169816), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Usage reports can catch up after startup database preparation without another transcript update. [#169846](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169846), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Background usage totals refresh after a quiet period, while explicit usage and cost reads flush pending data. [#171378](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171378) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Requested session totals refresh ahead of subsequent unrelated bulk work. [#171480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171480) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Concurrent usage refreshes can commit while a report is being prepared. [#171433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171433) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce cold usage-refresh overhead for large session inventories and add more useful database worker diagnostics. [#171868](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171868), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Refresh background usage and estimated-cost totals according to elapsed time after a clock correction ([#184212](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184212)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Batch large session usage reads to reduce failures from worker input pressure during concurrent reports ([#183407](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183407)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse transcript preview facts for session lists and remember the next cron-session expiry. [#171449](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171449) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse unaffected session rows after unrelated plugin reloads while refreshing agents whose relevant settings changed. [#171519](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171519) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse session and catalog display data during repeated requests. [#171724](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171724) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid loading chat presentation code at session maintenance worker startup until it is needed. [#171697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171697) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Encode transcript messages once for broadcasts to multiple subscribers. [#171701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171701) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Improves health and status responsiveness while large fleets prepare session information. [#168649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168649) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT).
  - Moves agent-session preparation reads off the Gateway's main thread during concurrent requests. [#168755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168755) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduces database writer contention during transcript maintenance and names known long-running tasks in stall warnings. [#168801](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168801) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Release superseded session-list presentation data sooner to reduce retained Gateway memory during session changes. [#169056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169056), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Resolve session targets without waiting for unrelated display rows to rebuild or succeed. [#169082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169082), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Resolve full session IDs and labels from matching metadata without waiting for unrelated sessions. [#169148](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169148), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Catalog updates refresh affected agents' session information without rematerializing unrelated agents' sessions. [#169210](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169210). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session-list polling and creation reuse roster and model information where appropriate. [#169263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169263). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Committed session-list notifications can finish after their producing task ends, including queued Swarm launches. [PR #170231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170231). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Connected clients can receive committed session rows after the request that changed them finishes or is cancelled, instead of falling back to a list refresh. [PR #170281](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170281). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Project, sharing-member, and GitHub option pickers avoid redundant reads and overlap independent work. [PR #170324](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170324). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session lists and activity recaps retain less data for large session inventories. [#170517](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170517). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Return retained observed-project snapshots while discovery refreshes in the background. On a cold listing, session-only repositories appear after discovery ([#170887](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170887)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Previously discovered session catalogs return available pages while providers refresh. Cold discovery retains its wait budget. [#171263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171263), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session ID lookup loads only the selected saved entry and checks that it has not been replaced. [#171326](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171326), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session listing avoids redundant validation work on prepared rows. [#171731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171731) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Sidebar session-list packing uses fewer temporary allocations, following earlier list work in [#171724](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171724) and [#171731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171731). [#171860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171860) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse agent roster and row-target work during sidebar session-list selection. [#172474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172474). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce repeated allocations in session-list readiness checks. [#172485](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172485). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Saved-session `chat.metadata` requests with `includeModels=false` avoid waiting for unused worker placement information. [#175606](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175606). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Handle unreadable rows in session listings and release queued collector holds after their parent ends ([#187589](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187589)). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).

  </details>

- Typing your first prompt is more responsive when a large model catalog is loaded.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">

  - Reduce repeated work in the New Session cloud environment picker. [#170651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170651) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - New Session typing avoids repeated work while editing a draft. [#171511](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171511) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - New Session typing avoids repeated model work after the catalog loads. [#171530](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171530) Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

- Side chat and project pickers can open without waiting for unrelated session preparation.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Restore Side chat without waiting for unrelated session writes ([#170968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170968)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let project listings proceed during unrelated state writes ([#171069](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171069)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let project and session-catalog pickers respond without waiting for unrelated session row preparation after a restart. [#171714](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171714), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway client documentation explains how to reconcile an optional in-flight run snapshot after replaying cursor catch-up messages. [#171183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171183), thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [NianJiuZst](https://github.com/NianJiuZst).
  - Git repository identity workers avoid loading unrelated preparation code, reducing retained memory in the measured workload. [#171926](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171926) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Defer Git and package-manager installation discovery until a scheduled update check or explicit request, respecting update-check opt-outs ([#181983](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181983)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jonathanlindsay](https://github.com/jonathanlindsay), [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225) and [@Card4Philippe](https://github.com/Card4Philippe).

  </details>

- Speech and transcription choices apply their saved options more consistently. Fish Audio’s expressive Drama 3 preview remains opt-in, with the existing default unchanged.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Talk choices remain available during provider refresh. [#172556](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172556). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fish Audio supports the opt-in Drama 3 preview for speech and Talk, with one voice per request. [#170473](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170473). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Talk keeps a configured custom Azure Speech endpoint when only the voice is overridden. [#151253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151253). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Automatically discovered Python Whisper receives a configured or requested language hint. [#164841](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164841), [#164840](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164840). Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - GPT-Live subscription voice can use a valid pasted ChatGPT access token carrying an account ID for Voice Call, Discord and Talk. [#166211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166211). Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Talk voice reads and selections clean up authorization resources after success or error. [#170386](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170386). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Talk provider choices retain the selected speech provider's configuration during readiness checks. [#170412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170412). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Talk checks speech, transcription and realtime providers together when building the provider list. [#170806](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170806). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Talk provider choices avoid repeating realtime provider preparation within a request. [#171099](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171099). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Talk configuration reads reflect the applied configuration. Pending or rejected edits appear after a successful reload. [#171607](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171607). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Talk provider choices reuse transcription readiness and resolved audio models within a request. [#172538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172538). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - ElevenLabs batch transcription receives configured options, including explicit false values. [#177975](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177975), fixing [#168580](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168580). Thanks [@HeZ2z](https://github.com/HeZ2z), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [@exGeni](https://github.com/exGeni).
  - Talk speech and realtime provider keys accept different case or surrounding whitespace. Select a provider explicitly when more than one is configured. [Commit 4e1f951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4e1f9518dfaab09f128762ed689389cf9d8a0df9). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) and [@gideonpraise](https://github.com/gideonpraise).
  - DeepInfra and OpenRouter speech requests use a two-minute default timeout. Configured and per-call timeouts take precedence. [#175614](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175614). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Fixes affected spoken replies, captions and iPhone Talk playback, including an iPhone relay Talk crash on the first reply.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Attach delayed automatic speech to an existing answer, including after reload ([#171090](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171090), related [#170937](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170937)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

Thanks [@wangyan2026](https://github.com/wangyan2026), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@eiriktorheim](https://github.com/eiriktorheim) and [@vobornik](https://github.com/vobornik).

[#168416](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168416), report [#168415](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168415) and related [#167810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167810). Thanks [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and related reporter [nathan-85](https://github.com/nathan-85).
  - macOS Talk waits for the accepted run to finish and speaks its matching complete reply, including slow responses. [#164485](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164485), reports [#153145](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153145) and [#127558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127558). Thanks [PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [rsaeks](https://github.com/rsaeks).
  - iOS Talk microphone recovery notices and host-entry guidance are translated in 21 non-English locales. [#181265](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181265).

  </details>

- Approved work on paired computers survives more unrelated pairing changes, while actual connection and permission failures remain visible.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Show current pairing or connection failures during automatic placement instead of a misleading capacity error ([#168531](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168531)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Clear surviving owned child processes during forced node-worker stops so capacity can become available again ([#168746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168746), [report #160643](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160643)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), reporter [aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80), and [linhongyu510](https://github.com/linhongyu510) for the cleanup investigation.
  - Let scheduled paired-node workspace and worker-bundle maintenance finish when preparation outlasts its scheduling callback ([#168780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168780)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Correct approval ownership for routed worker commands while retaining node policy checks ([#148612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148612)). Thanks to [beenhero](https://github.com/beenhero) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Warn when a remote node background or yield request runs synchronously ([#156553](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156553), [report #156524](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156524)). Thanks to [NianJiuZst](https://github.com/NianJiuZst), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and reporter [service-cyber](https://github.com/service-cyber).
  - Correct missing plan identity that could reject an already approved remote-node command ([#161980](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161980), [report #151622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/151622), [related issue #142492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/142492)). Thanks to [Program-123](https://github.com/Program-123), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), reporters [gaojian125](https://github.com/gaojian125) and [enderwillsaveyou](https://github.com/enderwillsaveyou), and [rozenpl](https://github.com/rozenpl) and [daddydubzzz](https://github.com/daddydubzzz) for reproductions.
  - Avoid a duplicate finished notice when a synchronous node command already returned its output ([#164511](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164511), [related issue #154474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/154474)). Thanks to [holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and reporter [xiazixin](https://github.com/xiazixin).
  - Run authorized POSIX paired-node commands that end in whitespace ([#168120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168120)). Thanks to [yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Keep node commands, requests and events working when an unrelated pairing lookup or receipt reports a newer revision. [#169486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169486) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Related to [#166652](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166652).
  - Keep paired-node requests and events flowing while another device's pairing write is pending. Changes to the affected node's own authority still restrict its access. [#169575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169575) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [@gisk0](https://github.com/gisk0) for the diagnosis and alternative proposal.
  - Settle delegated `sessions_send` replies and workspace conflict reports in the target worker session instead of reporting a false writer-claim failure. [#168946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168946), addressing [#159801](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159801), by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Tell agents that paired-node directory browsing lists folders only, and suggest a file listing through node-host execution when permitted. [#169224](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169224) by [@PratikRai0101](https://github.com/PratikRai0101), with thanks to [@keiseisales](https://github.com/keiseisales) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169192).
  - Let cancelled node commands finish when a progress acknowledgment is delayed, so an idle node can update. [#169273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169273) by [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).
  - Allow Gateway shutdown and restart to proceed when a retained cloud worker is disconnected, with a warning that its physical cleanup is deferred. [#168907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168907) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce work during node-list refreshes for large paired-device rosters. [#171876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171876) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keeps the matching connected node current as its changed pairing permissions are approved. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/94f180d9ab19d969d59298312cff8ed019e18b58)

  </details>

- Eligible workspace moves keep their selected destination for retry. Moving an active remote session interrupts its turn without replaying it, and leaving an offline device requires confirmation.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Session menus expose moves between eligible local and remote destinations and preserve the destination when retrying a move that failed while the source was draining. The existing placement chip remains available. [#171690](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171690) · [Session lifecycle](/gateway/cloud-workers/session-lifecycle) · Contribution from [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Required worker profiles and draft recovery ([#158903](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158903)). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [sallyom](https://github.com/sallyom), and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Picker selections and smaller comparable defaults for unconfigured cloud profiles ([#156786](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156786)). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Preserve active turns during independent worker dispatch ([#155464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155464), [report #155333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155333)). Thanks to [bulingbuling688](https://github.com/bulingbuling688), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [rvelasquez](https://github.com/rvelasquez).
  - Clarify worker idle retention, optional worker-local inference, placement, and recovery guidance ([#168260](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168260), [issue #168255](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168255)). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Repository environment settings and actions are grouped together, with shared controls under Advanced. [#170892](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170892) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Cloud startup shows snapshot and preparation phases, and image listings can include the reason for a cold start. [#170895](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170895) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Eligible cloud conversations can reuse compatible snapshots and ready workers. Ready workers incur provider charges, and changed setup inputs may require a new image.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Crabbox 0.73.0 minimum and managed acquisition ([#168188](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168188)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse warm images for eligible empty workspaces and pause retries after unsupported capture until the refresh interval ([#168371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168371)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Share overlapping cloud machine and operating-system metadata requests while allowing later refreshes ([#156605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156605)). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Optional warm-image capture during session reclaim ([#168454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168454)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep Crabbox command retry and cleanup time budgets consistent when the system clock changes ([#165889](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165889)). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Warm-image profile setup can retain stale setup-derived state after `setupEnv` changes ([#168892](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168892)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Skip separate runtime preparation when a warm image already contains the requested runtime. [#168974](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168974) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prefetch runtime files at boot for eligible Linux warm images with cron. When system Node resolves under `/usr`, the prefetch may also read unrelated files under that directory. [#169886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169886) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep warm-image cleanup within its elapsed-time budget across system clock changes. Timed-out deletions remain tracked for retry. [#169908](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169908) by [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Compatible Crabbox project snapshots remain reusable across runtime updates. Changes to setup or machine inputs still require a compatible image. [#170780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170780) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Refresh Cloud inventory after plugin activation and use canonical repository labels for worktree snapshots. Existing labels update on their next metadata write. [#171176](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171176) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Background upload for an eligible first AWS snapshot requires Crabbox 0.78.0 or newer and an established-capture receipt. A later worker can start cold while the image is pending. [#171729](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171729) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid repeated capture-failure warnings during cleanup when a Crabbox capture is already paused, while preserving recovery guidance. [#171059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171059) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Ready-worker reuse for empty workspaces follows the configured pool limits and requires a compatible reserve to be ready. [#170929](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170929) · Related work [#168371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168371) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Cloud projects can reuse prepared dependencies and supported Dev Container setup commands. This covers selected lifecycle commands, not the full Dev Container specification.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Linux workers can install a checksum-verified Node runtime and select a repository-declared version. Linux images need compatible system tools and HTTPS access to nodejs.org, while macOS and Windows still need Node supplied by the image or setup. [#170796](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170796) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use global Crabbox settings for cloud worker discovery and suppress Corepack download prompts in detected pnpm and Yarn preparation. Project-scoped commands retain their workspace. [#171706](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171706) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Run fallback Corepack and its package manager on the worker Node for affected pinned repositories while keeping the requested Node for repository scripts. Package manager engine checks see the worker Node. [#171147](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171147) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Clarify cloud setup, repository preparation, snapshots and reuse in the documentation. [#171767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171767) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Correct the worker placement reference to explain that a concurrent placement change can reject a turn without an automatic retry. [#171205](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171205) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use the tiny machine class by default for Mantis desktop browser smoke, while preserving explicit and environment overrides. [#171765](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171765) by [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Cloud recipes support selected Dev Container setup and lifecycle commands, plus pnpm, Yarn, npm and available Bun lockfile detection. Recipe environment values do not propagate to session processes. [#170862](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170862) · [Environment file guide](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/7e15622f37bd0ced194a3b0fccacb4ad6bc78d85/docs/gateway/cloud-workers/environment-file.md) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Cloud setup can call trusted services using approved destination-bound secrets without putting the secret in the repository. A trusted destination can return a credential in its response; supported Linux workers and clients are required.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Official setup integrations can grant remote jobs access to selected secrets through destination-bound proxy egress, including HTTP Basic authentication. This provides the Gateway capability for integrations. [#170810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170810) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Setup secrets use operator-approved names and destination bindings. Bind only trusted services, since a destination can return a credential in its response. Repositories cannot choose secret values or destinations, and unavailable or unbound secrets fail without a plaintext fallback. The feature requires an owned Linux coordinator worker, the matching Crabbox runtime helper, and clients that honor the supplied HTTPS proxy and CA settings. [#171073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171073) · Related work [#170810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170810) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Unavailable cloud profiles show restart or retry guidance, and provider-quota retries respect the reset wait.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Report saved suggested profiles as unavailable until the running Gateway activates them, with restart and retry guidance. [#171025](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171025) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve exhausted-quota reset waits for cloud worker retries after HTTP 429 responses. [#171823](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171823), related to [#171760](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171760), by [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), with [@Kimiyu-186](https://github.com/Kimiyu-186) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Public conversation links can show progress, images and grouped tool steps in the chat layout. Review existing shares because readers can see this additional content too; image contents are not redacted.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Refresh the existing public conversation page with a chat-style layout, light and dark themes, and copy controls. [#168156](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168156). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Public readers can see progress, grouped tool summaries, images and attachment names, with visible-item pagination. [#169644](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169644), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Eligible public links show a session preview or recorded work receipt, with a generic fallback when the publication is unavailable. [#169527](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169527), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Long HTML conversation exports display their transcript and navigation again. Regenerate older exports to get the fix.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Restore the transcript and navigation in long HTML session exports. Previously generated exports need to be regenerated. [#151029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151029). Thanks [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).

  </details>

- Tool and conversation text retain their original contents in history and sharing. Review content before sharing it because credentials from file reads can be included.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserve original tool and conversation text across model context, transcripts and sharing. An interrupted private completion from an older release may need its outcome checked and a fresh request started if an exact-input retry is rejected. [#169063](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169063) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Pending tools recheck access after hooks run instead of using stale permission. A dashboard save already accepted before access is revoked may still finish.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Check current membership before a pending tool executes. [#170539](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170539) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Run durable cross-database dashboard saves in a worker after checking source access. [#170541](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170541) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Usage reports cover larger selections, distinguish missing measurements from zero and keep export controls usable. Exports still refuse incomplete selected data; missing provider measurements cannot be reconstructed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Mark interrupted or unmeasured responses as unavailable across status, chat, session metadata, and usage summaries. Missing provider measurements cannot be reconstructed. [#120008](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/120008), [#119244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/119244); related open report [#151347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/151347). Thanks [@OWWZO](https://github.com/OWWZO), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [@EvolveAegis](https://github.com/EvolveAegis).
  - Reject malformed `usage.cost` agent selectors instead of silently reporting costs for the default agent. [#158696](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158696). Thanks [@alix-007](https://github.com/alix-007) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Show million-token values in `m` units in `/usage full` and custom usage templates. [#164684](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164684). Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Usage overview filtering, totals and paging cover matching sessions beyond the first 1,000. Detailed reports and exports remain available. [#169660](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169660), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Grynn](https://github.com/Grynn) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127245).
  - Aligned usage reports use stored partitions, retain accessible results and show deferred rows as computing. Reports have a 15-second deadline and a 20,000-instance limit after creator filtering. [#171336](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171336) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Usage backfill handles changed ownership of custom or shared stores and resumes slow batches. Reports can show partial totals while backfill progresses. [#171632](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171632) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Usage exports refuse incomplete data and offer guidance to refresh or narrow the range. [#171613](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171613) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The Usage export menu improves keyboard focus and dismissal. [#171347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171347) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Usage session details stay current as the view changes, and unavailable values update when the language changes. [#171630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171630) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Return marked partial totals and narrowing guidance when a usage read exceeds its time budget. [#171904](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171904), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Export a complete selection as JSON or CSV while unrelated sessions refresh. Incomplete selected sources still block export. [#171679](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171679), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show provider-reported totals for selected timeline intervals even when the By Type breakdown is incomplete. [#171872](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171872), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep retained transcripts with legacy colon-containing session IDs in usage reports without interrupting background refresh. [#172316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172316), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Session owners can allow, require approval for or block messages between conversations. Both directions default to Always, and older versions do not enforce these controls.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Choose Always, Ask, or Never for sending and receiving peer messages in session settings. Ask requires approval for the individual message. These choices do not govern human input or external channel delivery. [#162316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162316). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Memory search gives clearer provider-failure guidance and preserves usable indexes during more recovery cases. Searches can still fail while recovery is underway.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Software vector scoring can use less CPU and temporary memory on large indexes when sqlite-vec acceleration is unavailable. [#164773](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164773), with thanks to [etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). See [#114913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/114913) and [#128140](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128140).
  - Memory status and Doctor explain when a requested sessions source is excluded because transcript indexing is not enabled. [#160320](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160320), with thanks to [Olli0103](https://github.com/Olli0103), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [benjaml4](https://github.com/benjaml4). See [#159872](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159872).
  - When an index records vector dimensions, mismatched query embeddings produce an error with an agent-specific force-rebuild command. [#168465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168465), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Enabled loop detection recognizes repeated memory searches with unchanged ordered hits. [#169088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169088), thanks to [ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [PatrickTrent](https://github.com/PatrickTrent).
  - Reduced unnecessary session metadata reads during memory-search visibility checks ([#181247](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181247), [issue #181092](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181092)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini).
  - Kept Active Memory timeout streaks through cooldown expiry so another timeout renews the pause ([#181297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181297), related [issue #180978](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180978)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@nikolay-bratanov](https://github.com/nikolay-bratanov).
  - Applied the existing LanceDB recall deadline across cold provider initialization, embedding, and search for provider adapters ([#182340](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182340)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Kept LanceDB recall deadlines stable when the system clock changes ([#184711](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184711)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Applied the same elapsed-time protection to cold-start embedding deadlines ([#186037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186037)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Added a Gateway warning when an all-zero query embedding skips vector recall, with guidance to check the embedding service and retry ([#183475](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183475), related [issue #183425](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/183425)). Thanks [@lonexreb](https://github.com/lonexreb), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [@feiniaoivp](https://github.com/feiniaoivp).
  - Embedding deadlines use elapsed time, preventing system clock changes from causing unnecessary retries or accepting late results. [#169360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169360), thanks to [Bartok9](https://github.com/Bartok9) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - OpenAI OAuth embeddings pick up renewed credentials for direct and batch requests. An eligible same-origin expired-token response gets one retry. [#172237](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172237), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [digitalfrost84](https://github.com/digitalfrost84).
  - Failed full reindexes retry with bounded backoff while a valid published index remains available for searches. [#138786](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138786), with thanks to [grynn](https://github.com/grynn) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). See [#138775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138775).
  - Embedding requests split at declared provider input limits before sending. Custom compatible providers must declare their limit to use this path. [#141686](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141686), with thanks to [vildevev](https://github.com/vildevev) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). See [#139040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139040).
  - After a recognized limit rejection, embedding retries use a valid reported item limit to avoid unnecessary rejected batches. [#140508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/140508), with thanks to [saladinyao-cyber](https://github.com/saladinyao-cyber) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Managed llama.cpp memory indexing can use its local service when a retained API-key reference is unavailable. Other local providers still require their configured credentials. [#158108](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158108), with thanks to [ysumatta](https://github.com/ysumatta) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Malformed or empty embeddings produce errors, failed reindexes retain the published index, and an empty Ollama query vector no longer silently suppresses semantic results. [#168263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168263), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - After a memory database worker is lost, a later request can restore access to the existing index. Requests during recovery can still fail, and writes with uncertain outcomes are not replayed. [#171494](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171494), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Memory status and Doctor resolve a selected third-party plugin's saved credentials and report the provider unavailable if they cannot be resolved. [#172135](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172135), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [PaulClawitzki](https://github.com/PaulClawitzki).
  - Doctor explains that indexing may stop when neither primary nor fallback embeddings work, and gives agent-specific credential or keyword-only recovery guidance. [#172223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172223), thanks to [serg0x](https://github.com/serg0x) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Memory tools find the intended agent’s permitted notes through more CLI and plugin paths, without opening access to untrusted content.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Trusted workspace profiles and memory reach the model when a third-party memory plugin lacks a provenance classifier. Untrusted or invalidly identified content remains excluded. [#171241](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171241), thanks to [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Sessions opened at an explicit memory path can append and reopen messages with an ordinary canonical session key. [#171284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171284), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restored memory authority for eligible owner sessions and cross-agent subagents, and enforced role and admin checks on private memory reads ([#181359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181359)). Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
  - LanceDB resolves its default store from the active state directory, and Doctor checks that store for its legacy-memory migration. [#168944](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168944), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Memory search and standard session-backfill preview, apply and rollback route through the running local Gateway, as do Matrix verification status, bootstrap and device commands. Other operations require exclusive offline ownership. [#168471](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168471), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Memory Core writes from other processes require the Gateway or exclusive offline access. Workboard reads overlapping a change can briefly show an older snapshot until the next request. [#168841](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168841), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Refused a deleted or replaced source database before disclosing or writing backfilled memories ([commit 3d9595f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3d9595fe12769ce63cb54992911728b95442e243)).

  </details>

- Memory setup can check an optional embedding provider before saving it, and interrupted onboarding can resume without replacing valid saved settings.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Guided and classic local onboarding and memory configuration offer the optional provider and model check. Skipping, a failed check or declining to save leaves existing settings in place. [#163693](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163693), with thanks to [PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [actwebmaster](https://github.com/actwebmaster). See [#16670](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/16670).
  - A completed memory import can show its result and Continue action after the onboarding view is reactivated. [#168791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168791), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Selected Memory Core refreshes its cached managers after provider settings change, so corrected embedding settings can take effect without restarting the Gateway. [#151263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151263), with thanks to [ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [Mirksen](https://github.com/Mirksen). See [#151055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/151055).
  - Non-interactive embedding-only onboarding accepts `openclaw onboard --non-interactive --accept-risk --auth-choice llama-cpp --llama-cpp-embedding-only`. Enabled local-memory agents must agree on the embedding model. [#170153](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170153), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [dh-js](https://github.com/dh-js) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141793).
  - Provider-reported prompt and total token counts reach `/v1/embeddings` responses. Unavailable, invalid or incomplete usage returns zero rather than an estimate. [#154721](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154721), with thanks to [xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Configured model IDs and authorized overrides can contain slashes when the prefix is not a registered provider. [#161170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161170), with thanks to [xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Embedding requests honor provider-specific private-network restrictions and report malformed request headers more clearly. Redirects to private services may need explicit permission.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Provider private-network denial now applies to memory embeddings, including remote overrides, and redirects to another private origin require permission. [#169847](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169847), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [dmorn](https://github.com/dmorn) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/94166), and [mushuiyu886](https://github.com/mushuiyu886) for [earlier policy work](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/94344).
  - Gemini embedding and batch requests and LM Studio discovery, preparation and embedding requests honor explicit private-network denial. Private DNS opt-in belongs on the embedding provider's ID. [#170173](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170173), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx).
  - Invalid or unresolved Google and LM Studio embedding headers fail with the affected setting identified. [#169361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169361), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128536).

  </details>

- Memory indexing does less repeated work while you chat. Recent conversation changes may take longer to appear in search.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Growing session transcripts retain unchanged indexed chunks and only embed and write changed content. [#103201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/103201), with thanks to [wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [wangbaobisheng](https://github.com/wangbaobisheng) and [Hawkeye2611](https://github.com/Hawkeye2611). See [#40919](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/40919).
  - Managed local non-batch indexing limits concurrent file jobs to one. [#137484](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137484), with thanks to [shojikumaru](https://github.com/shojikumaru), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [wtom](https://github.com/wtom). See [#136175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/136175).
  - Targeted session updates avoid loading unrelated forgotten-session records. [#168098](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168098), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). See the separate related report [#167838](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167838) from [nkarkare](https://github.com/nkarkare).
  - Index setup and rebuilds move more database work away from the Gateway thread. [#168860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168860), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Completed agent database opening requests no longer remain unnecessarily in memory. [#168349](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168349), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session transcripts are indexed after a quiet period, with pending work deferred while turns are running. Searches use the published index and do not force pending transcripts to be indexed. [#170404](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170404), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Handled session indexing failures are retried during idle periods, with older pending changes retained. [#170719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170719), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A failed rebuild gets its final automatic retry after the rebuild cooldown, while memory maintenance avoids redundant cache and index reads. [#170792](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170792), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh agents can initialize indexing so later session turns become searchable. [#170220](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170220), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recurring memory sync reuses committed index information to reduce database work on the Gateway thread. [#169058](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169058), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Memory searches reuse read-only connections to reduce setup work. [#170183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170183), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session indexing batches changes and preserves unrelated session caches. [#170302](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170302), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Published memory-index reads reduce contention with concurrent writes. [#170407](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170407), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Memory startup and built-in status do less database work on the main thread. [#171322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171322), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Large, mostly unchanged memory sources reuse unchanged vectors, avoiding work that can delay conversation writes. [#171402](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171402), thanks to [salad-maker](https://github.com/salad-maker).
  - Memory retrieval workers retain less memory while preserving search and Forget behavior. [#172392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172392), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Bounded temporary embedding-cache growth during full rebuilds while preserving cache reuse ([#183089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183089), [issue #182886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182886)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
  - Background memory indexing waits until its agent database is ready and checks access before proceeding ([commit 51d97ef](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/51d97efee0de2bdf39d76278a24d30c7d343317c)).
  - Kept pending indexing work after an expected background rebuild interruption without consuming an error retry ([commit 71692b0](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/71692b03a277790e5781705dcdfeb6f4ba0c810f)).
  - Reduced unnecessary memory retention after native agent database opening ([commit 06e6524](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/06e6524335e7eed5f44dbd3521c0bb5b60a8322d)).

  </details>

- Memory Wiki searches and links handle more filenames, Unicode spellings and Markdown link forms.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Compile and lint avoid a second vault compile after changes, and compile returns the actual updated-index count. [#169028](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169028), thanks to [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin) and [etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
  - Large vault status counts avoid the reported timeout. [#170553](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170553), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [migelizaga-rgb](https://github.com/migelizaga-rgb) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170466).
  - Identity validation uses less temporary memory for vaults with long compile histories. [#171845](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171845), thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Filename and extensionless-path lookups read matching candidates instead of every page. Page ID lookups and misses still scan the vault. [#171961](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171961), thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
  - Fixed wiki lint and backlinks for inline Markdown links with titles, parentheses, or angle-bracket destinations ([#181380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181380), [issue #181379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181379)). Thanks [@loading2332](https://github.com/loading2332).
  - Resolved percent-encoded local Markdown paths for lint and backlinks ([#181794](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181794)). Thanks [@L4XB](https://github.com/L4XB) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Matched equivalent composed and decomposed Unicode text in local Wiki search ([#184353](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184353)). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Memory Wiki and Dream Diary leave malformed originals untouched, while marked handwritten Notes survive page regeneration. Preserve damaged originals and repair a copy; unmarked text on older generated pages is not protected.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserved marked human Notes when OKF concept pages are regenerated ([#175707](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175707), [issue #175700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/175700)). Thanks [@YangManBOBO](https://github.com/YangManBOBO), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Kept stale OKF concept pages containing handwritten Notes when a concept is removed from the bundle ([#179657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179657)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retained malformed source pages and their synchronization state during bridge and unsafe-local pruning ([#174573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174573)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserved existing OKF concepts with unreadable source text and stopped imports with an unreadable root index before concept writes ([#176009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176009)). Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007).
  - Stopped memory maintenance from rewriting files with malformed UTF-8 ([#179016](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179016)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor skips a split root-memory repair when either file contains malformed UTF-8 and explains how to preserve and recover the original bytes. If the legacy file was already archived, the warning identifies its location. [#169029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169029), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and co-author [wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), who reported [#168567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168567).
  - Doctor preserves local or custom Ollama embedding routes when migrating hosted Ollama chat settings. [#169661](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169661), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Supported ChatGPT and Hermes imports preserve readable text and reject malformed or special-file inputs without silently rewriting them.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Rejected special files in ChatGPT wiki import and preview. Replace the affected path with a regular file and retry. Symlinks to regular files remain supported ([#181755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181755)). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserved prose, pasted JSON, and embedded quotes in newly imported active-branch ChatGPT transcripts. Explicitly typed image parts remain omitted ([#182049](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182049)). Thanks [@L4XB](https://github.com/L4XB) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Hermes append-style `MEMORY.md` and `USER.md` imports reject malformed UTF-8 without changing the failed item's source or destination. Re-encode the source before retrying. [#172742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172742), thanks to [Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reported directory inspection and skill listing errors during preview. Resolve the access or storage problem and rerun preview before applying ([#175452](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175452)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoided blocking on pipes and other special files during migration. Replace an unexpected special file with an ordinary readable UTF-8 file and retry ([#180908](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180908)). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Memory checkpoints can save context before compaction even when token-use information is missing, without storing copied checkpoint blocks again.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - A transcript estimate can trigger a pre-compaction memory checkpoint when provider usage is missing or zero, without presenting the estimate as measured session usage. [#83178](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/83178), with thanks to [njuboy11](https://github.com/njuboy11) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). See [#83177](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/83177).
  - File-based pre-compaction flushes reject copied saved blocks and can retry with new text. Existing duplicates remain, and paraphrased repetition is not detected. [#168123](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168123), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [bit-777](https://github.com/bit-777) and [touraround](https://github.com/touraround). See [#164650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164650).

  </details>

- Paused or disabled Dreaming jobs stay stopped through recovery, and sweeps avoid more empty entries. Re-enable a stopped job explicitly when you want it to run.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - A useful Dream Diary sweep writes at most one combined reflection per workspace, and an empty sweep writes none. [#167887](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167887), with thanks to [diregoblin](https://github.com/diregoblin) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A fresh Gateway no longer reports a false dreaming cleanup failure when an agent database is first created. [#168175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168175), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Memory sweeps no longer cause unnecessary recovery checks on Gateway restart. [#168631](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168631), with thanks to [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman). See [#168620](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168620).
  - Disabling or replacing Memory Core no longer waits for a timeout solely because a startup scan is running. [#168229](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168229), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserved paused and failure-disabled Dreaming jobs and their failure history. Resume a stopped job with `openclaw cron enable <job-id>` ([#180694](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180694), [issue #180655](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180655)). Thanks [@Yun-0000](https://github.com/Yun-0000), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [@lisandromachado](https://github.com/lisandromachado).
  - Recorded Gateway-routed CLI memory search recalls in the requested agent's Dreaming workspace ([commit 8fc0987](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8fc0987fb086d68df8c345b50501c6b08e4f6e8d)).
  - Preserved malformed dream diaries during updates and omitted them from optional history ([#179022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179022)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Documented recovery for malformed dream diaries, including preserving the original and converting a copy before retrying ([commit 43c5a84](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/43c5a84ecaf9ce16a191f5498ed2550aca40da83)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Memory plugin reload can finish while slow cleanup of old interrupted dreaming sessions continues in the background. [#169945](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169945), thanks to [Stronggenetics](https://github.com/Stronggenetics) and [hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph) for the [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/152275).

  </details>

- Fresh supported desktop setups can guide preparation of a local Gateway, with explicit local and remote choices instead of assuming one installation path.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>
