  - Fresh Linux and Windows desktop setup prepares a local Gateway and opens onboarding, with manual recovery choices when needed. [#169512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169512) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Windows x64 desktop builds support local CLI and Gateway setup, a regular installer and the Windows stable update channel. Remote connections remain available if the signed runtime is missing. [#169522](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169522) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh Mac setup shows progress and offers guided dashboard onboarding when the Gateway supports automatic setup, with a manual fallback. [#169568](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169568) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac setup follows the dashboard through window changes and keeps the native setup page visible when an installation step is removed. [#170613](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170613) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac setup and status messages gain translations across 21 locales. [#170221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170221).
  - Local app-hosted setup saves a usable Gateway token and reduces runtime preparation and connection delays. If a required token cannot be saved, setup stops with a retryable error. [#169403](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169403). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh Macs can show AI setup choices sooner. [#169472](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169472). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Standalone macOS Bun reports missing or incompatible SQLite libraries with setup guidance. [#168564](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168564). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh macOS profiles can start the Gateway and continue automatic setup through supervised retries. [#173246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173246) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh Windows setup no longer mistakes its own state initialization for another process performing offline maintenance. [#176174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176174), [issue #176163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176163) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@tjarrau-dev](https://github.com/tjarrau-dev).
  - Fresh local setup in non-release Linux and Windows desktop builds uses matching staged core and Codex packages without npm on the user's machine. The first verified copy can take several minutes. [#171117](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171117) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Linux and Windows desktop apps reclaim unreferenced runtime files after a healthy activation while retaining current and previous selections and active references. [#171489](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171489) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Bundled desktop runtimes omit unused development files. The measured signed Apple Silicon Mac bundle was 62.5 MB smaller; the same staging change applies to bundled Linux and Windows desktop packages. [#172054](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172054) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Windows desktop setup guides recommend Windows Hub and document Tauri as an alternative. [#171114](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171114) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The Mac installation guide includes architecture-specific installer links pinned to version 2026.10.3 and the releases index for newer builds. [#171560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171560) Thanks to [@brianmcguire](https://github.com/brianmcguire) and [@lkanowski](https://github.com/lkanowski).
  - Packaged non-release Mac builds use same-commit official plugins for setup, avoiding Codex plugin and core version mismatches. Large plugin bundles can fall back to staging Codex alone, and explicit source choices remain respected. [#170776](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170776) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Mac permission prompts identify the affected computer and offer the next grant step, followed by a confirmed retry. Grant access in the app on that Mac.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Permission cards connect missing native Mac access to the app's grant flow and a user-confirmed retry. Computer Control can show multiple missing grants together. [#169635](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169635) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Chat permission cards offer the next step for the current macOS and OpenClaw access state, including requests handled in stages. [#171115](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171115) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - After a Screen Recording request, the card offers System Settings if access was denied. [#171175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171175) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Location cards direct you to System Settings after access is denied or unavailable, before offering to enable Location mode. [#171859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171859) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Protected Chrome data discovery and setup wait for an explicit browser action. [#171177](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171177) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - [Quick Chat permission setup crash fix](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/94940fb3e3017a6fdb3eccb81c809a929a74a056).
  - When Host Desktop is disabled, chat offers setup guidance and lets an authorized connection enable it when the desktop server is ready. [#177888](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177888), [issue #174368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174368) Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm) and [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).
  - Eligible owner turns honor remembered native runtime setup consent and use the unrestricted access described in the setup prompt when per-turn consent applies. Revoked consent and non-owner turns remain blocked. [#185920](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185920) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Mac window controls fit alongside native title-bar buttons without overlapping them.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Desktop panel headers and navigation controls share the titlebar while leaving room for native window buttons. [#171131](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171131) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The rail divider and collapsed sidebar background stay below macOS window controls. [#172451](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172451) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Downloading a conversation in the Mac app opens the system Save dialog.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Dashboard and conversation downloads in the Mac app use the system Save dialog instead of replacing the page with archive data. [#173654](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173654) Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Apple chat makes preparation and retry progress easier to follow and handles more reconnect cases.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - iPad pairing and Gateway onboarding messages have translations in more languages. [#185480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185480)
  - Preparation, response-wait and retry status in iOS and macOS chat, including accessible labels. [#166356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166356), related [#139926](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139926). Thanks [Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin) and [noelillinger](https://github.com/noelillinger).
  - Subagent waiting indicator and single-helper navigation in native Apple chat. [#166322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166322), related [#52960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/52960). Thanks [Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored) and [Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin).
  - iOS hardware arrow-key history leaves selected text and active input-method composition alone. [#143056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143056). Thanks [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - iOS buttons have clearer dark-mode contrast and room for larger text, and the Settings title keeps its spacing with the sidebar open. [#166379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166379). Thanks [Rocuts](https://github.com/Rocuts), [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Apple clients reconnect when explicit scopes or computer-use options change, and unchanged iOS instance reads no longer trigger false registry notifications. Mac Claude session browsing uses the app's worker and is unavailable while that worker is down. [#169617](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169617) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - iOS manual Gateway forms show inline host guidance and disable Connect for invalid entries. Enter a host name or IP without a scheme or port. [#169875](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169875), reported in [#144678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/144678). Thanks to [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [@bjesuiter](https://github.com/bjesuiter).
  - iOS chat follows its message connection for online status, and dismissed warnings stay hidden through identical repeated failures. [#169912](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169912), reported in [#169911](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169911), by [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin).
  - Mac Dashboard users can reload after non-HTML Gateway errors, and stale session links retry the normal access check. [#170361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170361) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac Dashboard reload and Gateway error messages gain translations in 21 non-English locales. [#170475](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170475).
  - The existing subagent wait label gains translations in the iOS and macOS catalogs. [#170172](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170172).
  - Native chat startup and retry messages gain translated catalog entries for supported locales. [#171084](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171084).
  - Native app chat can send again after reconnecting and keeps the matching resumed session. [#171528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171528) Thanks to [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen) and to [@azuretek](https://github.com/azuretek) for the report.
  - A failed Mac dashboard can reload when an authenticated connection returns to the same endpoint, while preserving healthy content and drafts. [#172646](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172646) Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
  - Mac Companion setup can finish AI verification with a saved sign-in after a wizard progress reply. [#171667](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171667) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@enter501](https://github.com/enter501) for the report.
  - The Mac Codex catalog remains available when the valid `nativeHookRelay` plugin option is configured. [#172592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172592) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac CLI startup diagnostics print literally when the app cannot resolve the local CLI. [#171802](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171802) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Native app lists and transcript copies use best-effort reads when data changes concurrently and can show incomplete or mixed results. Android Talk settings changed during a fetch may need a refresh or restart, and a failed iOS Talk close can await stale-session cleanup. [#169336](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169336). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Android preserves non-ASCII Gateway names and fixes inline-code rendering after an unmatched backtick.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - The Android CommonMark update addresses code spans following an unmatched backtick. [#170478](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170478), with a follow-up from [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Android discovery preserves UTF-8 Gateway names, and Google video fallback reports the operation that supplied the result. [#171920](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171920) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Wear OS reply, speech and retry controls work better on round displays.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Wear OS recovery keeps Retry visible on round displays, while shared lists and controls fit below the clock and remain scrollable with larger text. [#170321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170321) by [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
  - Wear keyboard input uses current state when it returns, and anonymous final replies no longer duplicate history. [#171476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171476) Thanks to [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
  - Wear returns from Pulse to Chat when Pulse is unavailable, including when restoring a saved watch session. [#172136](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172136) Contribution from [@IWhatsskill](https://github.com/IWhatsskill).
  - Wear reply speech and stop controls stay responsive when the speech engine stalls. [#172147](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172147) Contribution from [@IWhatsskill](https://github.com/IWhatsskill).

  </details>

- Android refuses embedded browser, desktop and terminal previews on connections whose certificate pin those views cannot maintain. Native chat remains available.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Embedded pages are refused on actively pinned connections because the embedded browser cannot enforce the native pin throughout the connection. A saved fingerprint that is not active does not trigger the restriction. [#180843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180843) Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).

  </details>

- Mac setup and update recovery recognize more healthy services and provide clearer repairs. Learned certificate pins can follow trusted renewals; explicit pins stay fixed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Native updates reduce stopped database backup and optional Doctor work while keeping recovery backups and required repairs. [#170087](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170087) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac updates avoid waiting on their own lock when equivalent temporary-directory paths are used. [#169860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169860) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac update and recovery checks recognize valid app-managed Bun services, addressing a recovery screen that could remain despite a healthy Gateway. [#170006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170006), with the report and investigation in [#169662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169662). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jokerman1302-eng](https://github.com/jokerman1302-eng), [@davidmackrory-byte](https://github.com/davidmackrory-byte) and [@josephsilvagnoli](https://github.com/josephsilvagnoli).
  - Mac updates report why a LaunchAgent inspection failed and suggest a deep Gateway status check. [#170429](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170429), reported in [#169995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169995). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot).
  - Mac service repair handles overlapping service loading after confirming the service is loaded, without interrupting a running Gateway. [#170740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170740) by [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Mac app recovery preserves a newer managed Gateway and can restore a missing Node service using its selected CLI. The fix requires updated app recovery code. [#170766](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170766) by [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - A verified external Mac terminal can restart the Gateway when an ancestor process cannot be inspected. [#170354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170354) by [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - The Mac app updates Peekaboo and swift-subprocess to bring in child-process pipe and descriptor handling fixes. [#170713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170713) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway startup in the Mac app can reach readiness sooner when using bundled Bun. [#169543](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169543) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac app Retry repairs plugin links and clears an incomplete-update warning after the required Gateway checks succeed. [#171508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171508) Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and to [@shanselman](https://github.com/shanselman) for the report and investigation.
  - Updates under the macOS Gateway service prepare the selected pnpm installation's environment. [#171936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171936) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@AntaresGG](https://github.com/AntaresGG) for the report, and [@MoerAI](https://github.com/MoerAI) for the reproduction.
  - Updates from macOS login shells can proceed when checks verify they are outside the Gateway service, even if the full process ancestry cannot be inspected. An older updater may still refuse before it can install this fix. [#172640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172640) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@giulianoaccorsi](https://github.com/giulianoaccorsi) for the report and diagnosis.
  - macOS service inspection and restart preserve multiline environment values and their whitespace. [#171740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171740) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for the report.
  - Two Mac Gateway recovery messages are translated in 21 non-English locales. [#172422](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172422).
  - App-managed upgrades attempt to repair official managed plugin versions before migration and pass repair warnings to the app. [#174257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174257), [issue #174120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174120) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@kazuyuki-eguchi](https://github.com/kazuyuki-eguchi).
  - Process-owning Gateway stops avoid individually retiring native skills watchers, a cleanup bottleneck on Macs with many skill roots. This applies to later stops after the updated Gateway is running. [#168273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168273). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - A macOS LaunchAgent restart reports an error when its restart helper cannot start, instead of claiming that the restart was scheduled. [#175229](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175229) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Updated Mac apps can use compatible saved device credentials for remote Gateway connections after unrelated local database upgrades. [#166993](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166993). Thanks [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Direct Mac Gateway connections using a learned certificate pin can reconnect after a trusted renewal for the same host. Explicitly configured pins remain fixed. [#168151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168151). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev) for the [earlier renewal work](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164435).
  - The Mac status menu explains when an incompatible bundled worker needs an app update and offers an update action when available. [#157948](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157948), related [#157444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157444). Thanks [PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13).

  </details>

- Mac commands find configured tools in more launch contexts. Relaunch after changing preferred tool folders; old shared PATH additions may need manual cleanup.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Named profiles and temporary Mac app copies no longer add their wrappers to the shared shell PATH. Named-profile wrappers remain available by full path; old PATH entries must be removed manually. [#169460](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169460). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - New Peekaboo skill dependency installs use the maintained OpenClaw Homebrew tap. Existing installations are not migrated automatically. [#166245](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166245), related [#166244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166244). Thanks [holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen).
  - Mac node commands find preferred tool folders across Finder, Dock, and login launches. [#171460](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171460) Thanks to [@brianmcguire](https://github.com/brianmcguire), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [@jamiezigelbaum](https://github.com/jamiezigelbaum) for the report.
  - Long commands through the Mac companion can return within the requested timeout, and lost results are distinguished from an unreachable app. [#172117](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172117) Thanks to [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen), [@ylcn91](https://github.com/ylcn91), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [@Colton-Harris](https://github.com/Colton-Harris) for the report and diagnosis.
  - macOS skill eligibility, installer recipes and remote binary checks recognize the macOS platform alias. [#172214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172214) Thanks to [@fhgffy](https://github.com/fhgffy), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [@jessecq1995](https://github.com/jessecq1995) for the related report.
  - Apple app builds find a supported Node executable when a version-manager shim leads PATH. [#168139](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168139). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac developer guidance covers signing and packaging a local build to replace a release installation. [#168527](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168527). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- The Mac memory readout counts reclaimable memory as available.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Mac host memory meters in Devices and the Gateway count reclaimable memory as available. [#144535](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144535), reports [#144176](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/144176) and [#156578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156578). Thanks [sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), [altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf), and reporters [hengtested-design](https://github.com/hengtested-design) and [s3d1K0](https://github.com/s3d1K0).

  </details>

- Anthropic discovery failures no longer discard a supported context-window choice, and the Sonnet 5.5 cached-input cost estimate is corrected.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Direct Anthropic sessions retain their selected supported context window when live model discovery fails. [#177702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177702), [issue #177701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/177701) Thanks [@nxmxbbd](https://github.com/nxmxbbd) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - New Sonnet 5.5 usage estimates use the corrected default cached-input rate. [#171723](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171723) Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- Windows installation and update recovery handle more backup-path and service-retry failures. Some fixes require an already-corrected updater, so an older broken updater may still need manual recovery.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Windows update recovery accepts short-name or case-variant paths to the same verified backup, avoiding a false rollback. [#168968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168968). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - The final plugin check for Windows updates from OpenClaw 2026.10.1 can use the pending-migration request expected by that updater. [#169031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169031). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Concurrent Windows updater starts coordinate initial handoff database creation to avoid false quarantine and lost records when the installed updater includes the fix. [#169404](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169404). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update service refresh recognizes equivalent Windows account names while protecting different or unresolved accounts. [#169481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169481) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor preserves internal dependency junctions when copying plugin captures between phases. [#169672](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169672), related to [#169586](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169586), by [@n572770](https://github.com/n572770).
  - Windows Git-source updates avoid recursive directory copies and can recover the verified previous build after a qualifying staging failure. [#170940](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170940), reported in [#170700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170700). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@stop-time](https://github.com/stop-time).
  - Windows update errors give recovery guidance for Task Scheduler denials recognized by English access-denied text or `0x80070005`. [#171109](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171109) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@Rene-F](https://github.com/Rene-F) for the report.
  - The default piped Windows installer accepts an omitted Node version. Explicit overrides still require a three-part version. [#183145](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183145), [issue #183088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/183088) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit) and [@ryandle](https://github.com/ryandle).
  - A failed Git pull during a Windows source install is aborted and the checkout checked before the installer continues. It stops with recovery guidance if the prior state cannot be confirmed. [#185378](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185378), [issue #127411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127411) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Windows updates and plugin loading handle deep paths and 8.3 short-name state paths that could block candidate migration checks. [#172769](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172769) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Windows pnpm updates can skip dangling links for absent optional packages. A failure before installation or service changes can also avoid a false restart failure report. The installed updater needs this fix to use it. [#181596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181596), [issue #181577](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181577) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@SMF12138](https://github.com/SMF12138).
  - Windows SQLite inspection helpers request hidden windows during updates. [#174969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174969), [issue #174906](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174906) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@throwingbones](https://github.com/throwingbones).
  - Windows managed recovery bundles and portable worker archives include the security helper needed after the original installation is replaced. [#187470](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187470) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Windows runtime recovery and Bun-hosted Node discovery recognize quoted absolute PATH entries. [#181678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181678) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Links2008](https://github.com/Links2008).
  - Forced Windows Gateway reinstall tolerates normal launcher timestamp settlement and can restore the previous task if activation fails. [#176945](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176945), [issue #176872](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176872) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@cnnceart](https://github.com/cnnceart).
  - Windows Gateway stop and restart can confirm a slow LAN port refusal within the shutdown window. If the check remains inconclusive, run `openclaw gateway status --deep` before retrying. [#174807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174807), [issue #174563](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174563) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng) and [@prizmizm](https://github.com/prizmizm).
  - Windows backup and Doctor cleanup can retire eligible inactive backup scratch without the recurring directory error. [#176836](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176836), [issue #176743](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176743) Thanks [@xutongxue233](https://github.com/xutongxue233), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@wg-mojo](https://github.com/wg-mojo).
  - Windows service takeover waits and macOS Gateway stop waits retain their intended duration when the system clock changes. [#182859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182859), [#183033](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183033) Thanks [@mikasa0818](https://github.com/mikasa0818), [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Windows commands handle more long-path, encoding and environment edge cases. Install native PowerShell 7 when a Store alias is unavailable and the 5.1 fallback cannot run your script.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Windows nodes preserve quoted prompts, URLs and paths with spaces in the standard `cmd.exe /d /s /c` launch path. [#158160](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158160), reports [#142847](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/142847) and [#158129](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/158129). Thanks [Dimitri-lcht](https://github.com/Dimitri-lcht), [igs-rogenlo](https://github.com/igs-rogenlo), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [kassol](https://github.com/kassol).
  - Windows ARM64 shell launches retain the `SystemRoot` value needed to start when a replacement environment omits it. An explicit caller value still takes precedence. [#169338](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169338). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Windows Bun service installation retains a successful saved-runtime read when temporary snapshot cleanup is deferred. [#169286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169286). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Windows Bun Gateway configuration discovery can continue after a successful read when a private snapshot remains temporarily busy. [#169337](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169337). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Windows Gateways with installed plugins can use the small-file copy path during startup. [#168313](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168313). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Windows-sensitive file operations use exact file identities with the updated fs-safe dependency. [#169469](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169469). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Windows Bun plugins share the Gateway SDK module instance and its state. [#169482](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169482) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Agent deletion supports large NTFS file IDs and recovery retains exact identities. Older numeric records keep their rounding ambiguity until they are verified and upgraded. [#169485](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169485), reported in [#137416](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137416). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@pkinjo](https://github.com/pkinjo), [@xupeijian](https://github.com/xupeijian) and [@karljamoralin](https://github.com/karljamoralin).
  - Windows Bun Gateway startup and deep status can proceed after a successful snapshot read when cleanup encounters a busy file. Cleanup may be deferred until process exit or a later startup. [#169572](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169572) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Skill preparation avoids repeated environment reads when required binaries are missing, reducing work on long Windows search paths. [#171066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171066), reported in [#171040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171040). Thanks to [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@funnysubaru](https://github.com/funnysubaru).
  - Windows reports incompatible AppContainer child-pipe operations and native helpers, and improves access to private paths. [#171728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171728) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jonathanlindsay](https://github.com/jonathanlindsay), and [@chelsealong](https://github.com/chelsealong).
  - Plugin capture and validation batch guarded file opens to reduce repeated work in the Windows startup path. [#172333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172333) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@SBC2016](https://github.com/SBC2016) for the report.
  - Windows Gateway startup no longer fails its database checks when the environment cannot be sent to the inspection process. [#176194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176194), [issue #176111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176111) Thanks [@skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@easyteacher](https://github.com/easyteacher).
  - Windows state-folder watchers handle 8.3 short names that could crash onboarding, the Gateway or CLI processes. [#172768](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172768) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Windows private SQLite files use paths suitable for reopening short files and creating files near the path-length limit. [Commit 47c7064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/47c7064d8fcd50515d1b220c6209b71a9aa4bfab)
  - Native plugins can load on affected Windows systems when security software changes only a companion file's metadata and its contents remain verified. [#179949](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179949), [issue #179923](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/179923) Thanks [@coaiMax](https://github.com/coaiMax) and [@paulcam206](https://github.com/paulcam206).
  - Unattended Windows Gateway commands skip inaccessible Store PowerShell aliases. If only the Store alias is available, the fallback is Windows PowerShell 5.1; commands requiring PowerShell 7 syntax need a native MSI or ZIP installation of PowerShell 7. [#182898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182898), [issue #182652](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182652) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@tre1d](https://github.com/tre1d).
  - Windows Claude CLI turns using stored credentials avoid a startup delivery stall. Claude Code may still use its own login instead of the selected OpenClaw profile. [#174571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174571), [issue #174204](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174204), [account selection issue #163332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/163332) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@scalesmart](https://github.com/scalesmart), [@layne-11](https://github.com/layne-11) and [@pandysp](https://github.com/pandysp).
  - Windows port commands explain the unsupported operation and alternatives while leaving existing data and engine selection unchanged. [#183512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183512) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Android and Termux accept the same state database after ordinary SQLite writes while retaining replacement-file checks. [#167719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167719), report [#167623](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167623) and related [#167791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167791). Thanks [yxshee](https://github.com/yxshee), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [grabowskidaniel359-design](https://github.com/grabowskidaniel359-design).
  - Secret input avoids the native call implicated in Rosetta crashes, APFS worktrees can use a matching helper under Rosetta, and Windows private files support long paths. [#168942](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168942), related [#167663](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167663). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Windows source checkouts entered through short paths can prepare plugin SDK declarations. [#169427](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169427). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Managed Linux desktops avoid an additional display-socket conflict, and AppImage host commands avoid inherited library-path conflicts.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Managed Linux desktops can select a free display when another X server has a hidden socket. [#169392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169392), report [#169391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169391). Thanks [holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Linux companion AppImage host commands no longer inherit its library path, addressing an SSH ProxyCommand conflict. This removes the inherited `LD_LIBRARY_PATH`, including user-added entries, from those commands inside the AppImage. [#183661](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183661), [issue #183656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/183656) Thanks [@demos-ra](https://github.com/demos-ra) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A failed DNS zone read leaves an existing zone untouched, and Linux camera commands report directory read errors instead of saying no cameras are available. [#174424](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174424) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Guided local image setup on Linux x64 CPU systems helps download and check OCR and vision models. A separately configured cloud chat model may still receive the original image.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Guided local OCR and vision setup with verified model downloads and separate checks for both tasks. [#148193](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148193), [#148056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/148056). Thanks [@duyufansh2008](https://github.com/duyufansh2008) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- A disconnected FaceTime call no longer requires a Gateway restart before the next authorized call.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - A disconnected FaceTime call releases the bridge without requiring a Gateway restart before the next authorized call. [#154230](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154230), [#154228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/154228). Thanks [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin), [@omarshahine](https://github.com/omarshahine) and [@beeven](https://github.com/beeven).

  </details>

- Voice Call commands keep JSON output parseable and reject unsupported special files instead of hanging on them.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Voice Call steer keeps startup chatter out of JSON standard output. [#169430](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169430). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Voice Call commands reject special files supplied as call briefs. [#183417](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183417). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Voice Call diagnostics reject nonregular custom logs without blocking on POSIX. [#172743](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172743). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Image generation rejects malformed options before making a provider request and accepts supported URL-backed results. Video results identify ignored options; explicitly pinned retired Together video models need replacement.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - ComfyUI image, video and music workflow polling keeps the configured timeout across system clock changes. [#162779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162779). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Image generate and edit commands reject unsupported resolution hints before provider dispatch. Supported values are 1K, 2K and 4K. [#170154](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170154). Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - OpenAI-compatible image generation and editing accept URL-backed results under the existing network and image-size policies. [#170278](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170278), [#162717](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162717). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), reporter [@lucthaobybgkw3232-eng](https://github.com/lucthaobybgkw3232-eng) and [@acedatacloud-dev](https://github.com/acedatacloud-dev) for the compatibility report.
  - Video generation reports ignored provider options in text and JSON results. [#171172](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171172). Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559).
  - Image generate and edit commands reject malformed aspect ratios before a request or output file is created. Positive numeric ratios remain supported. [#172082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172082). Thanks [@KrasimirKralev](https://github.com/KrasimirKralev) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Together video defaults to Wan 2.7 and uses a longer polling window. Explicit retired Wan 2.2 and Kling IDs need replacement. [#172364](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172364). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Generated media can wait for a busy chat without a premature failure warning, and more text and image attachments retain their expected formats.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Windows-1252 text and CSV attachments retain accents and symbols when their MIME type has no charset. [#165771](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165771). Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Attachments are retained when an input was committed but its admission result is uncertain. The request still returns its error and does not start the agent run. [#167369](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167369). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Generated image, music and video results queued behind a busy chat avoid a premature failure warning. A permanent delivery failure still reports the problem and retains the media reference. [#172637](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172637), with the earlier proposal in [#154064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154064). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen) and [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668).
  - Public image attachments use extensions matching their resolved formats, including queued deliveries. [#182494](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182494). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Background media handoff deadlines use elapsed time. [#185704](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185704). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Sandboxed WebChat turns load staged images; the same merge includes Linux service detection, model-list reuse and plugin inspection fixes. [Commit e26afcf](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e26afcff4beb0cfb90a1ca6e9cbde7d713472895).
  - When a generated-media batch fails, cleanup starts in request order and preserves the original save error. [Commit a316adf](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a316adfcce2e03619282801914a3daf7383cdf1c).

  </details>

- Media conversion and cache cleanup report failures without discarding an otherwise available conversion result.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Playback conversion reports output-file close failures while preserving the conversion result. [#174668](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174668). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Playback cache cleanup reports errors and avoids an extra eviction when an entry has already disappeared. [#175634](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175634). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Microsoft Teams can run multiple bot identities on one Gateway, each with its own registration, credentials and callback endpoint.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Multiple bot accounts, agent bindings and account-specific callback routes. The default bot keeps its existing endpoint, while named accounts have their own routes. [#112811](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/112811), [request #71058](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/71058). Thanks to [@jimmypuckett](https://github.com/jimmypuckett), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@ashukla147](https://github.com/ashukla147).

  </details>

- Threaded replies retain more of the intended sender and conversation context across supported channels.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Long channel threads provide the newest 50 replies as context when that window can be selected reliably. [#120824](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/120824), [#98870](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/98870). Thanks to [@Linux2010](https://github.com/Linux2010), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@qingminglong](https://github.com/qingminglong).
  - Reaction lists include available person IDs and names. [#151128](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151128). Thanks to [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Informative progress steps in Teams direct messages appear as distinct readable rows. [#157704](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157704), [#157702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157702). Thanks to [@rdbartram](https://github.com/rdbartram) and [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - Reactions and reaction lists target the current channel-thread reply. [#166582](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166582), [#166178](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166178). Thanks to [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Incoming dice rolls include the emoji and value in agent and reply context. Group mention requirements continue to apply. [#137112](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137112), [related outbound request #137110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137110). Thanks to [@adeepn](https://github.com/adeepn) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Voice notes and audio uploads can show their duration when `ffprobe` is available. Uploads still proceed if probing fails. [#142552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142552), [#142548](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/142548). Thanks to [@szafranski](https://github.com/szafranski) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Shared contact cards and replies to them retain the contact name and phone details without treating those details as a bot mention. [#144602](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144602). Thanks to [@JamesY023](https://github.com/JamesY023) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Quoted reply text stays attached to its message when automation context is present in a CLI-backed agent prompt. [#166869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166869). Thanks to [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Authorized Telegram private-topic reactions reach their originating session when cached topic context is available. On a cache miss they fall back to the base DM, or are dropped when `requireTopic` is enabled. [#175946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175946). Thanks to [xiaopan-xydt](https://github.com/xiaopan-xydt) and [RaphazZze](https://github.com/RaphazZze) for the report.
  - Opt in to targeting thread and topic sessions while keeping the usual permissions in place. [#168846](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168846). Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Retain delivered Discord native status replies, menus, model-picker and plugin replies with their commands in history and later context. [#169200](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169200). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Retain delivered Slack native argument menus and their commands in history and later context. [#169236](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169236). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep delivered Mattermost model and provider pickers and selection confirmations in history ([#169255](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169255)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Separate the current request from earlier thread context ([#169817](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169817)). Thanks to [yousan](https://github.com/yousan) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve multiline group slash-command arguments such as `/btw` questions and `/loop` prompts ([#183606](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183606)). Thanks to [L4XB](https://github.com/L4XB) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Feishu merged forwards include available sender and timestamp information, with sender-name lookup bounded to the first 50 rendered messages. [#150214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150214), [#150165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/150165). Thanks to [@chelsealong](https://github.com/chelsealong), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@peach980](https://github.com/peach980).

  </details>

- Fixes Teams replies and slow image loads in the affected cases. An allowlist containing only unresolved names still blocks access.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Give slow pasted images in Microsoft Teams a longer download budget and preserve a separate budget for optional message context ([#170437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170437)). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [EbulenConsult](https://github.com/EbulenConsult) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169917).

  - Microsoft Teams list items following fenced code blocks render separately instead of being absorbed into the code block. [#182959](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182959). Thanks to [L4XB](https://github.com/L4XB) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Prevent malformed Teams replies and attachment HTML from stalling message handling ([#183438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183438)). Thanks to [LeizhenZhang](https://github.com/LeizhenZhang) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep fully unresolved Teams team, channel, and group sender allowlists restrictive at startup ([#184989](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184989)). Thanks to [eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - Update the affected Teams authentication dependencies, including for managed npm plugin installations ([#187293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187293)). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and upstream contributor [Robbie-Microsoft](https://github.com/Robbie-Microsoft).

- Matrix recovery and room policies stay tied to the intended encrypted state and room identity. Follow the coordinated-upgrade and recovery warning above when sharing a Matrix account.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Exclusive ownership protects encrypted state, snapshot validation prevents corrupt restoration, and Matrix Doctor supports inspection and explicit recovery. [#160707](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160707). Thanks to [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit) and [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
  - Allow channel plugins to use optional asynchronous account hooks and allow Matrix persisted-state reads without blocking the Gateway thread. Bundled Matrix requires a matching V2 host. [#168481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168481). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Pace Matrix live-preview updates after slow sends and edits ([#169975](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169975)). Thanks to [xili-ytl](https://github.com/xili-ytl), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80) for the [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/106644).
  - Matrix tool and mention policies use room identity rather than an editable display name, while configured aliases remain supported. [#171645](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171645). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168134).
  - Ordinary authorized Matrix room messages follow configured reply visibility and silence policies, while actual commands still respond. [#179506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179506). Thanks to [xiaopan-xydt](https://github.com/xiaopan-xydt), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [doomy](https://github.com/doomy) for the report.
  - Suppress repeated incidental Matrix mention highlights during streaming previews while retaining final mentions ([#183372](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183372)). Thanks to [L4XB](https://github.com/L4XB) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore Matrix execution approval requests to the originating room and approver DM when the native handler is async-only ([commit 9356d64](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9356d64ceac6cc6187997677467584e10a3a9236)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Telegram handles more voice, progress and delivery-recovery cases. The first legacy-queue import marks messages older than 24 hours failed, even if they were never processed; opt-in recovery can still duplicate a send whose acknowledgment was lost.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Telegram polling starts in source-checkout and captured-plugin layouts, including packaged builds. [#154286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154286), [#154180](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/154180). Thanks to [@xydt-juyaohui](https://github.com/xydt-juyaohui), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@markfietje](https://github.com/markfietje).
  - Replaying the same audio message suppresses duplicate transcript echoes while its receipt is retained, up to 24 hours and subject to the receipt cap. [#162565](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162565), [related cross-account report #146311](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/146311). Thanks to [@dankarization](https://github.com/dankarization) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Group voice mentions are recognized when a group-scoped rule allows audio transcription. Direct-only rules continue to exclude group audio. [#163746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163746). Thanks to [@jewseppi](https://github.com/jewseppi) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep Telegram's temporary progress preamble visible during verbose tool runs while sending diagnostics separately. [#168225](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168225). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Show authored preamble text without the internal heading in Telegram previews. [#168281](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168281). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve tables and their placement when optional Telegram rich replies include neighboring quotes or lists. [#168383](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168383), [issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168366), [rich-message guide](/channels/telegram/rich-messages). Thanks [@dhruv1220](https://github.com/dhruv1220), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@barry-ssb](https://github.com/barry-ssb).
  - Count the full Telegram command progress row against the configured line limit. [#168464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168464), [issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130144). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [@ml12580](https://github.com/ml12580).
  - Render Telegram progress notes as prose instead of monospace code. [#168723](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168723). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Retry eligible saved Telegram final text once after an ambiguous network failure. [#168738](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168738). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Avoid duplicate Telegram answers in chat history when another message queues during streaming. [#169019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169019). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep Telegram messages retryable when a handler times out while the original attempt is still pending, including a slow voice note or a message waiting behind a busy turn. Existing retry limits apply. [#169202](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169202), [related issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127229). Thanks [@diregoblin](https://github.com/diregoblin), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@Grynn](https://github.com/Grynn), [@travellingsoldier85](https://github.com/travellingsoldier85), [@PaGrom](https://github.com/PaGrom) and [@cablackmon](https://github.com/cablackmon) for the reports and investigation.
  - Avoid a session-wide scan when mirroring Telegram deliveries in long histories ([#169663](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169663)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - On first legacy Telegram spool import, mark messages older than 24 hours failed instead of dispatching them. This also excludes genuinely unprocessed old messages and leaves previously imported rows unchanged ([#170068](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170068), [replay limits](/channels/telegram/transports)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [angeliti999](https://github.com/angeliti999) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169784).
  - Report fractional Telegram attachment limits accurately in warnings and model context ([#170677](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170677)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Wait for pending Telegram photo albums before ordinary follow-up text ([#170690](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170690)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Long streaming replies avoid repeated visibility-check work after a preview is visible. [#171417](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171417), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171416). Thanks to [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

See [Telegram troubleshooting](/channels/telegram/troubleshooting). [#171564](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171564). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Dat1GUI](https://github.com/Dat1GUI) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171438).

[#171810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171810). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@dankarization](https://github.com/dankarization) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171754).
  - Edited media captions render rich content and plain fallbacks correctly, while spoken-reply code keeps its spacing and checklist markers handle mixed-case input. [#171877](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171877). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Telegram polling on Bun can save its checkpoint and resume from it after a clean restart. [#178909](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178909). Thanks to [brianmcguire](https://github.com/brianmcguire), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [qwexs](https://github.com/qwexs) for the report.

Thanks to [wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web), [Card4Philippe](https://github.com/Card4Philippe), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  - Resolve the implicit default Telegram bot's root secrets alongside named accounts ([#183618](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183618)). Thanks to [555aaditya](https://github.com/555aaditya), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [rodricksz4h5](https://github.com/rodricksz4h5).

- Affected channel replies survive more queue, stream and reconnect failures without false delivery confirmations. Unknown sends are not blindly repeated.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Silent maintenance turns suppress attachments and other rich content as well as text, while preserving errors, actual voice messages and notices explicitly marked for delivery. [#129244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/129244). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Final answers retain text after internal tool blocks containing quoted closing markers. [#149775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149775). Thanks to [@bodapatisaikrishna](https://github.com/bodapatisaikrishna) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - Channel tools remain available to later messages after the agent or operator task that started the account ends. [#159396](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159396), [related #152965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/152965), [#158070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/158070) and [#161151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161151).
