  - Queued follow-ups and collected inputs use the current provider after a plugin reload, allowing the affected replies to resume. [#159715](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159715), [#158375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/158375). Thanks to [@mbelinky](https://github.com/mbelinky), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and reporters [@chenkai8654](https://github.com/chenkai8654), [@N4DH](https://github.com/N4DH) and [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
  - Eligible long replies using reply instructions avoid repeated parsing and can arrive faster. [#166782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166782), [#166781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166781). Thanks to [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve interrupted completion replies for recovery before later messages. [#167814](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167814), [issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167766), [restart recovery guide](/gateway/restart-recovery). Thanks [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@marknilsn](https://github.com/marknilsn).
  - Keep non-steering messages queued behind subagent or command announcements until cleanup finishes. [#168077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168077), [queue steering guide](/concepts/queue-steering). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep background task replies in their originating WebChat and preserve reply destinations when mirroring transcripts. Reef also stops treating a 40-character revision hash in a GitHub HTTPS commit, blob or tree link as a secret solely because of its entropy, while other checks still apply. [#168248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168248), [Reef guide](/channels/reef). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep recognized silent reasoning placeholders silent when their final token ends in punctuation. [#168571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168571), [related reports](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/165025) and [#138662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138662). Thanks [@liwenjie200543](https://github.com/liwenjie200543), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), reporters [@eonicman](https://github.com/eonicman) and [@cipp-ashe](https://github.com/cipp-ashe), and [@he-yufeng](https://github.com/he-yufeng) for the investigation.
  - Keep queue recovery from interrupting a message whose original sender is still active. [#169010](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169010). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Let ordinary non-steering queued messages without a source identity proceed when unnecessary recovery information cannot be read. [#169138](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169138). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Send an eligible replacement for an immediately failed reply before later replies in the same turn, including text replacing a failed voice note. Buffered delivery can place the replacement later ([#169305](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169305), [voice output guidance](/tools/tts/output)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include a redacted reply error in diagnostic events when diagnostics and debug or trace file logging are enabled ([#169503](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169503)). Debug logs can still contain private content. Thanks to [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc) and [keshavbotagent](https://github.com/keshavbotagent).
  - Record intentionally filtered ClickClack scaffold-only messages as suppressed instead of uncertain delivery ([#169509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169509)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve a completed answer after one invalidated session read ([#169987](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169987)). Thanks to [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Suppress token-only uppercase fragments of the silent reply marker while preserving attachments and configured heartbeat acknowledgements ([#170190](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170190)). Thanks to [maliv2](https://github.com/maliv2), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [rboy1](https://github.com/rboy1) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170158).
  - Keep a successful channel send marked delivered if a later delivery observer fails ([#170482](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170482)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep routine channel bookkeeping from making old conversations appear newly active ([#170743](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170743)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Wait for a pending current reply before sending replacement answers, media, or failure messages ([#170937](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170937)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Deliver a failure notice for eligible requester continuations refused at initial plugin-runtime admission during reload ([#171021](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171021)). Thanks to [johan-eilertsen](https://github.com/johan-eilertsen) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reply preparation no longer waits for unrelated session retention cleanup. [#171136](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171136). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Eligible Web Push notifications can send after pending session updates settle, and delivery failures have more useful logs. Failed sends are not automatically retried. [#171145](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171145). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Canceling an input during preparation preserves later queued work. [#171718](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171718). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Queued inbound messages can retry when preparation fails before durable acceptance, with later preparation failures handled without replay. [#172080](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172080), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171837). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@bitfroest](https://github.com/bitfroest) and [@nissl24](https://github.com/nissl24).
  - Stalled channel identity writes time out and retry safely instead of waiting indefinitely. If retries are exhausted, the error points to `openclaw gateway status --deep`. [#172244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172244). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@desksk](https://github.com/desksk) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172091).
  - Streaming ordinary technical prose that mentions tools or functions avoids repeated full-reply trace-filter scans. [#172631](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172631). Thanks to [JasonBuildAI](https://github.com/JasonBuildAI) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Channel replies already delivered or held by delivery recovery no longer produce a false missing-answer notice. [#173798](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173798). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Agent webhooks waiting for execution capacity can return an overload response instead of premature success. Session conflicts can also return their proper response. [#175111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175111). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - A rejected background typing callback is reported without stopping later keepalive ticks or causing an unhandled rejection. [#175381](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175381). Thanks to [musatoktas](https://github.com/musatoktas), [Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot) for the report, and [cxbAsDev](https://github.com/cxbAsDev) for the earlier proposal.
  - When recovery checks an interrupted send with an unknown outcome, matching permanent errors settle the delivery as failed while transient errors remain retryable. The uncertain message is not blindly resent. [#180767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180767). Thanks to [wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web) and [Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot) for the report.
  - The optional extendable reply wait keeps its intended time budget when the system clock changes. [#182057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182057). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let an admitted reply settle before a queued command takes over, preserving reply accounting and command order ([commit 3678292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3678292a09f253000fd9b475b262106fbe5fde1c)).
  - Keep streamed completion separate from pending source delivery so final replies and attachments are handled correctly, including during timeout recovery ([commit b52afef](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b52afef09152302461eea247e47a385cfe419366)).
  - WhatsApp socket retries keep the same message ID for one send, reducing the risk of duplicate messages after an ambiguous failure. [#142434](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142434), [#142398](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/142398), [related acknowledgement issue #122758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/122758). Thanks to [@ashawwal](https://github.com/ashawwal), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@he-yufeng](https://github.com/he-yufeng).
  - Synology Chat paces each webhook independently while preserving order and spacing for messages to the same webhook. [#144991](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144991), [#127491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127491). Thanks to [@Abhishek-B-R](https://github.com/Abhishek-B-R) and reporter [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Feishu sends a complete static reply when a streaming preview was accepted but the final update was rejected. The earlier preview remains visible. [#152086](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152086), [#139443](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139443). Thanks to [@kqtxmaster-boop](https://github.com/kqtxmaster-boop), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@ArthurNie](https://github.com/ArthurNie), [@Simon2256928](https://github.com/Simon2256928) and [@ruirui666888](https://github.com/ruirui666888).
  - A later decrypted WhatsApp message can reach the agent after an empty encrypted placeholder, while repeated copies remain suppressed. Messages already marked complete are not recovered by this change. [#162078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162078), [#161829](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161829). Thanks to [@stanleys12](https://github.com/stanleys12), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), reporter [@hundredhandsio](https://github.com/hundredhandsio), [@jmejia-appropia](https://github.com/jmejia-appropia) and [@gabrielrojasc](https://github.com/gabrielrojasc).
  - Keep Google Chat's missing-typing-message fallback working when a 404 response's error body cannot be read. [#168107](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168107). Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and reporter [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Ignore IRC CTCP queries before pairing or agent dispatch, while allowing normal messages and actions through. [#168113](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168113). Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for landing the fix.
  - Reef state operations run off the Gateway thread with less repeated database work. [#168340](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168340). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Accepted channel-bound ACP turns can deliver replies after five minutes without replaying their input. [#170880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170880), reported in [#170827](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170827). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@fanmeng-felix](https://github.com/fanmeng-felix).
  - Long channel `/compact` commands can finish within their configured timeout without being replayed by the ingress watchdog. [#171011](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171011), reported in [#170982](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170982), with a related proposal in [#171027](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171027). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@bluet](https://github.com/bluet), and [@PratikRai0101](https://github.com/PratikRai0101).
  - Stopping an ACP turn no longer suppresses a separate queued follow-up. Cancelling the follow-up itself still stops its output. [#170883](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170883), related to [#170833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170833). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@fanmeng-felix](https://github.com/fanmeng-felix).
  - A steered follow-up keeps its own failure reply and attachments after an earlier message-tool reply. [#170916](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170916). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Streamed tool progress settles more cleanly before the final answer preview.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Fit long tool titles within the configured progress line limit while retaining useful path endings and status. [#168312](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168312). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Send verbose progress once and settle it before streaming answer previews. [#168345](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168345). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Message actions and A2A replies stay attached to their initiating tasks, and unsupported actions fail explicitly rather than starting unrelated work.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Discord normalizes plugin command names and skips names over 32 characters with guidance for a shorter alias. Slack spelling is preserved. [#126643](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/126643), [#126633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/126633). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - WhatsApp login validates its `start` and `wait` actions before invoking either operation. [#158719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158719). Thanks to [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [@mcaxtr](https://github.com/mcaxtr).
  - A2A push configuration requests return the protocol's push-not-supported error. [#167594](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167594), [#167591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167591). Thanks to [@clawSean](https://github.com/clawSean) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reject A2A task references explicitly instead of starting unrelated work. To start a new task in the same conversation, omit `taskId` and reuse `contextId`. [#167596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167596), [issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167592). Thanks [@clawSean](https://github.com/clawSean) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep mention-like text literal in Slack compact progress posts and edits ([#169760](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169760)). Thanks to [KhanCold](https://github.com/KhanCold).
  - Retry Discord message edits, deletes, pins, and unpins after transient failures, including reconciliation of an ambiguous delete ([#169806](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169806)). Thanks to [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), and to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for confirming the merge.
  - Deliver Tlon events using CRLF or CR line endings or multiple data lines ([#170125](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170125)). Thanks to [ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559).
  - Keep A2A retries with a retained message ID on their original task and match replies to the tasks that started them ([#170469](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170469)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [rh7](https://github.com/rh7) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169916).
  - Keep the Gateway running when a Buzz relay disconnects during authentication ([#170591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170591)). Thanks to [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [jarvis-modern-mail](https://github.com/jarvis-modern-mail) for the [report and investigation](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/147269).

  </details>

- Reloading a messaging account no longer lets stale connection work interfere with the replacement connection in the affected channels.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Retire unused Telegram token throttlers after token rotation or account removal ([#169536](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169536)). Thanks to [xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee).
  - Keep Feishu replies working when a settings reload overlaps an older monitor's cleanup. [#169226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169226), [issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169190), [independent investigation](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170054). Thanks [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), reporter [@junniar](https://github.com/junniar) and [@PratikRai0101](https://github.com/PratikRai0101) for the independent investigation.
  - Keep a stopped Tlon account disconnected when in-flight reconnect authentication finishes ([#169267](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169267)). Thanks to [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Cancel an outstanding Zalo poll when its account stops or restarts ([#169270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169270)). Thanks to [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).

  </details>

- Discord voice consultations handle more voice-change cases, and IRC reconnects show more accurate status. IRC does not replay missed messages.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep Discord agent consultations working after an in-call voice change. Update and restart the Gateway to apply the fix ([#171029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171029), [voice selection guidance](/nodes/talk/realtime-sessions#choose-a-talk-voice-from-chat)). Thanks to [ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [brainmonger](https://github.com/brainmonger) for the [report and investigation](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171013).
  - Slack pin tables show the message text, author and timestamp, and message tables render remote values safely for terminals. [#171042](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171042), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171041). Thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - IRC status shows disconnection and reconnect errors during recovery, then clears the error after reconnection. [#171053](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171053), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/101083). Thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii) and [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino).
  - Incoming Feishu rich posts preserve nested backticks within one code block. [#171222](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171222). Thanks to [@rudycelekli](https://github.com/rudycelekli) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Channel logs identify the event and underlying error when refreshing a message claim encounters a storage failure. [#171365](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171365). Thanks to [@ynaamane](https://github.com/ynaamane), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@yolo521-bot](https://github.com/yolo521-bot) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171034).
  - Incoming Tlon messages preserve nested code fences for the assistant. [#171584](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171584). Thanks to [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559).
  - A2A filters host notices from peer replies and reports failed runs on their originating tasks. [#172064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172064). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171985).
  - Slack messages and rich-text tables retain dates supplied without fallback text, using UTC for the timestamp. [#172182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172182). Thanks to [@fhgffy](https://github.com/fhgffy) for the fix and [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172180), [@skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A pending Discord voice reply can continue in the original voice channel's text chat after the call ends. Explicit cancellation sends nothing. [#172380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172380). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@brainmonger](https://github.com/brainmonger) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172233).
  - IRC detects a silent connection failure and starts the existing reconnect and channel rejoin flow after roughly four minutes. Messages missed while disconnected are not replayed. [#172961](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172961). Thanks to [rodricksz4h5](https://github.com/rodricksz4h5) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Malformed mail and stalled channel-login or send attempts are handled more explicitly, allowing later valid work to proceed where supported.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - IMAP skips an unreadable email so later valid messages can reach automation. The original remains in the mailbox, and the skipped message is not processed or resubmitted through channel dead letters. [#173231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173231). Thanks to [pgondhi987](https://github.com/pgondhi987).
  - Slack relay recovery waits for received-event writes to finish after a socket error. [#173407](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173407). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mattermost slash commands and model-picker interactions explain when a full pairing queue prevented a request from being saved. The bot owner can approve or dismiss pending requests in Settings → Channels → DM access requests before the user retries. [#178581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178581). Thanks to [HeZ2z](https://github.com/HeZ2z), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for the report.
  - iMessage bridge recovery recognizes structured send deadlines so later replies can recover. Failed sends are not automatically replayed, and recovery can also be triggered while a send's delivery is unsettled. [#180900](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180900). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Slack Socket Mode retries an isolated `invalid_auth` and can reconnect without a Gateway restart. Three such failures without a successful connection still block the channel, and definitive credential failures block immediately. [#181056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181056). Thanks to [mushuiyu886](https://github.com/mushuiyu886) and [marns](https://github.com/marns) for the report.
  - A2A outbound sends with a missing peer URL or unresolved token are treated as permanent pre-dispatch failures, avoiding futile queue retries. Correct the configuration and reload when credentials cannot be resolved. [#181326](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181326). Thanks to [potoior](https://github.com/potoior) and [exGeni](https://github.com/exGeni).
  - SMS rejects unsupported prototype-named MMS content types before sending them to Twilio. Already-staged media with those types returns HTTP 415. [#181773](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181773). Thanks to [Jimmy-xuzimo](https://github.com/Jimmy-xuzimo), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) for the report.
  - Feishu and Lark QR registration keeps its intended polling window when the system clock changes during setup. [#182180](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182180). Thanks to [mikasa0818](https://github.com/mikasa0818) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Overlapping WhatsApp plugin instances keep separate quoted-message caches, so a replacement cannot read or clear the retiring instance's quotes. [#182550](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182550). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- Signal, Discord, iMessage, LINE and Mattermost previews and long replies receive targeted display and disconnect-status fixes.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Signal reports an unexpected event-stream closure as the reason for recovery, helping operators check the daemon or proxy. [#182746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182746). Thanks to [wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [888achtachtacht-commits](https://github.com/888achtachtacht-commits) for the report.
  - Recognize an iMessage self-chat reply as an echo when it returns under the chat ID. A same-text message can still be suppressed while a pending echo marker remains ([#183383](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183383)). Thanks to [sukhdeepjohar](https://github.com/sukhdeepjohar), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [kunalchaudhary82](https://github.com/kunalchaudhary82).
  - Keep WhatsApp QR login waits stable across system-clock changes ([#183394](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183394)). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Attempt to close the Discord connection when startup fails after an overlapping restart ([#184033](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184033)). Thanks to [MarMarLabs](https://github.com/MarMarLabs) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fix CRLF boundaries in long Discord replies while preserving line endings when inline code crosses a boundary ([#184435](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184435)). Thanks to [xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Follow reply policy and typing settings for LINE direct-chat loading animations ([#185306](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185306)). Thanks to [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Send a fresh Mattermost final post when newer relevant activity has overtaken its preview ([#185422](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185422)). Thanks to [hannnnn-l](https://github.com/hannnnn-l) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Avoid bursts of Mattermost preview edits after a slow send ([#185483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185483)). Thanks to [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Settle cancellation cleanly when an X account stops or reloads during subscription setup ([#186226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186226)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The merge includes Discord message-action retries and model status identifying the credential source selected for the current route ([commit 8548651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8548651ff64ef4086abc492ea357b53b0b9bb913)).
  - The merge includes the fix that stops pending Tlon reconnect work from reopening streams after shutdown ([commit 9a41e1b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9a41e1b76ec6f5903101d2570b38a8f456ece605)).

  </details>

- Fixes incorrect capacity defaults when creating a supported Codex configuration.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Corrected capacity defaults for supported GPT-5, GPT-5.1, GPT-5.2 and GPT-5.3 Codex deployment families apply to newly generated configurations. [#114891](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/114891). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@snotty](https://github.com/snotty) for related evidence.

  </details>

- Eligible built-in chat Goals continue across turns, and an achieved Goal can resume in the same conversation. Saved Goals do not start automatically at boot; this continuation path does not cover native Codex or external runtimes.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Continue eligible built-in chat Goals across turns until they finish, pause, stop, become blocked, or reach a limit. Saved Goals do not start work at boot. Native Codex and external runtimes are outside this continuation path. [#167897](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167897), thanks to [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Show Resume progress and an inline retry on desktop and mobile when the outcome is unconfirmed. [#169789](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169789), thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Achieved goals can be explicitly resumed without recreating the goal or conversation ([#171999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171999)). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) for reporting the issue.

  </details>

- Supported MCP events can trigger existing automation instructions. The opt-in integration requires a public HTTPS callback; receiving an event does not mean the work has finished.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Subscribe to supported MCP events and run existing automation instructions when they arrive. Forward only `/plugins/mcp-events/callback/` to the Gateway. [#165399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165399), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Same-conversation follow-ups retain authorized access and can rearm a one-shot check ([#176422](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176422)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Scheduled jobs retain their chosen account and permitted tools through more handoffs, and confirmed results stay in the receiving conversation where supported.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - New isolated jobs retain their selected inference account and report invalidated authority ([#172590](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172590)). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Scheduled CLI jobs can use their native login without silently adopting a stored OpenClaw profile ([#172717](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172717)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Scheduled Discord message reads and edits work with Claude CLI authentication without a conflicting host account binding ([#172794](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172794)). Thanks [@patrick-erichsen](https://github.com/patrick-erichsen).
  - Fix false authorization failures when concurrent scheduled runs use the same unchanged saved API-key or OAuth account. [#179333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179333), [issue #179331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/179331). Thanks to [brandon-julio-t](https://github.com/brandon-julio-t) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Automation owners replacing allowed tools can capture fresh native app access, with guidance when capture is unavailable ([#170498](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170498), [reported in #170298](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170298)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@fbuechler](https://github.com/fbuechler) for the report.
  - Scheduled agents without an interactive sender can inspect or remove their own job within existing permissions ([#170864](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170864)). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Keep permitted tools available to an accepted manual run after its initiating request ends. Revoking the initiating device after activation no longer stops that run; disabling or removing the job still cancels it. [#168037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168037), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Gateway-pinned commands reject conflicting host or node targets before execution ([#176997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176997), [#169256](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169256)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm) for the report.
  - Keep delivered agent, command, and script results in the receiving chat or topic. Cross-agent destinations and native-only content without a text representation can produce a warning instead of a conversation entry. [#168996](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168996), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [Kimiyu-186](https://github.com/Kimiyu-186).
  - Keep a repeated automation result in later model context. [#169369](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169369), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Allow confirmed automation announcements to be committed during an active conversation turn, also helping restart handoff proceed. [#181506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181506). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Announce cron wakes retain the context of an existing CLI-backed chat across the next reply ([#176218](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176218), [#176208](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176208)). Thanks [@eliavb43](https://github.com/eliavb43) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Targeted system events from the selected conversation reach the isolated heartbeat prompt, including when the regular heartbeat schedule is disabled ([#175642](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175642), [#175599](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/175599)). Thanks [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@seriouslysean](https://github.com/seriouslysean) for the report.
  - Repair eligible older dashboard chat roots with Doctor so linked automations regain the appropriate memory access. [#182345](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182345). Thanks to [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).

  </details>

- Eligible missed automation runs can catch up after restart or clock changes without repeating completed slots. Cancelled work stays cancelled, and uncertain effects are not automatically replayed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Share queueing and capacity across timed, manual, and event runs, with cancellation and recovery for eligible queued work. [#169364](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169364), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168072).
  - Restore recorded cron results once and mark uncertain active runs interrupted instead of replaying commands, tool effects, or deliveries. Queued timed requests can resume, while manual or event requests whose transient payload is lost are skipped. [#169946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169946), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168072).
  - Keep eligible dashboard automation recovery available when restart draining interrupts a turn. [#170162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170162), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An interrupted early manual run preserves a separate scheduled one-shot occurrence. The interrupted attempt remains failed with unknown delivery and is not replayed ([#171727](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171727)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recurring jobs advance past a cancelled stop-time slot without replaying it ([#171988](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171988), [#171896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171896)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for the report.
  - Restart catch-up avoids repeating slots covered by a successful or skipped long run while keeping genuinely missed and forced occurrences ([#172497](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172497)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recover eligible missed recurring slots on normal ticks. Jobs configured to skip missed runs continue to do so, and an idle scheduler does not wake before its stored deadline. [#179928](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179928), [issue #179927](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/179927). Thanks to [andrewbowlds](https://github.com/andrewbowlds) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore admission of eligible catch-up runs after a restart or forward clock jump. [#187558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187558). Thanks to [romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda), [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd), and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Blocker explanations no longer turn completed runs into execution failures ([#170267](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170267), related [#161698](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161698) and [#165811](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/165811)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Delegated scheduled work waits for a child's result after a heartbeat-only handoff and honors an intentionally silent child ([#172407](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172407)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Cancelled runs retain unfinished cleanup and delivery work while returning cancellation promptly ([#170264](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170264)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - On-exit watches wait for command process cleanup before proceeding ([#171832](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171832)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Request cancellation when an already paused job is explicitly disabled during a forced run. Cron tool guidance also now describes workspace and sandbox inheritance for current conversations correctly. [#182350](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182350). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep a stopped task cancelled across restart when a completed helper still had a pending continuation, while retaining the helper's result. [#169453](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169453), thanks to [freeqaz-openai](https://github.com/freeqaz-openai).
  - Optional late subagent and `sessions_send` results can settle silently while replies to actual requests remain required. [#172509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172509), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@tre1d](https://github.com/tre1d) for the [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/158332).

  </details>

- Automation history and alerts show clearer failure and delivery reasons, including when a one-time job needs Run Now or rescheduling.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Guide agents to continue in the same conversation after waiting, and request owner-conversation repair for eligible terminal one-time failures, with an alert fallback when repair cannot start. [#169102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169102), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [erlightbourn-source](https://github.com/erlightbourn-source) and [lonexreb](https://github.com/lonexreb) for identifying the failure-alert defect.
  - Keep cancelled or restart-retired one-shots quiet and record terminal failures for Doctor and eligible notifications or repair. [#169314](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169314), with related work in [#131590](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131590) and [#167908](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167908), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [lonexreb](https://github.com/lonexreb), and [erlightbourn-source](https://github.com/erlightbourn-source).
  - Avoid a premature still-failing alert when a recurring run began before repair finished. Jobs continue running during repair. [#170073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170073), [issue #170014](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170014), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Automatic repair waits keep their timeout across system clock changes ([#171097](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171097), [#170014](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170014), related [#170073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170073) and [#171094](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171094)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - A failure alert refused before sending can trigger the existing in-app fallback for the job owner ([#173347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173347), [#173077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/173077)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Winoren](https://github.com/Winoren) and to [@Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot) for the report.
  - Failed automation notices show a concise recovery summary while leaving the agent's response visible ([#170799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170799)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Tell you to use Run Now or reschedule when a local-provider failure leaves no future automatic run. [#169329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169329), with related local-provider alert work in [#169278](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169278), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep an unresolved announcement destination from turning a successful cron run into an execution error or triggering execution backoff. The report and delivery diagnostic remain in history. [#168380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168380), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Stop labeling unattributed channel failures as model timeouts in cron alerts and history. [#168091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168091), thanks to [KhanCold](https://github.com/KhanCold), [geekforlife](https://github.com/geekforlife) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167865), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for confirming the landing.
  - Preserve the scheduled turn's identity when a primary model times out and a configured fallback continues it. [#168509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168509), thanks to [synthalorian](https://github.com/synthalorian), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [wongwilliam425-ui](https://github.com/wongwilliam425-ui) for [the related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168456).
  - Let a configured fallback receive the original saved prompt after a provider timeout without duplicating it. [#168605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168605), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [wongwilliam425-ui](https://github.com/wongwilliam425-ui) for [the related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168456).
  - Avoid false authorization errors in authenticated local model server logs during cron connectivity checks. [#169795](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169795), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [franssauermann](https://github.com/franssauermann) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157140).
  - Avoid an extra unhandled-rejection warning when a recovered scheduled job fails. [#169525](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169525), thanks to [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).

  </details>

- Automation editing preserves the selected job through more navigation and reconnects. History search includes delivery errors, and Team Reports can pause automatic collection while keeping manual reports available.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Open older linked runs directly and return to the normal history with Show all runs. [#169023](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169023), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore the selected automation and linked run after reconnecting to the same Gateway. [#169032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169032), [issue #152963](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/152963), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
  - Reload the latest settings after a stale pause or resume is rejected, so you can review them and retry deliberately. [#169290](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169290), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve the selected editor and a submitted Create & run now action when navigating during creation. [#169698](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169698), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A just-completed job can be edited or disabled without a false state-change error ([#172528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172528)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Run-history search includes delivery error text ([#170958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170958)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Delivery account suggestions use configured IDs instead of display names ([#171057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171057), [#171056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171056), related [#134368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134368)). Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii).
  - Job action and run-history menus use shared focus and selection behavior ([#170917](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170917)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The Automations model catalog can load after a fresh agent database is initialized ([#171321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171321)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Give clearer local errors and schedule examples for malformed `cron add` and `cron edit` input. Natural-language schedules remain unsupported. [#168163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168163), thanks to [xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Command automations preserve an explicitly configured `__proto__` environment override ([#171770](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171770)). Thanks [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).
  - Isolated cron runs avoid duplicating prior session lineage, and cleanup processes older oversized entries in pages ([#172072](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172072), [#171913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171913)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for the report.
  - Cron history cleanup avoids loading unrelated large payloads, reducing the risk of shared state-worker exhaustion ([#174530](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174530), [#174078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174078)). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and to [@asikorskiy](https://github.com/asikorskiy) for the report.
  - Scheduled run activation and completion avoid repeated database work ([#170874](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170874)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Release a delayed cron write and retry so other state writes have a chance to proceed. [#168291](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168291), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168072).
  - Keep busy cron operations from holding the shared database writer while waiting for host callbacks. [#168619](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168619), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168072).
  - Apply cron job edits to current data so concurrent runtime updates are preserved. [#169766](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169766), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168072).
  - Keep published session metadata consistent with fresh reads to avoid false ownership conflicts in scheduled work. [#169899](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169899), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep conversations counted as running in Online while delegated descendants are active, without counting the same conversation twice. [#168186](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168186), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Combine eligible session-creation notices into a bounded Home summary, leaving room for reminders and other notifications. Older entries can be omitted and the pending summary is lost on restart. [#168239](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168239), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stop repeated Dreaming malformed-key log errors for heartbeats using a session named `heartbeat`. [#168502](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168502), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [michaelrobertexp-source](https://github.com/michaelrobertexp-source) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168021).
  - Standing-intent expiry and cooldown maintenance runs at known deadlines and catches up after restart ([#171234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171234)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Standing-intent fire limits and cooldowns accept valid numeric strings ([#172329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172329), [#172297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172297)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@daddyclint82](https://github.com/daddyclint82) for the report.
  - Team Reports can pause automatic scheduling without disabling manual reports or access to stored reports ([#171840](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171840)). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Reject a FIFO roster promptly while continuing to support regular JSON files and symlinks to them. [#183614](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183614). Thanks to [Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep hot reload running while cron monitor reconciliation has a scheduled retry. [#170951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170951), [report #170644](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170644), earlier [report #135433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/135433) and [#148592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148592). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Navras98](https://github.com/Navras98), and [@davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini).

  </details>

- Worker-backed conversations tell you when setup is missing or a request produced no answer, rather than leaving the failure unexplained.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Early worker replies and workspace settlement ([#169484](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169484)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce repeated placement reads during paired-node worker turns, improving first-token time in the reported warm-turn measurements. [#168799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168799) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Skip empty result staging when both workspaces match the accepted base, reducing work at the end of unchanged node turns. [#168933](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168933) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse approved model and credential preparation across provider requests within a Gateway-proxied node turn. [#169294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169294) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce repeated bundle inspection during node retention by reusing installed status for the current connection. A bundle altered during that connection may not be detected until launch, installation or explicit inspection. [#169325](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169325) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid full retention cleanup on capacity-only node updates. Inventory-only cleanup waits for a successful periodic reconciliation. [#169371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169371) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse successful disk samples for unchanged idle worker placements for up to 15 minutes. Activity or runner changes prompt a refresh on the next maintenance pass. Disk-pressure warnings remain advisory. [#169712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169712) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session admission retries once when an unrelated worker placement change interrupts it. [#170085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170085) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Required worker inference supports explicit OpenClaw runtime and model selection when the Gateway has no provider credentials. [#170132](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170132) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - After a Gateway restart, recovery of interrupted worker tasks is attempted before a continuation or immediate follow-up starts. [#170161](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170161) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - If a native worker disappears mid-reply while the Gateway remains running, received reply text is saved to history on a best-effort basis and errors retain a clearer cause. Tool progress is not resumed. [#170223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170223) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Node-local inference reports missing model or credential setup instead of misleading update or authentication errors. [#170257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170257) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Worker turns without an answer request one after settled tools or fail visibly if the reply remains empty. A still-running background command is reported as such. [#170426](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170426) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Cloud worker turns accept longer provider response IDs when both the Gateway and worker use the updated validator. [#170461](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170461) by [@sallyom](https://github.com/sallyom).
  - The worker inference guide clarifies that optional workers handle bound sessions while sessionless helpers run on the Gateway. A required profile blocks those helpers rather than moving them to the worker. [#170012](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170012) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Worker setup refuses unsafe cache folders, and eligible capacity becomes available again after a confirmed host reboot.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Rejected known symlinked project-cache parents before worker toolchain setup ([#172079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172079)). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Honored Crabbox worker lifetimes during preparation and capture and waited for confirmed remote gate cleanup. Crabbox 0.77.0 or newer is required ([#172442](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172442)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Sanitized paired-node camera metadata in the human-readable camera list. JSON retains the original values ([#175624](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175624)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Let unrelated sessions write between commands in an append batch. [#171225](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171225) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Provide a source-checkout report for slow session updates. [#171148](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171148) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show phase timings for slow cloud worker dispatch when diagnostics are enabled. [#171749](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171749) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Expose worker message counts and known payload bytes by direction and kind in Prometheus. [#171940](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171940) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Release supported native worker capacity after a confirmed host reboot. Older records without a saved boot identity and hosts where it cannot be read retain conservative recovery behavior. [#168863](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168863) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Eligible project conversations can start responding while checkout finishes, and supported text resets retain their project folders. Workspace tools wait until preparation is ready; New Chat still leaves the previous worktree.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep more default agent guidance within small bootstrap budgets for newly generated workspaces. Existing workspace files are not rewritten ([#89894](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/89894), [report #75187](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/75187)). Thanks to [dwc1997](https://github.com/dwc1997), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [camerono](https://github.com/camerono).
  - Let the opt-in continuation-skip mode stop repeating workspace instructions after successful delivery in an already onboarded workspace ([#154923](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154923), [report #149867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/149867), [workspace configuration](/gateway/config-agents/workspace-and-bootstrap)). Thanks to [sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [playhard94](https://github.com/playhard94).
  - Preserve native managed worktrees, projects, and working directories through conversation resets. Control UI New Chat still leaves the previous worktree ([#160549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160549), [earlier rollover work #159506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159506) by [chelsealong](https://github.com/chelsealong)). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Avoid false worktree allocation failures after a brief stall ([#168214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168214)). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Preserve partial-clone filters and reuse clean worktree snapshot trees, leaving the checkout in place if required objects are missing ([#168682](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168682)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recover the committed values of local workspace edits after a lost worker reply ([#168094](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168094)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Omit confirmed directories from file-only session activity ([#167732](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167732), [related issue #96660](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/96660)). Thanks to [goldarowana](https://github.com/goldarowana), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and reporter [mmhzlrj](https://github.com/mmhzlrj).
  - Refuse `openclaw path set` edits on invalid UTF-8 in the opt-in oc-path plugin, preserving the original bytes. Repair or convert a backed-up copy before retrying ([#168491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168491), [related report #114895](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/114895)). Thanks to [wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and reporter [yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Start eligible conversations before checkout completes, with workspace-dependent tools waiting for readiness. Repositories with setup hooks and other incompatible paths still prepare fully first. [#169136](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169136) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Share default-branch preparation across concurrent creations while preserving a busy primary checkout. [#169070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169070) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Make a prepared, reserved worktree available during parallel creation without waiting again for unrelated allocation. [#169178](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169178) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse a successful default-base preparation for up to 30 seconds and allow an explicit ref to select an update immediately. [#169590](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169590) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore filesystem template use for full worktrees when a sibling uses sparse checkout. Targets with sparse settings, filters or external attributes continue through Git. [#169359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169359) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse validated templates during concurrent Btrfs checkouts and choose Git for later creations when a warm Btrfs checkout measures more than 250 ms slower. [#169440](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169440) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce resource use during bursts of worktree-backed session creation. [#168807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168807) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Free checkout names sooner during ordinary forced cleanup and delete retired files in the background. A crash or failed deletion can leave an inspectable `.openclaw-removing-*` directory. [#169690](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169690) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recover available missing partial-clone objects during an explicit deferred cleanup retry. Recovery is bounded and can continue on another retry. [#168864](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168864) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve accepted first messages and start their runs during concurrent session creation. [#169107](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169107) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Project lists and workspace preparation avoid more unnecessary waits when several conversations create or archive worktrees at once.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Return a conflict instead of overwriting a session created by a competing request. [#169625](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169625) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let ordinary session and transcript reads proceed while an unrelated write is busy. [#169838](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169838) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Project listings and the project picker no longer fail just because an unrelated session changes during discovery. [#170824](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170824) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Worktrees tabs support keyboard navigation and visible selection and focus in forced-colors mode. [#170446](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170446) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Managed-worktree branch suggestions can remain cached for up to 30 seconds. The chosen ref is validated when a worktree is created. [#170626](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170626) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). The related [#138555](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138555), reported by [@aleps001](https://github.com/aleps001), concerned a child-answer problem fixed earlier.
  - Concurrent sessions can read an actively retained warm worktree template without waiting for a mutation lock. [#170903](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170903) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Managed repository maintenance can remove redundant Git packs, including duplicates previously skipped by consolidation limits. [#170935](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170935) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Worktree creation errors include redacted timing details when preparation stalls. [#170835](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170835) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Slow session update logs distinguish archive and other request phases. [#170961](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170961) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid redundant history downloads when preparing pinned commits in existing partial clones. [#170970](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170970) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Related pack consolidation work in [#170935](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170935).
  - Reduce capacity admission waits for concurrent repository-backed worktree creation. [#170983](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170983) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Share pending template preparation waits among concurrent creates in the same process. [#171075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171075) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Repair eligible read-only directories during orphan cleanup and continue cleaning other worktrees when one fails. After fixing access, retry deferred cleanup with `openclaw worktrees gc --retry-deferred`. [#171305](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171305) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse unchanged branch inventories in repositories with large pack directories. [#171604](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171604) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Limit repeated Git housekeeping during frequent worktree archives while cleanup continues. [#171680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171680) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep Gateway Git inspection local in partial clones. Optional facts may be unavailable when their objects are missing, while explicit transfers remain available. [#171799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171799) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Allow eligible partial-clone pack consolidation after a deferred snapshot without fetching promised objects. [#171841](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171841) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Detect the Git object format directly during source-only worktree preparation, including repositories without a HEAD commit. [#171856](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171856) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Guest project choices and cleanup previews stay usable through more refresh and maintenance failures. Protected work and backup consent requirements still apply.

  - Saved guest New Session choices in the guest's own profile ([#172114](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172114), [#172083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172083)). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).

  - Continued verified pack cleanup despite missing snapshot objects and reported maintenance progress ([#171978](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171978)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stopped managed cloning on destination inspection errors ([#175952](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175952)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduced redundant Git processes during session refresh and worktree path discovery ([#172472](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172472)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep verified migration backups eligible for `openclaw update cleanup` after Doctor rewrites the database directory. Existing consent and verification requirements still apply; this does not repair backups already blocked by an earlier rewrite or remove retained NOCOW directories. [#169893](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169893) by [@xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [@farisoftfry](https://github.com/farisoftfry) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169674).
  - Restore guest worktree selection for registered and cloned projects without exposing host paths. [#171811](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171811), related to [#171792](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171792), by [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
  - Allow accepted guest child sessions to start after the parent yields, while retaining guest permissions. [#171668](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171668) by [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).

  </details>

- Staged new files and deletions made by a worker return to the accepted workspace more completely. Unstaged new files stay on the worker.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Newly staged Gateway files join the accepted workspace after reconciliation. Unstaged files remain outside that synchronization. [#170163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170163) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recovery retains the accepted workspace baseline so later worker deletions of synchronized files apply correctly. [#170213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170213) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Transferred folder repositories retain a credential-free HTTP, HTTPS or Git origin without a query or fragment when the node checkout has none. [#170224](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170224) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Accepted file updates preserve node-local Git history, refs, HEAD and remote settings. Node commits remain local and do not advance Gateway HEAD. [#170338](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170338) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Workspace reconciliation can continue after the reply writer closes. [#170556](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170556) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session views can leave the reconciling state when workspace settlement finishes. [#170575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170575) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Workspace tools remain usable after a session resumes from a child result or another internal continuation. [#170791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170791) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Worker recovery can find a placement added during an overlapping read on its next sweep. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3d99ff35fe59104aa3a66b5328e1d2c9ce30f044)
  - Initial worker session creation keeps its placement stable while the first turn is claimed. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1ac1a34c11e43006ea9d6b822593495782976505)
  - Required-worker chats stay on the worker admission path across policy reloads. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f586b491f164fb6cf6d793999d2b4bca294d7031)
  - Valid required-worker turns can complete and publish workspace results. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a1ac97bcbe7b4e4b2985f4deccbb4856b2131581)
  - Node worker downloads reuse validated content for repeated files within one transfer, while keeping the resulting files independent. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5268c0790b7fbfcc395cc1e42a9c5384bc10c335)
  - Portable worker archives support database admission after relocation. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9e52f76fa4861178687812a00416531c892ed0df)
  - New Gateway worktree files staged with `git add` can join the accepted workspace at the next completed cloud worker reconciliation. Unstaged new files remain local. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/85bebbe71f9bb7cb308d223e1bc7afaf0dbb6f7a)
  - Eligible agent sessions with matching agents, directories and equivalent runtime configurations can reuse the prepared workspace while keeping their requested execution directory. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/59b6c109167434cac64ea41fecda871501374750). Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman) and [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).

  </details>

- Sandboxed agents regain affected attachment, fetched-file and skill paths without acquiring host CLI access. Locally generated media can be copied back into their requesting workspace for reuse.

[#171938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171938) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@0xtc23](https://github.com/0xtc23) for the original work.

  - Telegram reply targets and plugin loading appear in this [integration merge](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/23c02ca32c4817929ebcea3ae9c1e206b19c1145).
