  - Reported portal access problems to the requesting browser and preserved open app state ([#172366](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172366)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Corrected source lines for `openclaw path resolve` and `openclaw path find` after standalone carriage returns ([#184866](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184866)). Thanks [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reduced retained memory for prepared session forks with Unicode-heavy requests ([#172032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172032)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Fixes affected Linux installs failing when the filesystem refuses a reflink copy; covered update errors keep credentials masked.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Linux snapshot copying can recover from ZFS reflink refusals that blocked Gateway startup and Doctor. [#170812](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170812) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [@Glukmann](https://github.com/Glukmann) for reporting [#169071](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169071).
  - Credentials in host process cleanup errors are masked before they reach retained results or notifications. [#170941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170941) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), following [#169063](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169063).
  - Preserved OpenShell copies and the remote skills view when local inspection fails ([#176458](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176458)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restored compatible Node runtime floors and added security patch warnings ([#186589](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186589)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Backported the compatibility and advisory changes for installs and updates ([#187626](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187626)). Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- You can undo a Skill Workshop review, restoring edited skills and archiving skills it created. Change notices follow the current conversation rather than a reset or deleted one.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Review-wide undo restores edited skills and archives skills created by the review. Button-capable channels can offer Undo, and plain-text channels can use `/learn undo <id>`. [#167996](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167996). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Background reviews wait for ten model iterations, so feedback can take longer to appear. A foreground skill change resets the count. [#168679](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168679). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Skill dependencies that explicitly request extraction can use a supported archive filename supplied by a generic download endpoint. [#141312](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141312). Thanks [@hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - The environment reference documents the existing ClawHub install telemetry opt-out, `CLAWHUB_DISABLE_TELEMETRY`. [#114376](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/114376). Thanks [@kobihikri](https://github.com/kobihikri) and [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Change the default destination for background skill notices, with optional channel delivery and Undo. [#170025](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170025) by [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Suppress outdated change and Undo notices when the reviewed session has been reset, replaced, or deleted. [#170035](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170035) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Guide background reviews toward focused, reusable procedures. [#170277](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170277) by [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Skill discovery preserves more icons and metadata, retries safe reads through device transitions and notices external edits on refresh. A broken ignore file excludes its directory until repaired.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Refresh external file-backed skill changes on a coalesced schedule, while explicit actions request an immediate scan. [#170341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170341) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Settings saves and agent creation no longer wait for a healthy skill discovery process to retire during configuration refresh. [#171157](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171157) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include the visible skill location and host guidance in exact-name reads. [#170965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170965) by [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Retry safe Skill discovery and reads after a temporary paired-node transition. [#171208](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171208) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Check installer readiness only for selected skill dependencies. [#170967](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170967) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve skill icons and homepages when inline YAML metadata has a trailing comment. [#171224](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171224) by [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).
  - Reuses published workspace skills during unrelated background scans. First discovery and explicit refresh still wait for publication. [#171857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171857), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuses shared skill-root reads within a multi-workspace scan. [#171871](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171871), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@SBC2016](https://github.com/SBC2016) for the [related Windows startup report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170928).
  - Reduces memory used for skill discovery in a measured workload. [#171285](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171285), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keeps detached Skill Workshop reviews out of user-only reply hooks. [#171651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171651), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Rejects local skill installs whose required description contains only a YAML comment. [#171812](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171812), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Skip a skills directory and warn when its ignore file is broken or unreadable. Repair the file so the directory can be included in a later scan. [#176681](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176681). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Sanitize skills library metadata in human-readable terminal output. JSON and requested file contents remain verbatim and may contain terminal controls. [#178313](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178313). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Honor an empty bundled-skill allowlist in skill status and model prompts. [#182731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182731), thanks to [SC-10749](https://github.com/SC-10749) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [Card4Philippe](https://github.com/Card4Philippe) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182637).
  - Preserve the completed-turn anchors needed for Workshop learning when an identical prompt snapshot is recorded during a later turn. [Commit 5a1cd1a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5a1cd1abf119a7393a3004acaafd6b18299ecf81).
  - Keep a background Workshop self-learning review running through later conversation turns. A session reset or permission change still cancels it. [Commit 3dc2d7a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3dc2d7ab2dc22acd7cc96dd267dcb0967bd31f6b).

  </details>

- Trusted connected apps can use the opt-in MCP relay without sharing Gateway credentials or opening an inbound Gateway port. Trust the relay with the traffic it carries, and review the access granted to each app.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - The disabled-by-default relay supports Gateway sign-in or a single-use pairing code. Trusted bundled and verified official plugins can provide remote Control UI and Gateway access under a person’s grant without sharing Gateway credentials. [#168233](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168233), [#170540](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170540). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- MCP tools can reconnect for later calls after a server restart, and repeated structured results no longer appear twice. Uncertain in-flight calls are not replayed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Legacy SSE MCP tools reconnect after a server restart changes the session endpoint, allowing later calls. An uncertain in-flight call is not replayed. [#167423](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167423), [issue #167371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167371). Thanks [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@abacha](https://github.com/abacha), and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve explicit MCP configuration during reloads and remove an unintended separator from later streamed text blocks. A request that races a reload may return a supersession error and can be retried. [#168981](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168981), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Remove duplicate MCP structured results even when the JSON text uses different formatting, while retaining distinct text and recovery guidance. [#169180](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169180), thanks [@xilopaint](https://github.com/xilopaint) for the fix and [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169121), and [@jipeng6036-del](https://github.com/jipeng6036-del) for the implementation.

  </details>

- Connected MCP Apps keep unsaved input and scroll position when minimized and reopened, and affected sign-in and Safari startup paths work again.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep unsaved input and scroll position in a loaded Dashboard MCP App when its side panel is minimized and reopened while its connection remains valid. [#169441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169441), thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Accept the matching portless loopback registration during MCP sign-in. [#170876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170876) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [9fw2pq8sgb-art](https://github.com/9fw2pq8sgb-art) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170848). See the [MCP transport guidance](/cli/mcp/transports).
  - Keep the elapsed-time budget for pending MCP tool cleanup during clock changes. [#170495](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170495) by [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), with test contributions from [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Restores conversation recovery after removing an unavailable stdio server on the affected POSIX runtime. [#171397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171397), thanks to [@joelagnel](https://github.com/joelagnel). The [related Windows issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw-windows-node/issues/1717) has other unresolved symptoms.
  - Preserves intentional leading and trailing whitespace in `mcp add` executable and working-directory paths. [#171620](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171620), thanks to [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Checks authored MCP configuration for literal secrets, including values in included files, instead of warning on populated environment references. [#171737](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171737), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini). See [the reported warning](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171469).
  - Expands MCP Relay guidance for Codex, multiple Gateways and Team access, and corrects the embedded Control UI credential guidance. [#171575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171575), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- MCP failures identify the relevant server, and accepted long-running calls avoid some premature timeouts. Unsupported or uncertain requests still need inspection before retrying.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Accept mixed-case JSON content types from authenticated local MCP clients. [#172290](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172290). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reject external-link requests from retired or unfocused embedded MCP frames. [#172312](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172312). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Name the server and suggest checking credentials or signing in again when HTTP MCP initialization receives an authentication refusal. [#172331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172331). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@rclarke0](https://github.com/rclarke0) for the report.
  - Let quiet HTTP MCP tool calls use their configured request timeout without an earlier stream inactivity cutoff. [#172510](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172510). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@xilopaint](https://github.com/xilopaint) for the report.
  - Release callbacks after MCP Events requests complete and retain a redacted cause when a transport fails. [#172613](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172613). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include the server name in accessible names for MCP sign-in and enable or disable actions. [#178162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178162). Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm) and [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
  - Attempt to remove temporary MCP configuration directories after failed Claude or Gemini CLI writes, including partial files that may contain credentials. Removal is best effort. [#179603](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179603). Thanks [@xiaopan-xydt](https://github.com/xiaopan-xydt) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@YangManBOBO](https://github.com/YangManBOBO) for the partial-write reproduction.
  - Mask recognized credentials in MCP CLI list and show output while preserving saved credentials. [#180321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180321). Thanks [@brandon-julio-t](https://github.com/brandon-julio-t), and [@wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web) for regression coverage.
  - Keep long MCP event descriptions from splitting an emoji or other supplementary character at the existing length limit. [#181741](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181741). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Let accepted MCP relay runs continue beyond the submission deadline while retaining the deadline for unaccepted requests. [#181863](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181863). Thanks [@crwohlfeil](https://github.com/crwohlfeil) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Restore MCP App initialization in affected Safari and macOS WebKit setups. [#182485](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182485), thanks to [anmol0b](https://github.com/anmol0b) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [technicalpickles](https://github.com/technicalpickles) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164447).
  - Keep the MCP App raw-details menu usable across transcript refreshes. [Commit fcfcc06](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/fcfcc060446494b1188b503b1c476c78a284a090).
  - Replace an unexpectedly closed MCP loopback listener on the next CLI-backed run. Already failed calls are not retried automatically. [#183352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183352), thanks to [SC-10749](https://github.com/SC-10749) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [eli-pavlov](https://github.com/eli-pavlov) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139340).
  - Allow the next turn to recover after a remote MCP session deletion fails when local client and transport cleanup succeeds. The remote session can remain until it expires. [#182189](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182189), thanks to [aniketkrs](https://github.com/aniketkrs) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [goslingmanagment](https://github.com/goslingmanagment) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182174).
  - Keep MCP request deadlines and human-input allowances stable across system clock changes. [#184056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184056), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Preserve meaningful whitespace in MCP TLS client certificate and key paths. Previously saved paths that were already trimmed cannot be reconstructed automatically. [#181883](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181883), thanks to [wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Return the standard structured JSON failure for MCP relay commands. [#183361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183361), thanks to [Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve complete Unicode characters in the retained tail of a long MCP proxy error. [#183942](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183942), thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update MCP schema URI parsing to reject malformed identifiers and retain valid relative references. Previously accepted malformed schemas may now fail validation. [#187285](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187285), thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Plugin install and inspection failures give clearer reasons without blocking inspection of healthy plugins.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Plugin command failures show actionable reasons while preserving JSON and debug output. [#168850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168850). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - QA Lab lets accepted suites finish during shutdown, while interrupted plugin CLI changes stop retrying without replaying an uncertain accepted operation. [#158087](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158087). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Local plugin and hook writes refuse conflicts with the Gateway state owner and explain how to retry. Existing Gateway plugin routes and offline marketplace reads remain available. [#168757](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168757). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Repeated plugin enable or disable requests leave the config file unchanged when the requested state is already applied. [#168633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168633). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Updates avoid repeatedly adding a redundant bundled plugin load path that Doctor removes. [#168226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168226). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Registry inspection clears a stale warning for a removed plugin load path. Use `openclaw plugins registry --refresh` to update the persisted ledger. [#168167](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168167), [issue #167847](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167847). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@giodl73-repo](https://github.com/giodl73-repo).
  - Standalone plugin setting maps show their Custom entries heading again, including in filtered results. [#157903](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157903). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen) and [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - External TypeScript plugins using NodeNext-style relative imports can start faster, with gains depending on the plugin graph. [#168391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168391), [issue #168130](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168130). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@jakeaadams](https://github.com/jakeaadams) for the report.
  - Configured [plugin tool guards](/plugins/hooks/tool-policy) run during standalone `openclaw agent exec` and can block commands before execution. [#159333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159333), [issue #158824](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/158824). Thanks [@joseairosa](https://github.com/joseairosa) for the report and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Summaries and new model completions from retained plugins can continue after an unrelated plugin reload. [#168916](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168916), thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman) for the fix and [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156026).
  - Inspect an incompatible plugin and its repair guidance without blocking inspection of healthy plugins. The incompatible plugin remains blocked from running. [#168977](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168977), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Fixes affected plugin changes using stale settings or conflicting with cleanup. Some legacy plugins need a restart after retired files are removed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Clean up aged tokenless plugin files after the grace period, including files that are still open. Affected legacy producers may need a restart if their files are reclaimed. [#169198](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169198), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Dhotei](https://github.com/Dhotei) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/165035).

  - Deliver queued next-turn plugin context to Codex, ACPX, Agents API and Copilot, including on a retry within the same run. [#169558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169558), thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Report an error to extensions when cleanup of a canceled command cannot be confirmed, with the original command result available as the cause. [#169629](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169629), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Apply the subagent concurrency cap to plugin background completions. [#170273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170273) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [rogerallen1](https://github.com/rogerallen1) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/95746).

[#170392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170392) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170334).
  - Remove confirmed completed migration warnings from update results and history while retaining warnings for pending work. [#170413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170413) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), with thanks to [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for the report and follow-up. See [update status and history](/cli/update/status-and-history).
  - Show state-maintenance and ownership recovery reasons in ordinary plugin command output. [#169907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169907) by [ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [jamiezigelbaum](https://github.com/jamiezigelbaum) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169862).

[#170646](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170646) by [licheer-zte](https://github.com/licheer-zte), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [paulcam206](https://github.com/paulcam206). See the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170409).

  </details>

- Supported Gateway-hosted conversations can reload a plugin and continue with the result. If the Gateway exits before the result arrives, inspect plugin state before retrying.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Lets an agent in a Gateway-hosted conversation with turn-yield support use `reloadAndResume` to end its active turn, reload a plugin and continue with the result. If the Gateway exits before the result arrives, inspect plugin state before retrying. [#171257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171257), thanks to [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson) as co-author and for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170881), and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for integration work.
  - Registers hooks from explicitly enabled standalone plugin files, including tool-call policies. Listing a file in load paths alone does not enable it. See the [tool-policy guidance](/plugins/hooks/tool-policy). [#171330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171330), thanks to [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for integration and verification.
  - Lets plugin install, enable, disable and reload commands wait through brief contention when the Gateway confirms the operation did not start. Uncertain outcomes are not replayed. [#171390](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171390), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Removes managed plugins' diagnostic subscriptions when their instances retire, including on reload or unload. Other resources such as timers and sockets still need their own cleanup. [#172052](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172052), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keeps plugin installation progress usable across hover and pinning, and returns focus to the install button when Escape closes it. [#171346](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171346), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuses preparation for plugin inventories and stops reporting missing dependencies for providers disabled by discovery policy. [#171356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171356), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Defers llama.cpp execution modules until first use and reuses checked plugin paths during POSIX validation. [#171319](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171319), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keeps bundled plugin browser builds from rewriting tracked source manifests while using freshly built browser metadata. [#172037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172037), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the Gateway responsive to requests while large external plugin packages are prepared. Plugin initialization and synchronous CLI loading can still block. [#172750](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172750). Thanks [@ExoHumann](https://github.com/ExoHumann), and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for the related report.
  - Preserve settings and enabled or disabled policy when reinstalling or updating plugins with mixed-case IDs. Existing ambiguous disabled aliases may need manual recovery with `openclaw plugins enable <plugin-id>`. [#180789](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180789). Thanks [@anmol0b](https://github.com/anmol0b), and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for the report and earlier fix.
  - Keep invalid numeric plugin settings on the same setup question so they can be corrected. [#176787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176787). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Clean up eligible retired plugin files periodically while the Gateway is running. Protected and mapped files remain in place. [#176851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176851). Thanks [@harjothkhara](https://github.com/harjothkhara) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@PaulD-LeWagon](https://github.com/PaulD-LeWagon) and [@viernesmybot](https://github.com/viernesmybot) for their reports.
  - Avoid false hook isolation failures when shared-memory properties are omitted from the cloned input, and reduce snapshot work. Shared memory delivered to a hook remains blocked. [#172301](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172301). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve system events that plugins select for deferred delivery. [#181138](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181138). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Installed plugin lists can appear before remote catalog details finish loading, and cleanup handles more affected retirement failures.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep plugin service startup, reload and stop timeout budgets stable when the system clock changes. [#181663](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181663). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep plugin instance cleanup's timeout budget stable across system-clock changes. [#181675](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181675). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore catalog and chat activity artwork for MCP Relay and Muse Code. [#172299](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172299). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore catalog and chat activity artwork for Advisor and MCP Events. [#172716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172716). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Show normal-level warnings when plugin cleanup fails during session reset or deletion. Cleanup remains best effort. [#172513](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172513). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot) for the report.
  - Warn when a document extractor or bundled web provider fails to initialize while another remains available. [#174299](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174299). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include a bounded, redacted explanation in failed 1Password resolver responses. After restoring secret access, run `openclaw secrets reload` to retry affected owners. [#177060](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177060). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@gisk0](https://github.com/gisk0) for the related report.
  - Keep long plugin runtime errors from splitting an emoji or other supplementary character at the existing length limit. [#181742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181742). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Return installed plugin rows while remote catalog enrichment runs. A refresh shows completed details. [#184921](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184921), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve the exact stale install record during removal when its name collides with another plugin's alias. [#184628](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184628), thanks to [Links2008](https://github.com/Links2008) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and to [itanyplus](https://github.com/itanyplus) for [the related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182635).

  </details>

- Plugin setting forms and uninstall previews handle more stale results, masked secrets and mixed-case identities without losing the intended action.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Strip terminal controls and escape line breaks in plugin names shown in uninstall previews. [#183708](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183708), thanks to [wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep a late inspection result from showing a stale error or replacing pending uninstall controls. [Commit 1ac5ebc](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1ac5ebc04789ec97e017f185fbf85786cac5d0d4).
  - Show plugin action notices inside the overview detail panel. [Commit 35362bd](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/35362bd45e293a9144625853966be85374b31e2c).
  - Prevent a failed plugin's cleanup from interrupting healthy plugin and model preparation. [#183660](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183660), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let plugin setup retirement return its timeout result even when artifact removal is slow. Removal can continue afterward. [#187562](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187562), thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Reuse a live plugin inspection during model-catalog preparation without a duplicate registration error. [#187567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187567), thanks to [romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject malformed plugin SVG transforms without freezing the Control UI. [#182674](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182674), thanks to [SebTardif](https://github.com/SebTardif) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve Unicode characters at the length limits of ClawHub MCP scope and setup previews. [#182753](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182753), thanks to [wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666).
  - Complete authorized `reloadAndResume` requests after turn cleanup while retaining permission and revocation checks. [#182929](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182929), thanks to [PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [lisandromachado](https://github.com/lisandromachado) for [the related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180662).
  - Save unrelated Control UI settings when an affected plugin secret reference is fully masked. Changing its backend still requires an explicit ID. [#183571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183571), thanks to [lonexreb](https://github.com/lonexreb) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [Valkster70](https://github.com/Valkster70) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/183562).
  - [Decision-provider configuration in plugin inspection](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a07acefb6abb8efe41aacd271aeca81cb71fa2bc).

  </details>

- Plugin authors get supported asynchronous alternatives and clearer migration guidance for affected storage and session APIs. Migrate the announced synchronous calls by December 31, 2026; some unsupported calls already refuse synchronous use.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Async conversation route-owner preparation replaces deprecated synchronous hooks for plugin authors. [#169593](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169593), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Legacy synchronous Mention Inbox recording queues work after commit, with `recordCommittedInputAsync` available when completion is needed. [#169594](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169594), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Async alternatives cover session, approval routing, sandbox and harness reads while synchronous adapters remain deprecated. [#169622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169622), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Remove five never-stable synchronous SDK names and retain published compatibility APIs. The affected names are `withOpenClawAgentDatabaseRuntime`, `loadMemorySessionMetadataBatch`, `createAccountScopedBindingAdapter`, `prepareConversationRouteOwners`, and `captureSessionEntryCurrentCheck`. [#170034](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170034) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update Plugin SDK and database worker migration guidance for awaited APIs. [#169879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169879) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore the deprecated session header link helper for existing browser plugins. Current headers do not provide a link for it, so migrate to a custom accessory before its planned removal after January 12, 2027. [#169746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169746) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Restore 29 private SDK exports used by existing community plugins while their authors migrate. [#173777](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173777). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and [@FtlC-ian](https://github.com/FtlC-ian) for the report.
  - The keyed-state V2 store supports awaited operations and conditional atomic updates. Existing stored data needs no migration. [#168478](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168478). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The SQLite V2 store offers authority-bound commands, explicit preparation and operations that preserve absent storage. Memory Core adopts it for selected writes. [#168857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168857). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prepared session patches and authority-aware route updates give plugin authors guarded session mutations. Legacy paths remain supported until the next SDK major. [#168453](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168453). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Native harness integrations gain V2 binding lifecycle and asynchronous ownership hooks, with synchronous compatibility retained. [#168479](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168479). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Approval publication can be awaited while the synchronous publisher remains available for migration. [#168482](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168482). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - V2 conversation-binding services and adapters provide awaited binding operations. Bundled Discord, Feishu, iMessage, Slack and Telegram paths adopt them. [#168483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168483). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Plugins can prepare message-action descriptions asynchronously, including discovery for Matrix accounts with saved credentials. [#168484](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168484). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Approval and placement operations have migration paths for legacy synchronous placement methods and opaque approval callbacks. [#168855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168855). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) for the reused updater compatibility work.
  - Solid 2 views require the compiler and runtime and an explicit JSX import-source pragma. The X replies view uses the same plugin view contract. [#168408](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168408). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Plugin authoring, manifest and SDK migration guides use clearer language for existing behavior. [#168701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168701), [issue #168322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168322). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Set `fallbacksOverride` in `before_model_resolve` to replace a run's fallback chain. An empty list stops the run if its selected model fails, while omitting the override keeps configured fallbacks. [#168893](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168893), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@dwty11](https://github.com/dwty11) for the [report and clarification](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/118946).
  - Malformed fallback overrides are treated as an empty list, preventing a malformed value from causing a crash or unintended fallback. [#169702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169702), thanks [@Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx) and [@dwty11](https://github.com/dwty11) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/118946).
  - Use V2 GitHub publication request and personal-action capabilities and async lifecycle methods. Existing writer signatures and completion timing remain supported until the next SDK major. [#168954](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168954), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Replace retired synchronous GitHub publication coordinator calls with async or V2 methods. Personal OAuth compatibility methods have a separate contract. [#169581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169581), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Move synchronous placement readers to their async equivalents. These readers were deprecated, and the placement changes below describe their later compatibility limits. [#169086](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169086), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Await skill-command discovery for channel menus. The synchronous SDK list methods remain available during their deprecation period. [#169089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169089), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use async credential preparation and provider-selection APIs. Released synchronous methods retain their behavior through the next SDK major. [#169111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169111), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Await session metadata, conversation, model-menu and speech-preference preparation. Synchronous SDK calls remain available with warnings. [#169122](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169122), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use `loadCombinedSessionStoreForGatewayAsync` for cross-session visibility. The synchronous loader remains available through the next SDK major. [#169523](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169523), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Replace deprecated unbound native-session deletion callbacks with the documented factories. Session context consumers can finish with the snapshot they captured, even if the transcript changes afterward. [#169553](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169553), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Await transcript reads, statistics, catalog titles and input completion. Legacy deletion callbacks now run after the session commit, and failed companion cleanup may need a safe retry. [#169566](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169566), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session input completion avoids a false failure after an unrelated write. Plugin authors can migrate recorder callbacks to `completeProcessingAsync`. [#169573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169573), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Move Memory session metadata reads to `loadMemorySessionMetadataAsync` or `loadMemorySessionMetadataBatchAsync`. The synchronous forms are deprecated until the next SDK major. [#169579](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169579), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use async state APIs for plugin hosts, including snapshot publication, cron path resolution, transcript search views and web-search provider lists. Before async preparation, the synchronous TTS path resolver uses explicit config or environment settings, or the default path. [#169580](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169580), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Replace the synchronous session catalog title helper with `readSessionTranscriptCatalogTitleAsync`. [#169620](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169620), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Await replacement placement and Mention Inbox methods. Synchronous placement `get` and `getMany` work only for prepared facts, and some other older methods throw or return unavailable. Legacy Mention Inbox recording is queued, so use `recordCommittedInputAsync` when the caller must wait for completion. [#169626](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169626), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retained synchronous storage adapters can now emit migration warnings while Board and plugin metadata reads use async APIs. [#169631](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169631), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Await single or batched transcript statistics and use `onPreparedAgentRunStart` for prepared run-start callbacks. Applicable synchronous APIs remain during the current major. [#169637](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169637), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Move deprecated synchronous config, auth, model registry, ACP, session-link and Reef hooks to their async replacements. Legacy calls remain supported during the deprecation period and may warn. [#169714](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169714), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Add `forkSession` for questions against prepared session context, subject to session access, supported providers, and retained context. [#170146](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170146) by [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Allow authorized plugins to steer supported active runs with hidden, attributed guidance and an explicit next-turn fallback. [#170152](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170152) by [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Shows informational notices for observed deprecated plugin APIs and their replacements. [#171752](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171752), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Sets the SQLite SDK migration window while keeping existing calls available for now. It also preserves a future cron retry during startup recovery. [#171775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171775), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Adds awaited helpers for historical transcript-key lookup and TTS prompt preparation, with prepared preferences available for supported synchronous TTS calls. [#171443](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171443), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Lets admitted agent hooks and Gateway services share plugin-owned data for the same run. [#171307](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171307), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Lets plugin authors filter agent-event subscriptions by phase before unwanted events are copied. [#171534](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171534), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Adds an opt-in metadata-only event payload for short top-level scalar values. Full payloads remain the default. [#171610](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171610), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Aligns bundled plugins with current host APIs. Installing newer bundled plugins separately on older hosts is unsupported, and FaceTime declares a minimum host version of 2026.10.3. [#171633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171633), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore `resolvePath` and `inlineView` in registered callbacks, including ONNX verification with explicit model directories. [#184110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184110), thanks to [Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668).
  - Independently validate affected plugin settings when schema validation runs without acceleration. [#182514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182514), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject oversized bounded arrays and sets before element traversal in the patched Zod 3 consumer, including A2UI catalog schemas. Invalid-input diagnostics and refinements can differ. [#187304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187304), thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Reduce allocation and CPU work for adjacent transforms on plugin-backed model streams. [#185083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185083), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Document five async plugin SDK replacements and their narrow import paths while retaining existing synchronous contracts. [Commit 8fc31c9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8fc31c91811524b3bae3091a3861b5febd160151).
  - [Custom snooze reuse for one hour per Gateway and account, alongside session ID lookup, Gateway shutdown, plugin run-context access, and batched skill discovery changes](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/993c748b53ca3cd781167886061aa879d576a87b).
  - Retired synchronous GitHub publication methods now name their awaited or V2 replacement before rejecting calls in [#169750](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169750). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Supported ClawRouter deployments can show available pool capacity and model eligibility without exposing the API key to the browser.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - View pool headroom and model eligibility in the Control UI. The configured API key stays on the Gateway. [#169514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169514), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use the model-request credential for the ClawRouter pool dashboard. [#170041](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170041) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Board cards show more accurate status during refreshes, and board navigation remains usable on narrow screens.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Local Workboard commands make changes visible to the running Gateway. Offline dispatch does not start workers. [#168457](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168457). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Board widget HTML over the existing 10 MiB limit stops loading with a clear error. [#167260](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167260). Thanks [@hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - The Workboard guide explains the existing agent tool grants and the tools automatically granted to card workers. [#168222](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168222). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Workboard card status follows the overall task outcome through model fallback, avoiding false failures while work continues. Session-only links can settle on the next minute sweep. [#168215](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168215), [issue #167574](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167574). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223), [@asikorskiy](https://github.com/asikorskiy) for the report, and [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin) for the related proposal.
  - Leave unchanged widgets untouched during Board layout edits. [#168999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168999), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore later Workboard, Logbook and Team Reports operations after a storage failure once the underlying problem is resolved. An in-flight write may have an unknown outcome and is not replayed automatically. [#170138](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170138) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [Jeehut](https://github.com/Jeehut). See the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169981).
  - Load board summaries and session-linked cards in desktop chat instead of full card history. [#171135](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171135) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171078).
  - Prevent normal or forced promotion from reopening completed cards. [#171080](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171080) by [sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), with thanks to [SabaTech-dev](https://github.com/SabaTech-dev). See the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171048).
  - Reveal overflow-selected board tabs on narrow screens and support keyboard access to long widget menus. [#170767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170767) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Anchor Workboard details menus to their controls. [#170511](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170511) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid unnecessary database initialization for initial Board snapshot and widget reads. [#171245](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171245) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Completed Workboard cards stay completed after later partial failures, and manual done or blocked moves release their claims. Moving a card does not stop its running worker.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Refuse experimental Workboard PostgreSQL selection when existing SQLite application objects are present. [#170266](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170266) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The port includes disabled Workboard stores, offers a dry run and can resume or roll back an interrupted operation. Shared state and agent databases remain on SQLite. [#171368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171368), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restores review handoffs after dependencies finish. [#171414](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171414), thanks to [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@asikorskiy](https://github.com/asikorskiy) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171380), and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for verification.
  - Prevents promotion of cards outside `todo`, including when `force` is used. [#171743](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171743), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@SabaTech-dev](https://github.com/SabaTech-dev). See also [the promotion report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171048).
  - Preserves full run IDs and completion notifications, with a 512-character limit for new IDs. [#172235](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172235), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@gregbond](https://github.com/gregbond) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172203).
  - Load matching packaged Workboard assets from the Control UI origin when the Gateway is separate. Custom or mismatched plugins continue to use the Gateway asset route. [#172352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172352). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Show agents why a card claim was refused, including when a blocked card needs to be unblocked. [#172504](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172504). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reconcile cards linked to archived sessions, including multiple cards sharing a session or run. [#172505](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172505). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve completed cards and links after a later step in a multi-card operation fails. [#172615](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172615). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Release claims and settle active records on manual done or blocked moves. [#184241](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184241), thanks to [jondihaynes](https://github.com/jondihaynes) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep Workboard automation nudges on their intended cooldown through system clock changes. [#183356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183356), thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include session changes from nested operations in Boards transaction receipts. [Commit bfea15e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/bfea15edc0d66d880c5f58744817fd6297844768).
  - Workboard promotion accepts only todo cards. Telegram transcription receives group or direct-chat context, and project and session listings can proceed without unrelated runtime preparation. [Commit 50c9f67](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/50c9f67aabed13aca99524391e3aca2ef6e05a95).
  - Add experimental PostgreSQL-backed Workboard storage. Later changes add the supported port command and schema-migration infrastructure; PostgreSQL remains opt-in rather than a shared-state or agent-database replacement. [#169214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169214), [storage design](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169254). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Report experimental database engine selection in Doctor. Workboard is the only supported PostgreSQL store, and a selected PostgreSQL store does not silently fall back to SQLite. [#169933](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169933), [portability design #169254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169254). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Experimental PostgreSQL Workboard writes avoid a duplicate admission request, and PostgreSQL connection secrets must use an environment, file or exec reference in [#170037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170037). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - PostgreSQL backup, restore, and update protection, including a refusal from `backup sqlite` that directs PostgreSQL users to `backup create` ([#170828](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170828)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Meeting transcript retries restore all available pages, capture settings retain library filters and captions can continue after the joining CLI disconnects.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Failed automatic captures clean up unchanged empty candidates. The same change retains pending provider model choices and corrects authentication selection for explicit Gateway connections. [#137092](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137092). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Teams and Zoom meeting commands validate explicitly empty `--mode` and `--transport` values before connecting. [#167871](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167871). Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Restore every transcript page after an authorized retry from Summary or Transcript. [#169000](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169000), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Return to the selected meeting and library filters after opening capture settings. [#169279](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169279), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep periodic meeting captions running after the joining CLI disconnects. [#178415](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178415). Thanks [@sukhdeepjohar](https://github.com/sukhdeepjohar) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@sercada](https://github.com/sercada) for the related report.

  </details>

- Companion clients can discover and preview verified official ClawHub packages, with more useful command failures for operators testing integrations.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - QA command failures report their reason on stderr and exit with status 1. A failing `qa coverage --json` also reports on stderr. [#168802](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168802). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Accepts and preserves optional lowercase categories in Claw manifests. [#171532](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171532), thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
  - Supplies official ClawHub catalog discovery, package previews and exact-artifact verification for companion Gateway clients. [#171622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171622), thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
  - Returns a parseable JSON failure for two invalid cleanup option combinations in the experimental `claws remove --json` command. [#172281](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172281), thanks to [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668).
  - Retains shutdown diagnostics and recovery information for inspection when a QA desktop warmup is cancelled. [#171482](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171482), thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- Covered tool and file actions reject more malformed or unauthorized requests. If a plugin changes an approved call’s arguments, the changed arguments need approval too.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Separate approval for plugin-rewritten arguments on the trusted inline path, [#142700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142700) and [reported issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/152465). Thanks to [giodl73-repo](https://github.com/giodl73-repo) and [giodl73](https://github.com/giodl73).
  - Bounded thinking allowance for exec approval reviewers, [#150200](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150200). Thanks to [leapdragon](https://github.com/leapdragon) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve valid union values during argument repair, [#158974](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158974). Thanks to [Shenrui-Ma](https://github.com/Shenrui-Ma) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep tools available when literal schema examples or defaults contain dynamic-reference keys, [#160542](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160542). Thanks to [Shenrui-Ma](https://github.com/Shenrui-Ma).
  - Keep deeply nested tool schemas callable with a 128-level validation boundary, [#164836](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164836), with reports [#141306](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141306), [#153700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153700), [#153701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153701), [#153713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153713) and [#153743](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153743). Thanks to [RXQ6](https://github.com/RXQ6), [qxr666-sga](https://github.com/qxr666-sga) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject overlapping agent edit and Skill Workshop patch matches, [#168125](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168125). Thanks to [yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Generic agent node invocations cannot bypass a denied browser tool through browser proxy commands. [#179880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179880). Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - Unsafe `test` and `[` predicates no longer pass automatically as safe builtins in POSIX exec allowlist mode. [#180880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180880). Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - Reject malformed UTF-8 secrets plans before applying them, [#167379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167379). Thanks to [Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prevent redundant preparation of existing stores from revoking an active tool caller's authority in [#170658](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170658). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Agent `--message-file` paths preserve leading and trailing spaces, preventing a neighboring file from being read instead. For `agent exec`, only the exact value `-` selects stdin. [PR #171585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171585). Thanks [Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Browser fill rejects pipes and other special fields files instead of potentially hanging. Regular files and symlinks to them remain supported. [#183420](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183420). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Completion installation and profile inspection reject pipes and other special-file profiles instead of hanging. [#181763](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181763). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the transcripts tool registered across caller-less turns while enforcing authorization ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d499b183f2938c5ad3b3f6f0c79402863ee695c5)).
  - Multi-file `apply_patch` checks predictable update-hunk failures before writing. [PR #160409](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160409), [issues #117742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/117742) and [#127496](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127496). Thanks to [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), with reports from [yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Denied filesystem glob checks in the experimental opt-in Codex sandbox avoid stalling the Gateway on crafted patterns. [#167296](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167296), [#166684](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166684). Thanks to [xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee) and [Takhoffman](https://github.com/Takhoffman).
  - When workspace-only file access is disabled, accept active session workspace images in Codex steering and reject sibling or shared workspace images in the covered cases. [#181528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181528) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Recheck a plain host assertion before embedded prompt submission when the host may have become inactive in [1fe1e63](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1fe1e63fe00d3cdb8da09920146cf273b8b4e2f8).
  - The runtime runs on the Gateway host and refuses policies it cannot enforce. [#170487](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170487), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Set `plugins.entries.agentsapi.config.forwarding.gatewayTools` or `forwarding.mcpServers` to `false` to disable the corresponding forwarding path. [#170728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170728), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Code Mode can read bounded browser observations and gives clearer failures for affected syntax, memory and wait errors. A failed wait does not undo actions already dispatched.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Wrapped Browser observations and snapshot references in Code Mode, [#164833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164833) and [#164641](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164641). Thanks to [alkor2000](https://github.com/alkor2000) and reporter [potterdigital](https://github.com/potterdigital).
  - Recovery guidance for invalid Code Mode waits, [#128886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128886), [#128859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128859) and [#167610](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167610). Thanks to [sashankh](https://github.com/sashankh), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc), [shad0wca7](https://github.com/shad0wca7), [potterdigital](https://github.com/potterdigital) and [benprotzekmuntoni](https://github.com/benprotzekmuntoni).

Thanks to [sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313), [pennymolt](https://github.com/pennymolt) and [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Reuse prepared catalog data across repeated Node Code Mode calls in [#169045](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169045). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Release closed-database metadata that could exhaust Code Mode worker memory in [#169956](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169956). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep control tool declarations stable across catalog changes in [#170282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170282), addressing [#170233](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170233). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@imadal1n](https://github.com/imadal1n).
  - Give invalid shell commands and Node worker memory exhaustion more useful errors in [#170947](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170947). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Bounded Node output capture and recovery from sustained GC pressure or heap exhaustion, with partial output retained within the configured limit. [PR #172134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172134). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retirement of workers making frequent major collections near their heap limit, including when individual collections are cheap. [PR #172552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172552). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Per-worker GC and heap metrics use up to 64 reusable worker slots, with script-level attribution for overflow. [PR #172486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172486). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Node Code Mode leaves collection timing to V8 instead of forcing idle inspector collections between or during cells. [PR #172284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172284). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Healthy Node workers can be reused after settled validation, guest, and bridge failures without retaining failed-cell globals. [PR #172305](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172305). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Pending Code Mode exec and wait calls remain visible to session recovery after nested calls finish. Normal deadlines and recovery limits still apply. [PR #171570](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171570), [issue #171366](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171366). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini).
  - Code Mode guidance distinguishes thrown errors from returned failures and advises checking effects before retrying. [PR #171815](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171815). Thanks [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Truncated syntax feedback preserves Unicode characters at the clipping boundary. [PR #176230](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176230). Thanks [hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Suspended Code Mode runs expire according to elapsed time even when the system clock changes. [#180599](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180599). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Concurrent large shell analyses release parser memory sooner. [#183312](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183312). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Missing-subcommand JSON errors ([#184128](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184128)). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668).
  - Preserve cleanup uncertainty in buffered command results ([#187271](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187271), backport of [#184270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184270), addressing [#184237](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/184237)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).

  </details>

- Computer control used by an MCP agent is released when its owning run ends; recording and transfer restrictions remain in place.

- Web fetch preserves more exact numbers, code blocks and document boundaries while excluding additional recognized hidden page content.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">

  - Decode UTF-8 pages and feeds with false UTF-16 metadata when no byte-order mark or HTTP charset overrides it, [#168115](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168115). Thanks to [yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  - Readability text extraction keeps headings, paragraphs, and list items separated on affected pages.
