Thanks [@HeZ2z](https://github.com/HeZ2z) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for reporting the issue.
  - Basic HTML extraction separates definition-list terms and values in text and Markdown. [#180557](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180557). Thanks [@AmelioMansour](https://github.com/AmelioMansour) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fetched text keeps readable link labels without fragments of unusual link destinations. [#180605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180605). Thanks [@AmelioMansour](https://github.com/AmelioMansour) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - HTML extraction preserves boundaries around more blocks, including quotes, captions, and navigation. [#181740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181740), related [issue #176244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176244). Thanks [@l4xb](https://github.com/l4xb) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for the related report.
  - Web fetch excludes text hidden by recognized zero-X or zero-Y inline scale transforms. [#181785](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181785), [issue #105438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/105438). Thanks [@Jimmy-xuzimo](https://github.com/Jimmy-xuzimo) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80) for reporting the issue.

  </details>

- Web search respects more configured provider choices, limits and timeouts and returns clearer snippets and failures.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserve managed search timeout diagnostics in [#170372](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170372), addressing [#169972](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169972). Thanks [@xiajiahang](https://github.com/xiajiahang), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@letsc4](https://github.com/letsc4). See the [web tools guide](/tools/web).
  - Configured search provider names validate regardless of letter case. [#177836](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177836). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Long early search snippets no longer crowd out later result titles as readily. [#179703](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179703). Thanks [@canvinus](https://github.com/canvinus) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Explicitly enabled search plugins participate in automatic provider selection when search is enabled, no provider is pinned, and credentials are available. [#179781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179781), [issue #179665](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/179665). Thanks [@wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [@duardor968](https://github.com/duardor968) for reporting the issue.
  - Tavily, Firecrawl, Firecrawl-free, and DuckDuckGo respect configured search result limits and timeouts. An explicit result count takes precedence. [#180115](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180115), [issue #180062](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180062). Thanks [@mushuiyu886](https://github.com/mushuiyu886) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for reporting the issue.
  - Invalid Exa and Parallel search endpoints no longer expose their configured values in errors shown to agents. [#182534](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182534). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Beam isolates unavailable mirror credentials and picks up restored credentials after a secrets reload. [#176510](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176510). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Makes policy-approved web search and fetch directly available to native local Ollama runs when Tool Search is unset. A search provider still needs to be configured. [#169556](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169556), [issue #145137](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/145137). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@lator57-gif](https://github.com/lator57-gif).
  - Kimi search uses the replacement endpoint and supports a result count from one to ten. [#171835](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171835), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [Glukmann](https://github.com/Glukmann) for [reporting the issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169072).

  </details>

- Affected turns keep the intended request and available tools after discovery or interruption, instead of receiving stale input.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Restore bounded interrupted-request context for built-in and authorized CLI turns, [#168279](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168279). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Guide agents to focus on the current request unless asked to continue the earlier one, [#168906](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168906). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [alter](https://github.com/alter) for related investigation.
  - `/btw`, `/side`, and `/loop` preserve multiline questions and recurring prompts, including indentation. [#176293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176293), [issue #176250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176250). Thanks [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for reporting the issue.
  - Changed MCP tool names start a fresh native CLI session so its tool catalog can refresh while the OpenClaw chat remains. History recovery follows the existing account boundary. [#176577](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176577), [issue #175670](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/175670). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@jackylecalvez2](https://github.com/jackylecalvez2) for reporting the issue.
  - Agent guidance omits reasoning and status command hints when the channel has no enabled command route. [#181997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181997), [issue #181956](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181956). Thanks [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@Card4Philippe](https://github.com/Card4Philippe).
  - Agent guidance no longer recommends disabled chat update and restart commands. [#182001](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182001), related [issue #181956](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181956). Thanks [@MonkeyLeeT](https://github.com/MonkeyLeeT) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@Card4Philippe](https://github.com/Card4Philippe) for reporting the issue.
  - Retain delivered slash commands and replies in history with sensitive recorded values redacted in [#169026](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169026). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve the order of captions and images in OpenAI Responses tool results in [#169119](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169119). Thanks [@Shenrui-Ma](https://github.com/Shenrui-Ma) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Send deferred Tool Search results to the model without duplicating structured data in [#169537](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169537), addressing [#169492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169492). Thanks [@TUARAN](https://github.com/TUARAN), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [@kseczhouyb](https://github.com/kseczhouyb).
  - Use `dispatch_action` consistently for generic Tool Search in [#169552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169552), related to [#145137](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/145137). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@lator57-gif](https://github.com/lator57-gif).
  - Tell a resumed Claude CLI session about missed turns after switching models, with a session-history hint when that tool is available. The notice does not itself include the missed messages. [#168726](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168726). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Supply earlier-message metadata and policy-permitted history lookup after an account switch, without automatically replaying the transcript or durable context ([#168781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168781)). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Downloads are easier to find in chat and Files, and file-detail disclosures keep their expanded state across tab switches.

  - Visible document downloads in the chat transcript and Files panel. [PR #171171](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171171), [issue #171098](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171098). Thanks [LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [arquiteto-raiz](https://github.com/arquiteto-raiz).

- Supported settings and provider setup can run while the local Gateway stays running. First-time setup and unsupported writes still need it stopped; a saved change is not necessarily active until its required reload or restart.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Local Gateway ownership for provider auth and MCP credentials, probes and saved-server changes, [#168135](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168135). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Route basic agent creation through the running Gateway and protect direct state writes from conflicting owners. Later provider, configuration and baseline-setup changes add their supported live routes. [#168417](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168417). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Debug proxy captures follow the active Gateway or exclusive offline owner, [#168459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168459). Update an older Gateway or stop it before offline capture. Run the Gateway when proxy child commands change other OpenClaw state. Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The running Gateway commits supported CLI changes and follows its reload policy. An updated CLI talking to a still-running v2026.10.7 Gateway may report a failure after saving a reload-off change, so check the configuration before retrying. [#182951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182951). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Allow affected `session.store` configuration writes ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/943da4fc9366e2acc1710542897b4e229064c6c1)).
  - Apply agent creation and updates while another agent is being deleted, without requiring another save or restart. [#171459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171459), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Route CLI secret changes through the running local Gateway, or use exclusive offline ownership when it is stopped. An unreachable, replaced, or older owner causes the write to be refused. Manual model catalog refresh requires the Gateway to be stopped. [#168108](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168108). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject malformed UTF-8 piped into model token and API-key commands before it can overwrite saved credentials. [#167251](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167251). Thanks to [Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Disable external HTTP hooks at startup when their secret cannot be resolved, while letting the Gateway start. A rejected reload keeps the previous valid hook settings. Supply the secret to the Gateway service and reload or restart, or disable the hooks. [Hook configuration](/gateway/config-hooks), [#165019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165019). Thanks to [eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - Show or hide a secret without submitting the prompt, while keeping the typed value. [#169925](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169925). Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
  - Config and root-level private writes preserve existing shared state-root permissions while keeping sensitive files and private child directories private. [#171869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171869), following the report in [#169011](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169011). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Config saves involving secret references can acknowledge a change already applied by the watcher instead of waiting for a timeout. [#171922](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171922) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), following work in [#109792](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/109792) and [#169249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169249).
  - Preserve provider setup results and activation guidance when temporary credential cleanup fails. [#172379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172379), reported in [#172368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172368). Thanks to [@skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@giannis88](https://github.com/giannis88).
  - CLI configuration writes with reload off save without directly restarting an updated Gateway. Older Gateways may still restart. [#184392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184392) Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).
  - CLI writes accepted for a Gateway restart report success and a restart message. Acceptance does not mean the configuration is already active. [#184532](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184532) Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).

  </details>

- Terminal commands preserve more exact path and argument values and provide improved completion suggestions. Regenerate installed Bash or PowerShell completion scripts for their fixes.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Complete quoted PowerShell option choices, [#167421](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167421). Thanks to [LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi).
  - Reset the log cursor when the selected file path changes, [#150791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150791) and [#150843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/150843). Thanks to [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve Unicode joiners in exec and background process output, [#160403](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160403). Thanks to [Shenrui-Ma](https://github.com/Shenrui-Ma).
  - Avoid cold compile-cache writes delaying the exit of one-shot CLI commands in [#170684](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170684). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh validated shell snapshots can be reused without another validation shell, and process diagnostics identify more helper launches. [PR #172310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172310). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Shell completion suggests accepted global flags after subcommands. Regenerate cached completion scripts with `openclaw completion --write-state` to pick up the suggestions. [PR #171571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171571). Thanks [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Trajectory exports accept safe directory names beginning with two dots, such as `..diagnostics`. [PR #171609](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171609). Thanks [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) and [altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - Gateway SSH probes honor an explicitly specified port 22 even when the SSH alias specifies another port. [PR #171092](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171092), related [issue #127456](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127456). Thanks [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Bash completion keeps suggestions within the option value being entered. [#183778](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183778). Thanks [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi).
  - Preserve writer identity through CLI reply completion ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/97e9b91f1f9413e32d31a8170dbafc8c9cfd7f3e)).
  - Separately installed ACPX can find the host bridges for enabled plugin and OpenClaw tools. The bridges remain opt-in. [#164272](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164272), [#164211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164211). Thanks to [jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [ajmtrz](https://github.com/ajmtrz).

  </details>

- Affected terminal reports display untrusted names and paths without letting control characters distort the output. JSON and explicitly requested raw values can still preserve those characters.

- You can steer an active Claude CLI task at its next scheduling boundary without interrupting a long-running tool. Other coding runtimes keep their own supported steering behavior.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Text steering for active Claude CLI turns, with accepted messages recorded in the session and incompatible or unconsumed input following the existing follow-up path. [PR #159816](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159816), [issue #124731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/124731), [related issue #156848](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156848). Thanks to [kiranvk-2011](https://github.com/kiranvk-2011), [Shagrat2](https://github.com/Shagrat2), and [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Apply saved per-session MCP server selections on subsequent Claude CLI turns, including warm processes. [#168393](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168393), [issue #168207](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168207), [MCP guide](/tools/mcp). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@dprev](https://github.com/dprev) for the report.
  - Retain the originating turn's permitted native and OpenClaw tools after a background command completes, subject to current session restrictions. [#168670](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168670), [issue #168342](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168342), [CLI backends guide](/gateway/cli-backends). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for the report.
  - Avoid a duplicate question when switching away from Claude CLI and back ([#168965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168965)). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve managed on-request approval settings for plugin turns and side questions so those requests can start under the configured policy. [#172639](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172639) Thanks to [@stevenlee-oai](https://github.com/stevenlee-oai).
  - A compatible Codex context refresh can continue a conversation without stopping a running preview or background command. [#177716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177716) Thanks [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen).
  - Unblock Codex native-session steering during transcript appends in [ca3798a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ca3798a38171b430162daa793425c7dd37111568).
  - Preserve multiline ACP steering in [#185456](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185456), fixing [#185455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/185455). Thanks to [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Affected coding conversations retain usable history and follow-ups through more restarts and compactions without moving replies to the wrong thread.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserve subagent replay context through tool hooks ([#185170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185170), closing [#185127](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/185127)). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - After manual Claude CLI compaction, the next turn starts a fresh process with the compacted history. [PR #157766](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157766), [issue #157483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157483). Thanks to [deepujain](https://github.com/deepujain) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), with the report from [von8794](https://github.com/von8794) and related evidence from [goslingmanagment](https://github.com/goslingmanagment).
  - Claude CLI history deduplicates matching forwarded and owner messages while preserving sender attribution. [#163521](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163521). Thanks to [rogeriochaves](https://github.com/rogeriochaves), [llmsommelier](https://github.com/llmsommelier) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Claude CLI history hides recognized generated runtime prompts and keeps oversized tool results classified as tool activity. [#166080](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166080), [#165041](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/165041). Thanks to [Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [IndySweeney](https://github.com/IndySweeney), [agierror](https://github.com/agierror) and [Heighte](https://github.com/Heighte).
  - After a Gateway restart, the first prompt can resume an initialized one-shot ACP conversation when the agent supports loading or resuming sessions. [#166368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166368), [#124852](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/124852). Thanks to [alkor2000](https://github.com/alkor2000), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [jhgaylor](https://github.com/jhgaylor).
  - Last-completed session forks respect a newer reset boundary instead of replaying cleared context. [#166660](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166660). Thanks to [Shenrui-Ma](https://github.com/Shenrui-Ma) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Long Codex sessions avoid an extra OpenClaw compaction request after native-only compaction when the host transcript is unchanged. [#168051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168051), [#168015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168015). Thanks to [ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [robertoronderosjr](https://github.com/robertoronderosjr).
  - Remove recognized historical requester guidance and duplicate imported turns from displayed chat history ([#168943](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168943)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Clarify display-only history cleanup and preserved transcripts in the [Claude CLI guide](/gateway/cli-backends) ([#168851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168851)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Keep session-specific runtime facts in private turn context so ordinary local Claude CLI conversations can reuse the same system prompt ([#168928](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168928)). Thanks [@alkor2000](https://github.com/alkor2000), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [@Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx). Thanks [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for the report.
  - Wait for background cleanup before a cold resume without taking over a thread still owned by running work. [#188170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188170), adapting [#187776](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187776). Thanks [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Exact-ID lookup can find, read and continue a Claude session beyond the ordinary discovery limit, which still applies to browsing. [#176742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176742), [#176659](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176659) Thanks [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@ceo633](https://github.com/ceo633).
  - Claude CLI forks whose saved checkpoint was removed by compaction can use existing fresh-session recovery when authorized history and retry budget are available. [#181308](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181308), [#181254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181254) Thanks [@mushuiyu886](https://github.com/mushuiyu886) and [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored).
  - Preserve transcript continuity across automatic idle and daily agent resets for conversation recall and memory indexing. [#184157](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184157) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@namest504](https://github.com/namest504).
  - Preserve the request prefix across thinking-level changes in supported native Codex sessions. [#182397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182397) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Reject durable context reads when the current input has been removed or rewritten in [777e85c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/777e85c9eece4e6dddb33c9e7ed18389a8e52ef3).
  - Resume stalled turns from saved input after worker loss ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4a73e919157bd9420376f6e198945b6a00c60405)).
  - Avoid repeating saved continuation instructions on compaction retries ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d49faed633709262226f571dd3d4d1b9c2484d7d)).
  - Require a live context relay for protected Codex continuations ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/368809d328482716baf1da66f8e589e5626697ee)). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

- Codex, Claude CLI and other supported coding-agent setups find their available models and tools more consistently and explain missing account access.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Explain how to enable Codex on an unavailable paired device or select an OpenClaw-harness model ([#168641](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168641)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Honor `lightContext` for CLI-backed subagents by omitting workspace bootstrap files when requested in [#169848](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169848), addressing [#169704](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169704). Thanks [@xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
  - ACP setup and the bundled router expose the existing `grok-build` agent target for users with the Grok CLI installed and authenticated. [PR #99193](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/99193), [related issue #99191](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/99191). Thanks to [TheAngryPit](https://github.com/TheAngryPit).
  - Claude CLI receives guidance for registered OpenClaw tool names and deferred discovery, including message delivery when the tool is available. [PR #153032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153032), [related issues #157321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157321), [#139599](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139599), and [#147006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/147006). Thanks to [kiranvk-2011](https://github.com/kiranvk-2011) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), with related evidence from [lakemike](https://github.com/lakemike) and reports from [philknows](https://github.com/philknows), [destinedenergy](https://github.com/destinedenergy), and [wbumgardner-max](https://github.com/wbumgardner-max).
  - Select a qualifying installed Codex for managed turns and model discovery ([#168228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168228)). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Discover native models on demand and retire unowned clients after 30 idle seconds, while keeping clients with active or retained work. [#168387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168387). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retire unused Codex processes after model discovery times out while allowing active work to finish. Future requests get a replacement client. [#168365](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168365), [issue #168159](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168159), [recovery guide](/plugins/codex-harness-runtime/recovery). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT) for the report and verification.
  - Use the ChatGPT account route for affected isolated and utility Codex completions when available, without requiring an OpenAI API key. Explicit profiles and utility-model choices remain authoritative. [#168552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168552). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Use the account's native Claude Code model menu and effort levels for CLI selections, while retaining existing configured choices. [#168728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168728). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep automatic titles, conversation labels and Activity recaps on Haiku through Claude CLI when Opus or Sonnet is the primary CLI model, unless an explicit or compatible runtime choice takes precedence. [#168603](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168603). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Check native session forks against their selected runtime's capacity, using 100,000 tokens when capacity is unknown. [PR #169792](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169792). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Preserve literal underscores in imported agent names and avatar paths. [#171581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171581), thanks to [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) for the fix and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for the follow-up.
  - Let healthy ACP-only session stores finish slow startup preparation and become available without a restart. [#171447](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171447), thanks to [ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [potterdigital](https://github.com/potterdigital). See the [reported issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171395).
  - Restored native Claude spawns after sign-in and distinguished inconclusive checks from confirmed logout. [#172471](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172471). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Avoided a false cleanup failure after Claude CLI stdin has already closed. [#171969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171969). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Prevented late Codex desktop watcher notifications from scheduling a restart during shutdown. [#172049](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172049). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep native session access waits within their intended elapsed-time budget when the system clock changes. [#172780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172780) Thanks to [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Codex Computer Use marketplace discovery keeps its elapsed-time allowance when the system clock changes during installation. [#178986](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178986) Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Explicitly enabling ACP keeps the bundled acpx backend available at startup under the existing plugin policy. [#176876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176876), [#102205](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/102205), [#102221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/102221) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@ZOOWH](https://github.com/ZOOWH).
  - ACP readiness no longer requires an unused default workspace when agents have explicit workspaces. [#176879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176879) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Failed Claude CLI skills plugin preparation attempts to remove its temporary files while preserving the original error. [#179611](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179611), [#179557](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/179557) Thanks [@xiaopan-xydt](https://github.com/xiaopan-xydt) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve the remaining attempt budget when a core upgrade retains an older Codex runtime plugin. [#182995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182995) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@kip-claw](https://github.com/kip-claw), [@cjn119-ui](https://github.com/cjn119-ui), [@fray-ai](https://github.com/fray-ai) and [@sukhdeepjohar](https://github.com/sukhdeepjohar).
  - Allow ChatGPT subscription relay preparation to use the managed gateway-only proxy's default loopback settings. [#183232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183232) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@indra622](https://github.com/indra622) and [@ermaxinc](https://github.com/ermaxinc).
  - Keep configured native Codex tool declarations stable across owner and completion turns, reducing a source of session resets while retaining owner checks for execution. [#183286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183286) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Supported long conversations compact earlier or recover from more context-limit failures. Summaries can still miss details, and an oversized prompt may need shortening.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - A best-effort summary lets safeguard compaction continue after exhausted quality checks, with a warning when details may be missing. [PR #130393](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130393), [issue #120162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/120162). Thanks to [amittell](https://github.com/amittell), [alexm](https://github.com/alexm), and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Compaction retries receive whole missing identifiers where they fit, along with a count of omitted values. [PR #138415](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138415). Thanks to [amittell](https://github.com/amittell) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Dense CJK-heavy summaries can trim optional prose while retaining required requests and identifiers when those facts fit. [PR #138416](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138416), [related issue #119272](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/119272). Thanks to [amittell](https://github.com/amittell) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Requests assembled by compaction-owning context engines are checked for overflow before provider submission and routed to recovery when needed. [PR #139999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139999), [issue #139961](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139961). Thanks to [Phillis](https://github.com/Phillis) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The immediate estimate after OpenAI server compaction includes the foreground system prompt and tool costs. [PR #141341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141341), [issue #141340](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141340). Thanks to [ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Automatically capped long-context Chat Completions turns can compact and continue after exhausting their output budget without exposing an incomplete candidate or replaying completed tools. Buffering can delay the live stream. [PR #143840](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143840). Thanks to [forrystudio](https://github.com/forrystudio) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Successful native compaction skips complete plugin hooks, and the bundled notifier reports when nothing was compacted. [PR #150920](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150920), [related issue #81925](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/81925). Thanks to [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [vishwanathmuthuraman](https://github.com/vishwanathmuthuraman), and [castigiova](https://github.com/castigiova).
  - Hugging Face TGI input context-limit errors can trigger compaction and retry. [PR #158161](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158161), [related issue #158159](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/158159). Thanks to [amittell](https://github.com/amittell) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Native Ollama subagent spawns can reuse more of a stable prompt prefix. Results depend on the model template, cache slots and unchanged instructions. [#164804](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164804), [#166271](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166271). Thanks to [aaa2015](https://github.com/aaa2015), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [xyrolle](https://github.com/xyrolle).
  - Prompt-cache warnings distinguish Responses continuation input from history changes and identify changed fields and parameters without exposing their values. [#168019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168019). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Claude conversation checkpoints preserve opportunities for reuse after large documents, images and tool exchanges. Bedrock checkpoint eligibility and lifetimes follow model and profile support. [#168042](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168042). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use provider compaction on eligible direct OpenAI Responses and Anthropic API-key routes, with existing opt-outs ([#168164](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168164)). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Correct cache usage and cost estimates, provider routing hints, Gemini credential scoping and Bedrock checkpoint eligibility ([#168145](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168145)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Advance byte-limited history in steadier steps for local-model prompt reuse. Some older context may leave the model's view sooner ([#168157](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168157), [#159799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159799)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), reporter [@8bitsforge](https://github.com/8bitsforge) and [@axeg0](https://github.com/axeg0) for the additional workload.

Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@arthur-drozdov](https://github.com/arthur-drozdov) and [@rsdancey](https://github.com/rsdancey).
  - Avoid unnecessary recovery during fitting tool-heavy turns when the optional mid-turn compaction check is enabled ([#168338](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168338), [#101929](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/101929), [#168364](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168364)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), reporters [@sercada](https://github.com/sercada) and [@KarimVKarmali](https://github.com/KarimVKarmali), and [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) for investigating.
  - Show recovery guidance when a prompt exceeds the model's context budget. A single oversized prompt still needs to be shortened ([#168326](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168326), [#158159](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/158159)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@amittell](https://github.com/amittell).

[#168401](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168401), [issue #168088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168088). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Preserve bounded tool results across replay, stable browser cache keys during private subagent completion, and an Anthropic cache prefix that excludes temporary context. [#168392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168392), [prompt caching guide](/reference/prompt-caching). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Read opted-in `AGENTS.md` sections from the effective agent workspace during automatic and explicit safeguard compaction, with warnings when files or selected headings are unavailable. [#168446](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168446), [issue #168131](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168131), [compaction configuration](/gateway/config-agents/heartbeat-compaction-and-streaming). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@jakeaadams](https://github.com/jakeaadams) for the report and advice.
  - Prevent session-store admission stack overflows in large agent fleets and preserve the original operation error. [#168354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168354), [issue #168160](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168160). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT) for the report and verification.
  - Compact before a managed OpenAI-compatible tool request is sent with too little output room ([#169085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169085)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks [@eightHundreds](https://github.com/eightHundreds) for the report.
  - Recognize the older LM Studio context-overflow error and attempt the existing compaction and retry flow ([#169189](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169189)). Thanks [@xpeng5278-web](https://github.com/xpeng5278-web) for the report and fix, and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the follow-up.
  - Preserve committed user metadata across later requests and improve cache-drop explanations after telemetry gaps ([#168848](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168848)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep reusable prompt prefixes steadier across reply modes and preserve saved custom-context timestamps on replay ([#169091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169091)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retain image and retry history more consistently for replay and provider prompt-cache reuse ([#169237](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169237)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep subagent and cross-session message provenance consistent from first dispatch through replay ([#169238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169238), [related issue #164515](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164515)). For these turns, model-prompt enrichment that omits the complete stored message is omitted. See [prompt caching](/reference/prompt-caching). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Thanks [@TourAround](https://github.com/TourAround) for the report.
  - Keep large conversations moving when their session commit receipt exceeds the transport limit. [PR #169843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169843), [issue #169780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169780). Thanks to [TheAngryPit](https://github.com/TheAngryPit) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Start native compaction earlier for ChatGPT sign-in sessions, leaving more room for a large next message or tool result. [PR #169755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169755). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Continue eligible direct OpenAI API-key sessions with the provider's retained messages after compaction and restart. Invalid or oversized windows still use the existing fallback. [PR #169771](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169771). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Wait for the previous writer before checking a transcript for manual compaction, so its final committed message can be included. [PR #169931](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169931). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid repeating runtime context after every tool result in an OpenAI Responses turn. Existing duplicates remain until compaction. [PR #169632](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169632). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep encrypted reasoning replay consistent before and after transcript reload for OpenAI Responses SDK continuations. [PR #169689](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169689). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Allow Responses turns to reuse provider connections after a reply finishes. [PR #169802](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169802). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep WebChat tool definitions and retained Responses replay IDs stable across turns and history pruning to support prompt-cache reuse. [PR #170050](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170050). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Read-only forks retain completed parent answers and tool results. [#170973](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170973). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prepared fork expiry no longer retains completed agent turns in memory unnecessarily. [#171012](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171012), following separate retention work in [#170855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170855). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Omit-mode model history can preserve earlier goals by replacing selected tool-result bodies with notices. [#171239](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171239). Thanks [@YuriyPopovDev](https://github.com/YuriyPopovDev) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Rewrites of large tool-heavy histories reuse unchanged content to reduce main-thread copying. [#171200](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171200). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Manual transcript truncation completes index rebuilding before success. [#171134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171134), related to [#171085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171085). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@dustytext-bot](https://github.com/dustytext-bot).
  - Native Codex Ultrafast turns cap automatic compaction at 260,000 total context tokens while retaining lower configured limits. Incognito turns with incompatible creation settings are refused while their history is preserved. [#170950](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170950), extending [#170844](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170844). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor recognizes equivalent legacy attachments during imported session verification while continuing to reject real differences. [#171388](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171388), reported in [#171312](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171312) and related to [#171373](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171373). Thanks [@he-yufeng](https://github.com/he-yufeng), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@brookpw](https://github.com/brookpw), and [@junnie-stack](https://github.com/junnie-stack).
  - Continued a busy run after a follow-up triggers the optional mid-turn compaction precheck. [#172019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172019). Thanks [@mirasrael](https://github.com/mirasrael) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Fixed manual compaction for a small configured session limit on a model with a larger native window. [#172457](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172457), following the [summarization capacity check](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167916). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserved pending context-engine turn order through the agent database migration and maintenance. [#172074](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172074). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduced history copying during long-session transcript appends. [#172125](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172125). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retained an active oversized session to reduce repeated reloads and contention with other updates. [#172454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172454). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Load the full transcript for post-compaction maintenance only when a rewrite needs it. [#172564](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172564) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Share encoded model request bytes with session fork replay to reduce duplicate retained request storage. [#172565](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172565) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show the final automatic compaction failure and relevant recovery guidance instead of an earlier overflow warning. [#173310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173310) Thanks to [@TurboTheTurtle](https://github.com/TurboTheTurtle).
  - Keep idle Codex warm-session retention tied to elapsed time when the system clock changes ([#168771](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168771)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Lets ordinary default-model and lowercase model checks start without loading an unused catalog. [#169354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169354), [issue #88201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/88201). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@adamdksaad-ops](https://github.com/adamdksaad-ops).
  - Reuse prepared model lists across equivalent sessions. [PR #169653](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169653). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Model catalog reads avoid unnecessary request-attribution work. [#170912](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170912). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Renew unknown native model readiness before selection in [7edce1f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7edce1fbe322183a66ef4bce4cca218029069de4).
  - Reuse suitable model catalog data for covered cron and idle selection paths in [dd4b258](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/dd4b258a3cfa6fdef41535e5518fe260f5665a9c).
  - Keep compaction's safety deadline stable across clock changes in [#184293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184293), while retaining the SDK's epoch-valued deadline interface. Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve timeout and compaction recovery when a transcript append races finalization in [af82e00](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/af82e00296de8698a147bbbf52786cd3cdd2c61d).
  - Continue from provider compaction checkpoints without a misleading truncated-tool notice in [591aa77](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/591aa77c93c8591d0666ce1867b3671f1e5c57af).
  - Avoid unverifiable reasoning in affected ChatGPT tool-result continuations in [#185774](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185774). Thanks to [anyech](https://github.com/anyech), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [nikosml](https://github.com/nikosml).
  - Preserve compatible session authentication profiles during Codex and voice model selection ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f183f3867156a7fadc36af5e56983378de14fc80)).
  - Honor Qwen reasoning overrides supplied through payload hooks ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/fad1c5fa15b5d9d31a050646bdbd27beefa4a5a6)). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Keep text and images in their original order in OpenAI Responses tool results ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7b7f9ab9802d69db85ad1403b6c75cb1164aa080)).
  - Retain authorized trace diagnostics after native reply streaming ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/81e7a2190441faf0ae5afc9d585151c7d00a68fc)).
  - The session-control documentation clarifies the successful response to a Stop request after the selected run has already finished ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ae76b06c149232245f1002c34f7d8137bca75461)).
  - An integration merge includes Feishu's final-text fallback when a streaming card cannot be updated, Workboard card and worker-context reads, reduced memory and session database reads, and dialog lifecycle events ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/25e007273c2d4bfa71af01e871e8ce1a54fcb95d)).

  </details>

- Affected model streams retain readable answers without duplicate fragments or exposed tool-call markup.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Prevent duplicated opening text in affected OpenAI Responses and managed Completions streams. [#172653](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172653) Thanks to [@Richard355168](https://github.com/Richard355168) for the fix and [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/136262), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for investigation and verification, and [@Gavin-ifjc](https://github.com/Gavin-ifjc) and [@Lamer217](https://github.com/Lamer217) for related reports.
  - Allow bounded recovery from the recognized malformed OpenAI stream event using recorded tool results instead of repeating completed actions. [#173404](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173404) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve valid multiline Google Interactions stream events and report malformed event JSON instead of silently dropping content. [#173612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173612) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Enabled live previews receive answer text after reasoning from OpenAI-compatible Chat Completions streams. [PR #150685](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150685), [issue #132615](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/132615). Thanks to [leapdragon](https://github.com/leapdragon) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Hide recognized DeepSeek-format tool markup from replies and recover valid tool calls in managed turns. Direct calls and tool-free finalization filter the markup without executing it. [PR #169728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169728), [issue #159665](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159665). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [TOMUIV](https://github.com/TOMUIV).
  - Keep reasoning separate from answers for affected manually configured native Ollama models. [PR #169851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169851), [issue #134662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134662). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [papakpmartin](https://github.com/papakpmartin) and [xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn).
  - Ask supported utility models to turn thinking off for short titles and summaries, reducing empty results. Models that require reasoning can still exhaust their output budget. [PR #169920](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169920), [issue #153391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153391). Thanks to [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson) and [dwalthour](https://github.com/dwalthour).

  </details>

- Replay-safe model requests can recover from more timeouts, context-limit failures and quota errors using configured fallback. Billing and credential failures still require their own remedies.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Replay-safe model timeouts can advance to a configured fallback with a per-attempt timeout. [PR #141869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141869), [issue #141838](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141838), [related issue #103697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/103697). Thanks to [von8794](https://github.com/von8794), [alkor2000](https://github.com/alkor2000), and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - OpenRouter, Kilo Gateway, and other enabled providers that manage their own transient availability can be retried after upstream recovery. Credential, billing, and subscription blocks remain enforced. [PR #141825](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141825). Thanks to [clement-paradex](https://github.com/clement-paradex) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Repeated unchanged usage counters no longer keep a stalled Chat Completions request alive, while advancing token counts still indicate progress. [PR #145850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145850), [related issue #145203](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/145203). Thanks to [RileyJJY](https://github.com/RileyJJY) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with investigation by [anyech](https://github.com/anyech) and the report from [elharonyclaw](https://github.com/elharonyclaw).
  - Each admitted model retry or fallback gets its own recovery allowance. Queued work can wait longer while the current request uses it. [#164808](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164808), [#166497](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166497), [#165556](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/165556), [#145203](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/145203), [#133330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133330). Thanks to [anyech](https://github.com/anyech), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [vinsew](https://github.com/vinsew), [belbix](https://github.com/belbix), [tre1d](https://github.com/tre1d) and [aleps001](https://github.com/aleps001).
  - An unfinished ChatGPT Responses turn can recover from a normal connection close using the existing bounded recovery policy. [#167512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167512), [#167345](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167345). Thanks to [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), reporter [mightyx](https://github.com/mightyx) and [aleps001](https://github.com/aleps001).
  - Continue an eligible model retry when an optional retry-status callback fails ([#169141](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169141)). Thanks [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng).
  - Try configured fallbacks after context recovery is exhausted, while respecting model locks and replay restrictions. [PR #170141](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170141), [issue #9986](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/9986). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [amirarenco-create](https://github.com/amirarenco-create), [KarimVKarmali](https://github.com/KarimVKarmali), [engagepy](https://github.com/engagepy) and [zhupeng0o0](https://github.com/zhupeng0o0).
  - Configured fallback can handle provider-wrapped transient errors such as 503 or 429 in text-only responses. [#170527](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170527), thanks to [@TUARAN](https://github.com/TUARAN), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@diegoln](https://github.com/diegoln) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170388).
  - An unclassified empty normal completion no longer consumes the model fallback chain after same-model recovery. [#171199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171199). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reset the rate-limit retry budget after a completed model response, and explain when automatic retries are exhausted. [#171760](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171760), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recognized SiliconFlow prompt-limit errors enter the existing compaction and retry path. [#181519](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181519), [#181518](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181518) Thanks [@xpeng5278-web](https://github.com/xpeng5278-web) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recognized vLLM context-limit errors enter the existing compaction and retry path or provide overflow guidance when recovery cannot make the prompt fit. [#181521](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181521), [#181520](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181520) Thanks [@xpeng5278-web](https://github.com/xpeng5278-web).
  - Apply Ollama web search's configured result count and per-attempt timeout. An explicit search count takes precedence. [#182052](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182052) Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino).
  - Align tool-call and result names when replaying provider-prefixed transcript entries to native Ollama. [#181549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181549) Thanks [@xpeng5278-web](https://github.com/xpeng5278-web).
  - Apply provider-specific thinking controls to Kimi K3.1 Pro and Highspeed. The direct Moonshot route also handles native video and reasoning replay. Kimi Code cannot turn thinking off. [#181923](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181923) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Avoid a false model-refresh warning when the optional Claude CLI is absent, and give installation guidance when its authentication is explicitly selected. [#182729](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182729) Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Shorten oversized Claude CLI option descriptions instead of rejecting the entire question set. Oversized question text and labels still fail. [#182938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182938) Thanks [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the default llama.cpp model cache and managed binaries in durable shared state during temporary agent execution, preventing a later memory search from pointing at a deleted model. [#182781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182781) Thanks [@rjdotdev](https://github.com/rjdotdev), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@kir1ll](https://github.com/kir1ll).
  - Retry smaller embedding batches for the specific Mistral aggregate-token-limit response. A single input that still exceeds the limit remains an error. [#183567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183567) Thanks [@lonexreb](https://github.com/lonexreb) and [@drewjocham](https://github.com/drewjocham).
  - Leave fully redacted HTTP 400 and 422 provider errors unclassified instead of incorrectly labeling them as request-format errors. [#183006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183006) Thanks [@amittell](https://github.com/amittell), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prevent a closing tag inside a MiniMax tool argument from exposing the rest of the argument in affected streamed and final replies. [#183413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183413) Thanks [@gdpu11](https://github.com/gdpu11) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid Gateway stalls when assistant text resembles an incomplete or malformed tool call. [#183252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183252) Thanks [@LeizhenZhang](https://github.com/LeizhenZhang) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Model and reasoning choices follow the selected route more consistently. If an older model switch overwrote a saved thinking preference, set it again once.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Self-hosted Kimi K3 thinking selections reach the supported reasoning effort while retaining the existing Off default. [PR #139980](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139980), [unrelated issue reference #139040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139040). Thanks to [jsf9789](https://github.com/jsf9789) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Selected Qwen chat-template reasoning levels reach backends that declare supported efforts, while unselected turns keep the server default. [PR #148397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148397). Thanks to [areslp](https://github.com/areslp), [hippoley](https://github.com/hippoley), and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Configured `top_p` sampling values reach supported providers, including zero. When both spellings remain, `topP` takes precedence. [PR #148538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148538), [issue #127561](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127561). Thanks to [Raymondriter](https://github.com/Raymondriter) and reporter [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Copilot execution can use the matching account's reported prompt capacity in place of a synthetic estimate while respecting real limits and configured caps. Refresh discovery to replace older cached numeric estimates. [PR #156636](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156636), [related issue #156635](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156635). Thanks to [saju01](https://github.com/saju01) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), with design advice from [snotty](https://github.com/snotty), related reproduction from [vinaycodeaxe](https://github.com/vinaycodeaxe), and prior groundwork by [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) and [Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx).
  - MiniMax M3.1 Flash Preview catalog and reasoning controls support its five effort levels and avoid sending unsupported disabled thinking. Access requires an eligible account. [PR #160089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160089), [issues #160037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160037) and [#160491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160491). Thanks to [NianJiuZst](https://github.com/NianJiuZst), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [Takhoffman](https://github.com/Takhoffman), and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), with provider evidence from [php737](https://github.com/php737) and reports and investigation from [ns-spec](https://github.com/ns-spec), [Hansen1018](https://github.com/Hansen1018), and [olveww-dot](https://github.com/olveww-dot).
  - Select `openai/gpt-6-luna` for typed evaluations. ChatGPT and Codex subscription sign-in do not authorize this Platform API route. [#166320](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166320). Thanks to [jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [Kimiyu-186](https://github.com/Kimiyu-186) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - llama.cpp discovery honors the existing private-network opt-in for link-local servers. Enable it only for trusted endpoints because it also permits metadata addresses. [#163634](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163634), [#160389](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160389). Thanks to [harshitgavita-07](https://github.com/harshitgavita-07), [davidgrinc](https://github.com/davidgrinc) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Direct Z.AI GLM-5.3 and GLM-5.3-Flash calls apply the existing reasoning policy when thinking is omitted or set to off. [#164407](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164407), [#164328](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164328). Thanks to [Ayushdevo](https://github.com/Ayushdevo), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [nina-grech](https://github.com/nina-grech).
  - Map native Ollama thinking controls to each model's advertised values, including XHigh when supported ([#168166](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168166), [#157762](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157762)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Consult the admitted provider catalog for thinking levels in agent commands, replies and scheduled runs, while preserving explicit overrides. [#168566](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168566). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Pass configured context caps to Codex catalog-model threads on start and resume ([#168986](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168986)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Model switches retain the requested thinking level and show the effective level when it differs. [#169435](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169435), [issue #87925](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/87925). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hoobnn](https://github.com/hoobnn) and [@amittell](https://github.com/amittell).
  - Keeps a selected Codex Ultrafast tier across resumed and follow-up ChatGPT-authenticated turns when the account's model catalog supports it. [#169265](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169265), [issue #169264](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169264). Thanks [@SanderHelgesen](https://github.com/SanderHelgesen).
  - Preserves prior thinking-effort changes when OpenAI Responses streaming requests overlap in a conversation. [#169322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169322) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retains native Ollama reasoning across tool continuations and reopened sessions. Retained reasoning uses context and remains subject to compaction. [#169312](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169312), [related issue #147171](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/147171). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@wpu911](https://github.com/wpu911).
  - Let operators opt eligible custom-provider model rows into `responseModelEquivalent` when an endpoint resolves an alias to a served model. Actual configured fallbacks remain visible. [PR #170009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170009), [issue #170008](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170008). Thanks to [xyrolle](https://github.com/xyrolle) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid false model-route change notices for equivalent or dated OpenAI response IDs. [PR #169977](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169977), [issue #169976](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169976). Thanks to [xyrolle](https://github.com/xyrolle), [Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Use a 500,000-token fallback for GPT-6.1 Sol on OpenClaw subscription routes when no more specific account or configured limit is available. Direct API and native Codex sizing remain separate. [PR #169686](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169686). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Restore `/think off` for eligible native Claude CLI models without an Anthropic API key. Models requiring adaptive thinking continue to omit Off. [PR #169657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169657), [issue #169641](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169641). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Show the explicit Claude Code model name in the picker when the default alias resolves to that model. [PR #169670](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169670). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Update MiMo and Mistral recommendation order and include Muse Wonder in recommendations when a provider serves it. [PR #170106](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170106). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - OpenClaw uses a 260,000-token context cap for Ultrafast client and server compaction paths, respecting smaller configured limits. [#170844](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170844), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Standard, Fast and Ultrafast choices override an inherited speed tier where the model, account and route support them. The current request keeps its original choice. Keyboard navigation also reaches nested hovercard content. [#171783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171783), thanks to [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Allowed automatic child-session model selections are kept when stale routing is repaired. [#183728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183728) Thanks [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks [@Iolaum](https://github.com/Iolaum) for the report.
  - Recognize self-hosted reasoning aliases and make `/think off` work with underscore-prefixed Nemotron aliases. An explicit reasoning opt-out still applies. [#122022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/122022), [reported in #121597](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/121597). Thanks to [krell11](https://github.com/krell11) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Typed level directives recognize existing digit and underscore aliases, including `/verbose 1` and `/think extra_high`. [Typed level aliases](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168470), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168254). Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi), [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Deprecated-model errors use configured fallback or guide you to choose another model instead of suggesting a retry. [Deprecated model guidance](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168187). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Personal-account quota rechecks and credential refreshes no longer abort replies because unset optional fields are represented differently in storage. See [#170065](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170065) and [#169850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169850). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@modlethub](https://github.com/modlethub) and [@Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx).
  - Personal Codex requests avoid a synchronous final credential read while retaining an authorization check before dispatch. A reconnect or profile merge can invalidate a prepared request. [PR #170330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170330). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve the selected Codex account configuration for online `status --usage --json`, avoiding a misleading generic expiration result ([#170692](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170692)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stop retries and model fallback for retired input and permanent request rejection, while retaining transient recovery and context-limit compaction ([#170925](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170925)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recognize the provider's input-length error as a context overflow. [#171712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171712) by [ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225). Thanks to [xpeng5278-web](https://github.com/xpeng5278-web). See [#171673](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171673).
  - Async tool calls preserve reasoning replay context for later OpenAI Responses requests. [#171763](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171763) follows [#171431](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171431). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - DashScope and Model Studio routes use registered Qwen quota hooks. A custom native URL alone does not guarantee Qwen activation from a cold start. [#171750](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171750) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). The separate [input-length report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171673) from [xpeng5278-web](https://github.com/xpeng5278-web) is addressed by [#171712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171712).
  - Transcript admission conflicts stop the affected turn instead of triggering model or provider fallback. [#177956](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177956). Thanks [@hootrock](https://github.com/hootrock), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@hussont](https://github.com/hussont).
  - Preserve person-linked model account selections across session resets and rollovers ([#179433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179433), [#179432](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/179432)). Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino).
  - Isolated completions retain access to the agent's discovered model catalog. [#182952](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182952) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Settle fallback notices against the model admitted for the session after a successful reply. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b68da67ec7594e50734cb3333b2f3cf9707b8659), thanks to [joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).

  </details>

- Provider sign-in and discovery preserve more available account and model choices through credential refreshes instead of showing them as unavailable.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Startup cleanup logs and skips an unreadable managed GitHub profile directory while continuing to clean up other eligible retired profiles. [PR #171226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171226), [issue #171035](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171035). Thanks [fhgffy](https://github.com/fhgffy) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Resolve identity before the first self-profile read ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/466573573374954076729309c765ba457505ba57)).
  - MiniMax Portal OAuth renews access tokens through their issuing region. [PR #160459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160459), [issue #77467](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/77467). Thanks to [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), with reports from [rongtianhua](https://github.com/rongtianhua) and [mychataa](https://github.com/mychataa).
  - ChatGPT device-code sign-in keeps its polling timeout through system clock changes. [#162828](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162828). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - OAuth refresh waits retain their timeout budget when the system clock changes. [#167619](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167619). Thanks to [sunnyshu0925](https://github.com/sunnyshu0925) and [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Save supported API keys through the running local Gateway and refresh model availability without restarting. Explicit nonempty profile IDs and unsupported flows still require offline setup. [#168588](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168588). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Complete supported provider OAuth, device-code and plugin API-key sign-in in the terminal through the running local Gateway. Unsupported options require offline setup, and an older Gateway must be updated and restarted for this route. [#168671](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168671). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Resolve saved API keys correctly during provider model discovery so model lists can populate after sign-in. [#168617](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168617). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Use the configured managed proxy for a standalone `openclaw models list --refresh`. [#168554](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168554). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
