  - Make models found by an offline refresh available to a separate local agent turn while their provider credentials remain valid. [#168618](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168618). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reject the local-only `ollama-local` placeholder during Ollama Cloud setup and point to the hosted key page. Local Ollama setup is unchanged. [#168652](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168652). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Allow shared OAuth credentials to refresh during model discovery when historical or empty agent databases exist ([#168957](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168957)). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Lets a newly saved API key be tried on the next request after the old key hit a rate limit. [#169528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169528), [issue #151354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/151354). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [@blance2599](https://github.com/blance2599).
  - Fixes local Codex model discovery with an environment OpenAI API key in an isolated agent home without a selected profile. [#169350](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169350) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An explicit model authentication refresh reflects removed saved credentials. [#170201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170201), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Claude CLI authentication checks allow up to ten seconds and distinguish a timeout from a confirmed logout. [#170604](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170604), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@veritasaiflows-cell](https://github.com/veritasaiflows-cell).
  - Eligible account-only Codex models retain their subscription-backed route and OAuth profile during native model selection. [#170639](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170639), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Model Setup guides device sign-in and configures the selected agent for Muse Code. [#171526](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171526), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Provider Access offers the same sign-in while leaving the active model in place, so you can choose a Muse model separately. [#171790](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171790), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep recommendations for currently served models visible after upgrades, even when older catalog rows and prices cannot be used. [#172622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172622) Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep one agent's catalog changes from interrupting another agent's pending model-picker request, and add a bounded Prometheus counter for model-list requests. [#172514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172514) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Renew an expired Sign in with ChatGPT credential and retry the selected model once before fallback. Renewal scheduling also respects the expiry in a valid access token. [#172593](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172593) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@vinsew](https://github.com/vinsew) for the [expiry](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172562) and [recovery](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172563) reports.
  - Reuse provider ownership lookups within each model registry. [#172481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172481) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore session title generation for affected custom Zhipu and Z.AI GLM endpoints and give clearer warnings when title generation fails. [#172580](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172580) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@TommyLei666](https://github.com/TommyLei666) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171068).
  - Preserve valid `__proto__` keys in cloned Mistral tool schemas. [#172587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172587) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fix OpenRouter setup incorrectly rejecting valid keys or saved models because of a model identity mismatch. [#173292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173292) Thanks to [@chandraveshchaudhari](https://github.com/chandraveshchaudhari) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Recover valid DeepSeek tool requests in managed sessions through OpenRouter and compatible routes without exposing their markup or duplicating normal tool calls. Direct mode filters the markup without executing recovered calls. [#175058](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175058) Thanks to [@Linux2010](https://github.com/Linux2010), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@ZhimingQ](https://github.com/ZhimingQ) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174909).
  - Affected OpenAI models through GMI can use tools without the incompatible reasoning request. For those tool requests, enabled reasoning is automatically disabled or omitted. [#176709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176709) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The model catalog honors an agent's configured primary account pin, preventing a false missing-auth result for a usable profile. [#178522](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178522), [#178502](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/178502), [#178549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/178549) Thanks [@ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Admitted embedded and CLI agent turns retain their prepared model alias guidance during a configuration refresh. [#179192](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179192) Thanks [@ensignwesley](https://github.com/ensignwesley) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - OpenAI-compatible model retrieval accepts existing agent aliases and returns the canonical model object. [#180057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180057) Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Recognized billing failures retain account-credit guidance in session and run notices instead of being presented as rate limits. [#180867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180867), [#180856](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180856) Thanks [@eliavb43](https://github.com/eliavb43).
  - Known OpenAI models without an explicitly configured custom route can use their official routes when stale catalog metadata would otherwise block credentials. [#181356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181356), [#181353](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181353) Thanks [@sasan1200](https://github.com/sasan1200).

  </details>

- Model pickers show current supported choices and clearer setup guidance. Removing a catalog entry does not automatically migrate an existing pinned model.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - `openclaw models auth list` shows active subscription block reasons, expiry, source, and scope in text and JSON. [PR #133087](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133087), [issue #133065](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133065). Thanks to [Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Expanding a model catalog to additional providers can reuse unchanged plugin registrations and avoid repeated preparation work. [PR #162226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162226), [issues #161379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161379) and [#162230](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162230). Thanks to [netcrazed](https://github.com/netcrazed) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), with verification by [kiralee](https://github.com/kiralee).
  - Copilot setup verification and Ask OpenClaw work with an explicitly selected Copilot model. Verified setup uses the packaged stdio runtime, and replacing the SDK requires a restart and repeat verification. [#163578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163578), [#163406](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/163406). Thanks to [lifuyue](https://github.com/lifuyue), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and reporter [tjegbejimba](https://github.com/tjegbejimba).
  - Model alias and fallback additions reject known-invalid provider names before saving when the provider inventory is available. When it cannot be checked, the selection saves with a warning. [#165744](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165744). Thanks to [Sedrak-Hovhannisyan](https://github.com/Sedrak-Hovhannisyan) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preparing model lists for large configured provider catalogs avoids repeated reference parsing. [#166223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166223). Thanks to [fede-kamel](https://github.com/fede-kamel) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Ollama Cloud usage appears in chat `/status` and `openclaw status --usage` for a signed-in configured server running Ollama 0.40.1 or newer. [#167474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167474). Thanks to [usimic](https://github.com/usimic) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - `openclaw infer model run` reports failed local checks and failed, timed-out or cancelled Gateway checks as failures even when partial text arrived. [#167874](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167874). Thanks to [ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway-driven default Codex chats can use ChatGPT sign-in for account-listed models whose routes are missing from the built-in lists. [#168033](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168033). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Refresh LM Studio models with `openclaw models list --provider lmstudio --refresh`. Merge mode preserves configured rows and metadata; explicit replace mode keeps only configured models. [#168039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168039), [#150262](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/150262). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [jb-lopez](https://github.com/jb-lopez).
  - LM Studio gives cold loads more time and provides recovery guidance when a required capacity upgrade fails. The existing timeout can be adjusted in the [LM Studio guide](/providers/lmstudio). [#168044](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168044), [#141357](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141357). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep embedding-only models out of new Ollama chat choices and explain how to recover from an existing entry or missing embedding model. Re-onboarding replaces the provider catalog, so preserve custom entries first ([#168075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168075)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Honor selected llama.cpp model aliases and profile-qualified references, and report credential-save failures during existing-server setup ([#168232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168232)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Refresh loaded llama.cpp router models with verified context limits and tool support while preserving explicit pins ([#168309](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168309), [#139802](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139802)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@guarismo](https://github.com/guarismo).
  - Show a healthy managed llama.cpp embedding server as ready even when its optional metrics endpoint is unavailable ([#168204](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168204)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Remove matching saved models from the live picker when a configured provider with an explicit base URL is removed. Earlier removals are not cleaned up retroactively. [#168724](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168724). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reduce synchronous work in personal model lists after a model configuration reload. [#168733](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168733). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show account-listed OpenCode Zen models even when public metadata is missing, and explain client-restricted free-tier refusals without placing the shared credential in cooldown. Listing a model does not guarantee access. [#168601](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168601). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Choose an advertised GitHub Copilot model API when endpoint paths have a version prefix or trailing slash. [#168672](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168672). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Let affected cron and chat turns start after a model catalog update arrives during startup. [#168410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168410). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Repair configurations using the `https://ollama.com` origin with `openclaw doctor --fix` or update repair, including saved session and scheduled-job routes ([#168782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168782)). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve fallback eligibility and later default changes when selecting the agent's effective default model ([#169030](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169030)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Identify enabled external official Codex plugins older than 2026.9.7 before registration and show repair guidance. Plugin update failures also show their reason ([#168930](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168930)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks [@ezimerman](https://github.com/ezimerman) and [@ADLBAS26](https://github.com/ADLBAS26) for the reports. For an official npm installation, run `openclaw update repair` or `openclaw plugins update codex` and restart the Gateway. For a linked-source installation, update the source or remove the load-path override.
  - Reduce model availability lookup work for large native catalogs and the model picker ([#169048](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169048)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh setup checks available inference options and activates a verified model, while keeping an existing configured model. Codex access can use a shared home or a separate login. [#169628](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169628) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - AI choices can appear sooner during macOS, Control UI and CLI setup. [#169562](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169562) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Shows the reported model discovery error beside Retry while keeping saved choices available. [#169316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169316) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keeps model browsing on the replacement catalog during a refresh, avoiding a transient picker failure. [#169478](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169478) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Shows additional model fallbacks and how many clearing the fallback chain will remove. [#169511](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169511) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Fixes plugin-discovered models failing in sessions opened outside their agent workspace. [#169539](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169539), [issue #169205](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169205). Thanks [@ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@AVCarp](https://github.com/AVCarp).
  - Restores the requested configuration for installed native ESM provider plugins after a failed catalog refresh. [#169321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169321), [related #169272](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169272). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Tells clients when a request refused before its turn starts because the model catalog changed can be retried. [#169623](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169623) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fixes switching to an OpenAI model without the Codex plugin when the built-in runtime can serve it. An explicit Codex runtime selection still requires the plugin. [#169412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169412) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Warn about configured models missing from successful Ollama, LM Studio and supported OpenAI-compatible server listings. Offline servers do not produce this warning, and custom models remain selectable. [PR #169708](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169708), [issue #39811](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/39811). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [LindalyX-Lee](https://github.com/LindalyX-Lee), [dsantoreis](https://github.com/dsantoreis) and [exisz](https://github.com/exisz).
  - Healthy agents retain commands and model choices during a reload when another agent's model owner is unavailable. [#170805](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170805), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Models with invalid pricing are skipped with a warning while valid siblings remain available. [#170882](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170882), related to [#170458](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170458). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Managed Codex version checks avoid a pipe race that could leave Doctor without JSON output. [#171248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171248), reported in [#171169](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171169). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@dtump](https://github.com/dtump), and [@joshpetras](https://github.com/joshpetras).
  - The NVIDIA guide shows how to pair a text-only Nemotron primary model with a separate Super VL image-understanding model. [#171072](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171072). Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson) for confirming the model reference.
  - Removed unavailable bundled models and updated Cerebras, Together, DeepInfra and OpenCode defaults. The retired Groq model's oversized-request retry was also removed. [#171879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171879), with related [hosted catalog work](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167855) and [Doctor model-choice discussion](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169473). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Updated Google setup, Gemini aliases and model selection, including core Gemini 4 shorthand normalization. [#172339](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172339), following [Gemini 4 Argon support](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171852). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Set the default endpoint for key-only Qwen Coding Plan requests. [#172447](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172447). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Replaced Nemotron 3 Super with Nemotron 3.5 Super VL in NVIDIA onboarding and fallback choices, and preserved declared video input metadata in the model catalog. Nemotron 3 Ultra remains the default. [#172449](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172449). Thanks [@nv-kasikritc](https://github.com/nv-kasikritc) and [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
  - Moved Kimi K3 immediately after GPT-6 Luna in the recommended-model list. [#172461](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172461). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reduced repeated readiness work when listing large native model catalogs. [#172311](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172311). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Kept other agents' model choices cached in the Control UI when one agent's catalog changes. [#172418](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172418). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Added explicit Imagine Image 2.0 selection and errors for unknown bare image-model overrides. [#172445](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172445). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@JFLeonM](https://github.com/JFLeonM) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141547).

  </details>

- Affected provider routes preserve supported tool results, request options and conversation continuations instead of rejecting valid input.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - OpenAI and Azure Responses output requests respect declared model limits. [PR #128282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128282), [issue #127560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127560). Thanks to [parker-fawcett](https://github.com/parker-fawcett) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), with the report from [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Existing sessions with tool history can continue on managed no-tools Chat Completions models, with prior tool context replayed as text. [PR #148106](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148106). Thanks to [jimmy00415](https://github.com/jimmy00415) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Provider request transformations survive synchronous or asynchronous hooks that replace the outgoing body. [PR #160567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160567). Thanks to [YangManBOBO](https://github.com/YangManBOBO) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Send runtime context to compatible Responses servers using a user-role message they can accept. [#168546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168546), [issue #164791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164791), [provider roles guide](/concepts/context#runtime-context-and-provider-roles). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@flanker-stack](https://github.com/flanker-stack) for the report and investigation.
  - Let guarded model requests try IPv6 after an IPv4 route fails when Node address-family autoselection is enabled ([#168973](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168973)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@an2in](https://github.com/an2in), [@georgenaz](https://github.com/georgenaz), [@motookoyama](https://github.com/motookoyama), and [@orthogonalistic](https://github.com/orthogonalistic).
  - Applies configured cache-TTL context pruning to eligible OpenRouter Grok and Gemini 2.5/3 sessions. This pruning remains off by default for these providers. [#169548](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169548), [issue #169133](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169133). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@gregb100](https://github.com/gregb100).
  - Responses tool continuations and transcript reloads avoid spurious assistant-history mismatch warnings. [#170901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170901). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Corrected Groq Qwen thinking and reasoning output, and marked the Groq and Cohere models as text-only. [#171914](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171914). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fixed Ollama requests in source-based in-process Gateway hosts. [#172013](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172013). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduced repeated Ultrafast rejections within a run while retaining the saved preference. [#172315](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172315), following [tier selection work](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165990). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fixed thinking-off requests for Baseten DeepSeek V4 Pro 0813. [#172330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172330). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserved OpenAI Responses reasoning prefixes across session reopen and completed async calls across interrupted streams. [#172455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172455). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Inferred supported missing union-branch types in Moonshot and Kimi MCP tool schemas. [#172478](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172478). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@rmoskal](https://github.com/rmoskal) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/113130).

  </details>

- Gemini 4 Argon is selectable on supported Google routes, with its supported thinking choices. Rerunning Google API-key setup selects it as the primary model, so check that choice if you intend to keep another default.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Move a replay-safe turn to a configured fallback when the provider explicitly reports an exhausted organization token quota. Other rate limits keep their normal retry behavior. [#171873](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171873), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep provider sign-in status visible while settings change and refresh it when updated authentication information arrives. [#171579](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171579), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Allow Agents API to be selected for shared models with OpenClaw compaction settings. The hosted runtime manages its own compaction. [#171813](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171813), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Add a native Ollama configuration example for llmman that retains tool use with an explicitly configured model. [#171444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171444), thanks to [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin).
  - Update the recommended-model list to include Gemini 4 Argon and revise the leading order. [#171429](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171429), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for the approved curation.
  - Support Gemini 4 Argon selection, thinking levels and tool-call replay through Google and Vertex. Thinking cannot be fully disabled, and explicit caches and the Live API are unsupported for this model. [#171852](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171852), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- More unrelated conversations remain usable while other agents or large histories prepare.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Move bundled inference and Models logout authentication storage work off the Gateway thread. If the same profile reconnects while logout cleanup is still running, you may need to select that account again. [#171510](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171510), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid copying stored session metadata that is excluded from model context when preparing follow-up turns. [#171574](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171574), thanks to [joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
  - Reduce memory used to prepare optional session forks for long conversations. If fork capture fails, the parent request can continue. [#171683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171683), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep lean local-model Tool Search directories compact while leaving full descriptions and schemas searchable. [#171588](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171588), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [chitowntony](https://github.com/chitowntony).
  - Avoid loading unrelated memory and context plugins for simple completions, image descriptions and one-shot decisions. [#171822](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171822), thanks to [htuyer121](https://github.com/htuyer121) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [gorkem2020](https://github.com/gorkem2020) for tracing the affected paths, and [Cyb3rb1ade](https://github.com/Cyb3rb1ade) for the [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/163029).
  - Avoid a false managed local-model shutdown failure on Windows when the service has exited but its termination helper is still finishing. [#171587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171587), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) for the original repair and [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171472).
  - Move full Linux Codex cleanup process scans off the Gateway thread. [#171865](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171865), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep scheduled maintenance on track after clock corrections, refresh placement clients after uncertain worker results, and finish memory-worker cleanup before releasing the database after failed initialization. [#171415](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171415), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep unchanged agents available while another agent is created or deleted ([#169249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169249)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Wait for the requested agent to finish starting without holding up ready agents ([#168826](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168826)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks [@xiaozishan](https://github.com/xiaozishan) for the report.
  - Prevent a settled database write from leaving an agent stuck rejecting messages when a separate read remains open ([#169064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169064)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Skip completed child-session reads during subagent restart recovery so an unreadable completed session does not block later active runs ([#169034](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169034)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep unconfirmed subagent completion announcements eligible for bounded recovery after transcript contention ([#169015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169015)). Thanks [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep accepted steering messages in order and their consumption status aligned with the saved conversation ([#169061](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169061)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce agent roster and related chat-read work for large model and agent configurations ([#169123](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169123)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fixes a waiting agent failing to continue after an accepted answer. [#169306](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169306) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fixes steering a running native Codex turn hanging on Linux and blocking later conversation writes. [#169343](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169343) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Restores compatible Codex follow-ups after conversation preparation fails, including while a background command continues. Incompatible policy changes remain refused while that work is active. [#169431](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169431) Thanks [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Preserves separate watched-task results across a Gateway restart when tasks share a child session. Replacing the same task still invalidates its older result. [#169489](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169489) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fixes a next message failing while saved conversation history is rebuilt after native compaction. [#169583](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169583) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keeps recent context consistent in long conversations when messages arrive during a read. [#169619](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169619), [related issue #127629](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127629). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Moves GitHub discovery into the background so repository-backed agent turns can reach the provider sooner. Tool visibility may briefly reflect the previous account during a refresh. [#169488](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169488) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserves network-source labels through Tool Search and Code Mode for new tool results and subsequent replies. Existing history and stored memory are not rewritten. [#169560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169560), [issue #169332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169332). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@farisoftfry](https://github.com/farisoftfry).
  - Warm turns reuse a session settings snapshot and local computer discovery facts. [#170185](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170185), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Workspace prompt prefixes remain steadier as capabilities change, and live and replayed failed-turn histories agree. [#170516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170516), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An agent can continue once after malformed tool arguments follow consumed failures that were all proven not to have started. [#170615](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170615), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@r3n3x](https://github.com/r3n3x) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170522), [@fransqaas](https://github.com/fransqaas) for related work and [@akushonkamen](https://github.com/akushonkamen) for the investigation.
  - Responses tool calls using a supported alias no longer fail with a false tool-name change, including when spawning a child. [#170751](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170751), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preparation diagnostics distinguish request encoding without exposing prompt content, and context-engine input and output keep tool calls paired within the history limit. [#170830](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170830), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Deferred agents become available before background transcript repair completes. [#170849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170849), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Large agent fleets avoid repeated session-path and configuration work after Gateway readiness. [#170612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170612), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT).
  - Delayed worker results remain valid when unchanged agent database schema facts are republished. [#170194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170194), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Agent creation gives more accurate workspace, model and access choices instead of blocking valid setups with stale state.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Allow explicitly authorized local coding children to use their own tools ([#168085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168085)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Select a compatible hosted workspace and model from session creation and settings ([#168259](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168259)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve supported saved conversations across the binding upgrade ([#168150](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168150)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Drain active agent work before deletion and resume pending cleanup after restart ([#168199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168199)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve ACP tool narration and the final answer after commentary ([#168201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168201), [#167837](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167837)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@YiWang24](https://github.com/YiWang24).
  - Keep Claude CLI models in the picker after plugin or agent settings reload ([#168315](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168315)). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Show account-listed OpenCode Go chat models even when public metadata is missing ([#168286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168286)). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Show OpenCode Zen Kimi K3's supported thinking levels and send the explicit Off setting ([#168209](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168209), [#168174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168174)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show one xAI model group in early lists after a restart while retaining existing x-ai references ([#168180](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168180)). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Recognize the current agent's model aliases as slash shortcuts ([#168287](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168287), [#168122](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168122)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and co-author and reporter [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Local-provider preflight skips count toward the configured failure-alert threshold. Jobs stay scheduled, and full failure-alert opt-outs still apply. [#169278](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169278), [issue #168026](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168026). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Allow agent creation alongside the main agent's first session. [PR #169779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169779). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep an open agent database connection associated with its original database when a symlink is retargeted. [PR #169701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169701). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Skip stale multiple-store sentinel registrations during configured-agent startup and add standalone Doctor cleanup for invalid registrations. Cleanup is deferred during updates. [PR #170043](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170043), [issue #169764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169764). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [ourcoms2018](https://github.com/ourcoms2018).
  - Keep the revoked status and remove the Revoke button after a successful revocation. [PR #169695](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169695). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Doctor and startup can recover selected older stores and rebuild eligible damaged search indexes while preserving original data. Mixed or unknown damage remains refused; stop OpenClaw and keep a verified backup before offline repair.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Deleting one agent no longer falsely blocks registration for another with independent identity and database paths. [#170309](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170309), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor and Gateway migration process large compressed transcript archives record by record. An individual record beyond Node's string limit remains unsupported. [#170853](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170853), thanks to [@iamonlv426](https://github.com/iamonlv426) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Managed updates use private per-user temporary storage and can recover eligible old receipts when another user owns their former directory. [#170174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170174), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169382).
  - Updates and Doctor can retain SQLite recovery backups in deeply nested paths that previously ran out of temporary path space. [#170867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170867), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Linux child process memory-pressure policy, including Claude CLI and other eligible children. The adjustment is best effort and can be overridden or disabled. [#170899](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170899), reported in [#170869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170869). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@eliavb43](https://github.com/eliavb43).
  - Claude CLI process cleanup after Gateway loss on Linux and macOS. [#171016](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171016), reported in [#170868](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170868). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@eliavb43](https://github.com/eliavb43).
  - Reduced repeated admission and catalog work for large agent fleets. [#171371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171371), reported in [#171351](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171351). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT).
  - Retained databases for unconfigured agents no longer block admission, and an incompatible secondary agent can be refused while healthy agents remain usable. The affected agent receives `openclaw doctor --fix` guidance. [#171372](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171372), related to [#171348](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171348). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@LifeViwer](https://github.com/LifeViwer).
  - Credential file updates reuse Gateway readers and reconnect retained observers after a restart. [#171246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171246). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Long streamed replies use less Gateway CPU while preserving text corrections and Unicode boundaries. [#171361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171361), reported in [#171360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171360). Thanks [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Bound the wait for an active run during agent deletion and identify the session that needs to finish before a retry. [#172511](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172511), [issue #172416](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172416). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce work when admitting subagent spawns with large retained run registries. [#172480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172480) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce repeated SQLite worker dispatch work for large session inventories. [#172532](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172532) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject malformed UTF-8 in Claude instruction files before importing altered text. Save or convert the affected file to UTF-8 and rerun the migration. [#172953](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172953) Thanks to [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve the first agent's database ownership during concurrent initialization. [#174251](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174251) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Complete upgrades for verified empty native-first agent stores and report failure when a required held store remains unusable. Stores with missing deletion history that contain data or have uncertain contents remain protected. [#174297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174297) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore qualifying populated native-first agents with missing deletion history during update repair or `openclaw doctor --fix --non-interactive`, after verified backups. Recovery is limited to recognized profiles and carries a small risk of restoring data from a pending deletion. Other uncertain stores remain held and may require restoration. See the [database recovery guidance](/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery). [#175007](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175007) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway and media-migration corruption refusals point to agent-specific recovery. Stop the Gateway and back up the database and its SQLite sidecars before running `openclaw doctor --session-sqlite recover --session-sqlite-agent <id>` with the same state and configuration. [PR #170249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170249), reported in [#169390](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169390). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@xiajiahang](https://github.com/xiajiahang), [@nkeil](https://github.com/nkeil), and [@jonathanlindsay](https://github.com/jonathanlindsay) for the report.
  - Doctor skips affected OAuth cleanup when a legacy authentication file is unreadable, preserving saved profiles and showing the file to inspect or restore. [PR #170256](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170256), fixing [#168959](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168959). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@nosmile99](https://github.com/nosmile99), and [@Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot) for the report.
  - Gateway exec blocks recognized variable-bearing SQLite file URIs that target live OpenClaw state. Private database copies remain available for inspection. [PR #170253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170253). The separate working-directory issue [#134612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134612) was reported by [@phuc-nt](https://github.com/phuc-nt). Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - The first SQLite state database open can retry temporary schema contention within its existing deadline. [PR #170300](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170300). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - First session writes can initialize an absent store during a usage refresh, and writes no longer stall in the identified race with usage pruning. [PR #170308](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170308). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A SQLite worker admission race no longer rolls back a session append when a new connection opens as the last known connection closes. [PR #170423](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170423). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recovery warns and defers before replacing a configuration when its original cannot be saved. [#171217](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171217), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot) for [reporting](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171187).
  - Doctor and legacy SDK data selection respect the active profile and its state directory. [#171439](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171439), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor can migrate older histories with duplicate session headers that differ only by timestamp, while preserving the original archive. Conflicting headers still block migration. [#172133](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172133), reported in [#172109](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172109). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [wangxiajun68](https://github.com/wangxiajun68).
  - Skip the legacy notes migration when a file contains invalid UTF-8, preserving its original bytes. [#172293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172293). Thanks to [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Leave retired-agent stores untouched and warn about their paths while repairing eligible active sessions. [#172295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172295), related to [#172259](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172259). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).
  - Keep unrelated historical repairs out of recent canonical schema upgrades and allow same-version Doctor to perform deferred work. [#172390](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172390). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fix Bun SQLite worker transfers that could interrupt Doctor migrations and update maintenance. [#172175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172175). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Add migration support and reject incompatible servers or unexpected schema changes at startup. [#172265](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172265). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor avoids repeatedly scanning migration history when stale session import records point to archived transcripts. [#172890](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172890), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DongwonGim](https://github.com/DongwonGim) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172809), and [@skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223) for investigating.
  - Doctor can back up and rebuild eligible incompatible SQLite search indexes without discarding agent data. For offline repair, stop the Gateway and run `openclaw doctor --fix` with the Node runtime used by the Gateway. Findings mixed with other damage are not eligible. [Recovery guidance](/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery) · [#176234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176234). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@ndakota79](https://github.com/ndakota79).
  - Gateway startup can back up and rebuild eligible stale agent search indexes before admitting an agent. Mixed or unknown damage and failed repairs remain quarantined. [#176833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176833). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@ndakota79](https://github.com/ndakota79).
  - `openclaw doctor --fix` can verify and recover eligible held `openclaw` and `crestodian` system-agent stores after a schema update. Until repair succeeds, an affected old-schema store remains unavailable while healthy agents can start. [#176783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176783). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@PennyVibe](https://github.com/PennyVibe).
  - After disk space is freed and cleanup and integrity checks succeed, an affected agent can accept new writes without a Gateway restart. Interrupted writes have unknown outcomes and are not replayed. [#176902](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176902). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve malformed UTF-8 sources or their migration archives, warn instead of importing altered text, and keep damaged Skill Workshop recovery bundles available ([#180861](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180861)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666).
  - Preserve malformed node-host and Web Push sources or interrupted claims before recovery can move or discard them. Back up affected files and correct copies before retrying Doctor ([#180850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180850)). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Finish plugin repair after a verified session import when a retained historical archive is missing, while keeping the recovery warning ([#178779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178779), [#167385](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167385)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@lostsock1](https://github.com/lostsock1) and [@IliyaNexus](https://github.com/IliyaNexus).
  - Avoid repeating settled legacy-state scans and notices during a Doctor repair when the inputs have not changed ([#180576](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180576)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@MertBasar0](https://github.com/MertBasar0).
  - Keep agent database readers valid across a schema refresh when their validation receipt is unchanged. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0644d3a70aa1397d61b8d4ac66c9b815fcb432fb)
  - Reject stale reader validation after unsafe database-open or cleanup failures, while preserving readers after a clean refusal before opening. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/acc5395d48360aedc1dd7353085e1d9d315b3efc)
  - Avoid repeatedly hashing retained migration sources at startup, let Doctor retire missing trajectory diagnostics, and retry startup migration admission for a lost lease with up to three total attempts. [#170936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170936), [report #170693](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170693). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@NOVA-Openclaw](https://github.com/NOVA-Openclaw).
  - A later database close can release a completed operation's stranded state lock after storage recovers, allowing protected work to proceed. [#173225](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173225), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Interrupted tools preserve completed output and make uncertain outcomes explicit. Only work known not to have started or safe to replay is retried automatically.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep optional Ultrafast model discovery within its timeout when the system clock changes ([#168144](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168144)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Keep the session fork metadata timeout stable across clock changes ([#168298](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168298)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Release a canceled startup's queue position so a later attempt can proceed to its own handoff wait. Replacement writes still wait for the old process to exit ([#168347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168347)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Keep the native Codex inference connection timeout steady when the system clock changes after admission. [#168731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168731). Thanks to [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep Codex app-server process inspection within its intended time budget when the host clock changes. [#168736](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168736). Thanks to [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - Resume queued chats after a plugin reload when the exact old thread is unloaded, and preserve startup failures and cancellations as their intended outcomes. [#182104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182104) Thanks [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - R2 downloads resume after transient interruptions during verification or restore. Starting a new command begins a new download. [#171292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171292), reported in [#171165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171165). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini).
  - Added a safe retry or explicit failure for detected text-only tool calls. [#171898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171898), with related reports [#138245](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138245) and [#168744](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168744). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@5jrfrbd6z9-spec](https://github.com/5jrfrbd6z9-spec) and [@chitowntony](https://github.com/chitowntony).
  - Allowed consented Muse chats with conversation tool restrictions while retaining the native permission boundary. [#172361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172361). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserved recovered final replies and required complete, identified delivery before recording success. [#171929](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171929), related to [#155119](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155119). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Report unfinished tool execution timeouts as errors and retain completed output without automatically replaying the turn. [#172983](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172983) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@budzation](https://github.com/budzation) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172899).
  - Preserve blank lines when agents read files across pages, including reads that start on a blank line. [#173835](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173835) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep recognized final exit, signal, timeout and launch-failure messages when long command output is shortened. [#174030](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174030) Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Honor explicitly preserved login environment credentials in Claude CLI readiness checks so affected child-agent requests can proceed. [#175114](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175114) Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Interrupted tool recovery requires safe tools and refuses CLI runtimes that cannot enforce them. The same backport improves Codex handoffs and command results, preserves the active request during compaction, supports OpenClaw goal continuation in supported local and cloud-worker Codex sessions, and provides a masked GitHub reconnect flow in capable Control UI sessions. Native Codex automatic goal continuation remains disabled. [#180270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180270) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Native session read failures show their actual error, allowing eligible timeouts before execution to use the existing scheduled-job retry policy. [#175654](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175654) Thanks [@enki](https://github.com/enki).
  - Keep internal Gateway and plugin response timeouts on their elapsed-time budget when the computer clock changes. [#171094](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171094), related [#108423](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/108423) and [#154321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154321). Thanks to [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Changing agent settings, plugins or credentials preserves more unrelated configuration and eligible active work instead of causing unnecessary restarts.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - An authorized reset can restore the canonical Main session archived by restart recovery, retaining its identity and transcript while starting fresh model context. The interrupted task is not replayed. [#176675](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176675), [#137264](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137264), [#173412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/173412) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@enki](https://github.com/enki), [@r3n3x](https://github.com/r3n3x) and [@cavatison](https://github.com/cavatison).
  - Managed runtimes and images use the patched Node floor, and source contributors use pnpm 12.15.0. Immediate join and hook turns use event routing and authentication, while restarted subagents prefer the admitted caller's authority. [#181074](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181074), [#179992](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179992) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
  - Partial HTML-like tags in streamed replies avoid unnecessary full parsing where their meaning is clear. [#180765](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180765), [#180720](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180720) Thanks [@JasonBuildAI](https://github.com/JasonBuildAI) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Long streams containing tool-call XML avoid repeated parsing of settled text. [#181397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181397), [#175156](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/175156) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@JasonBuildAI](https://github.com/JasonBuildAI).
  - Use the configured inherited credential owner when preparing agent model turns. [#181812](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181812) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Return an empty agent list without waiting for model catalogs when the operator's role allows no agents. [#182159](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182159) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Reject explicitly blank `--identity-file` and `--workspace` values in `openclaw agents set-identity`. Omit a flag to use its normal default. [#182622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182622) Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Sanitize human-readable agent and team creation summaries while preserving the original stored and JSON values. [#183360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183360) Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Return structured JSON errors for caught session compaction failures, preserving the requested session key. [#184131](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184131) Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep the shared OpenCode session browsing timeout stable across clock changes. [#182295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182295) Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep queued macOS conversation navigation within its existing elapsed-time deadline when the system clock changes. [#182627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182627) Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep managed local-provider readiness and shutdown waits within their existing time budgets across clock changes. [#182778](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182778) Thanks [@mikasa0818](https://github.com/mikasa0818) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Protect routed config set, patch, and unset edits from predicted differences in reference or ownership preparation, and report when a copied session-store owner is cleared in [#184194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184194). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Retry superseded preparation before execution and defer automatic plugin reload while admitted work settles in [#185573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185573). A saved configuration can remain pending if that work never settles. Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid rebuilding prepared runtimes for availability-only authentication changes in [#185250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185250). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for reporting [#161468](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161468).
  - Wait for configured-owner authentication publication before admitting nested runs with a captured plugin generation in [8501893](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/85018936a3383184812792bdc58c98d1b53311dc).
  - Avoid an unnecessary restart when a newer model or credential update supersedes an older hot reload in [b285a4e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b285a4e9d8915c48d3164125ee515f0f9a1a2123).
  - Release Muse native sessions during plugin replacement and disable in [#184941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184941). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [pstarkgit](https://github.com/pstarkgit).
  - Match channel names consistently when binding or unbinding agents in [#184717](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184717), addressing [#184636](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/184636). Thanks to [a-raphie](https://github.com/a-raphie), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Prevent a false session conflict from blocking native token usage settlement in [#184427](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184427). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prevent stale native binding observations from repeatedly blocking cleanup and potentially exhausting memory under request tracing in [7cc1ca6](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7cc1ca64798cf9897b3f27e1751ae2a8e73d462d).
  - Drain usage cleanup during agent deletion and retry failed usage refresh cleanup in [5ac29c6](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5ac29c6c966ea36ca05fe3585fe56520f70bc474) and [6a3b706](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6a3b7067d5e98fa9eb4055eb10e99c85591d352e). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Defer a heartbeat monitor blocked by concurrent agent deletion without requiring a Gateway restart in [8484ee5](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8484ee5268faea844415392eff5a965068d8c207).
  - Reduce blocking deletion-status reads during hook retries while retaining deletion guards in [20d12e8](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/20d12e8a4dce7af751315e08494467d38d14d35f). Thanks to [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Prevent the Node 26 worker abort with file-backed or ignored stdin in [#187516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187516) and its backport [#187553](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187553). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - The migration adds indexed fields for transcript queries and delivery cleanup and scans existing rows during the upgrade. [#169414](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169414). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use the committed role policy after profile lookup for a pending operator turn, so a temporary downgrade restored during lookup does not reject it. A persistent downgrade still restricts the turn. [#169607](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169607). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve unrelated credential updates when Model Setup activation fails, and provide async replacements for deprecated synchronous OAuth SDK methods. [#169633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169633). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show the selected authentication profile or safe configured or environment credential source in `/status` and `session_status`. [#169721](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169721), [issue #82020](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/82020) and [earlier investigation #90689](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/90689). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and reporter [@davidjoshua7692-code](https://github.com/davidjoshua7692-code).
  - Reflect profile role downgrades and deletions in Gateway fallback authorization. [#169799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169799). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Remove the stale error from the reference returned after a successful Claw MCP configuration retry. [#169756](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169756). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Config set, patch and unset dry runs work while the Gateway owns state, and an unchanged offline set no longer creates an absent state database in [#170202](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170202). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Shared-state schema v22 preserves ACP resume data and receipt cursor history through the SQLite migration. The schema work prepares for PostgreSQL but does not enable its runtime use. [PR #170280](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170280), with the storage design in [#169254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169254). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Update preparation checks the intended target before confirmation and handles more supported package and Bun installation paths.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Refresh the Dev Git target when opening the Inbox update card and before confirmation. Opening the card does not start an update. [#158053](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158053). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Keep Doctor preparation and service maintenance within the same explicit update timeout. A service stop already in progress can still finish and be restored. [#166000](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166000). Thanks to [dankarization](https://github.com/dankarization) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Allow valid Dev updates and Mac recovery to proceed when saved receipt hints are missing or stale. An older updater may still need the documented manual route for its first upgrade. [#167459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167459), [reported in #167441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167441). Thanks to [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Supports `install.sh --runtime bun` with a verified pinned runtime and Gateway service integration on supported hosts. A fresh install with `--no-onboard` does not create a service. [#168832](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168832) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restores repair and retirement for interrupted npm updates on FreeBSD when the helper was prepared by an updater containing the fix. Package recovery does not restart or verify the Gateway. [#168775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168775) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserves the cleanup modules and pending cleanup needed for the first upgrade from published stable 2026.10.1 to a package containing these fixes. [#168740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168740) and [#168849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168849) by [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), with thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for independently checking the compatibility mappings in #168740.
  - Keeps the selected update target and candidate checks through concurrent configuration edits, and returns busy plugin lifecycle requests for an explicit retry. [#168882](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168882) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve configured Gateway heap controls and caller heap overrides in captured update-child environments. [#168889](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168889), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and issue reporter [@bluet](https://github.com/bluet).
  - Point failed updates with migrated data to profile-aware repair and make maintenance refusals more informative. [#168905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168905), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@ezimerman](https://github.com/ezimerman) for the incident report and repair follow-up.
  - Avoid retaining unrelated enclosing dependencies during updates, preventing needless copying and failures when those files change. The updater running the update needs this fix. [#168927](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168927), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce repeated reads and hashing of unchanged runtime files during installed-package updates. [#168958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168958), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Report an accepted managed Gateway update handoff as pending instead of a failure. The background helper still determines the final result. [#168993](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168993), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and issue reporter [@sayitfast](https://github.com/sayitfast).
  - Skip self-update when an installation is confirmed to be owned by pacman and direct users to their system package manager. [#169055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169055), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sahilsatralkar](https://github.com/sahilsatralkar), and issue reporter [@Pegorim](https://github.com/Pegorim).
  - Reduce repeated scans of a freshly prepared candidate during uninterrupted POSIX npm updates, with full content verification retained for resumed recovery. [#169075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169075), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep interrupted-update recovery within its elapsed-time budget when the system clock changes. [#169168](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169168), thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Document the first update from a Bun-pinned 2026.9.9 Gateway on macOS npm-global installations, including temporarily switching the service to Node, updating to 2026.10.1, and re-pinning Bun afterward. [Bun guide](/install/bun), [#168901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168901), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject a custom Bun executable whose name is neither `bun` nor `bun.exe` before installation changes, with guidance to rename it or omit `--bun-path`. [#168982](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168982), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update the shared Bun build used by opted-in users, including heap-limit compatibility and Worker lifecycle changes. [#169009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169009), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Provide a runtime-only source build and use the same mode for Team server deployments. [#169039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169039), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Updates and rollback preserve more unrelated settings, staged edits and recovery material instead of overwriting them when a later step fails.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserve the healthy configuration recovery copy when an external edit suspiciously truncates the configuration, so Doctor can restore it. [#152565](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152565), [reported in #152509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/152509). Thanks to [LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and [yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Preserve blocked local plugin settings and enablement during Doctor and update maintenance. The plugin remains blocked until its unsafe path is repaired. [#168111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168111), [reported in #168066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168066). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep a running Linux Gateway responsive when log rollover encounters a deleted temporary mount. Unavailable file logging can still lose records and emits a warning. [#160433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160433), [related report #130807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130807). Thanks to [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino), [WadeOwenWatts](https://github.com/WadeOwenWatts), [Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Prevent classified stopped-Tailscale startup failures from suppressing channels after reconnection. Restore Tailscale and restart the Gateway to recover. [#162266](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162266), [related report #144845](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/144845). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [odrobnik](https://github.com/odrobnik).
  - Restore worker V8 heap ceilings under ordinary Gateway heap flag combinations. Certain V8 flag-control modes remain problematic, including `--exit-on-contradictory-flags` with an explicit heap flag. [#166581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166581), [reports #157575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157575) and [#157630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157630). Thanks to [valkyriweb](https://github.com/valkyriweb), [xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn), [goslingmanagment](https://github.com/goslingmanagment), and [waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault).
  - Keep remote Gateway restart verification within its intended time limit when the system clock changes. [#168087](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168087). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - The latest and other recovery-critical or uncertain captures remain protected. [Update capture cleanup](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168177). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Older installed updaters and updates without a restart keep the previous Doctor inspection ordering. [Doctor inspection timing](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168350). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - `openclaw database verify-preservation` compares sealed original and candidate captures for the supported schema 24-to-25 migration. It requires both manifest digests and does not approve activation. Inspection of vector-backed tables may fail. [Database preservation check](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168321). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Healthy, recorded path-linked plugins can complete state migration during update activation. Unavailable and unrecorded paths remain deferred. [Linked plugin migration](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168190). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Update repair can finish alongside a legitimate active updater without disturbing its ownership. [Concurrent update repair](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168197), [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168000). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@farisoftfry](https://github.com/farisoftfry).
  - When the state database is busy, an installed updater with this fix can skip optional progress entries with a warning while still requiring recovery records. [Update database contention](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168210), [contention report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168072). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna).
  - Incomplete original-state capture warnings identify affected databases and relevant backup errors. An incomplete capture is retained for investigation without a completed recovery manifest. [#169282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169282), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169259). Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
