  - Newly created recovery copies include affected legacy Canvas documents and Memory Core files before migration. Older copies do not gain missing files retroactively. [#169436](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169436). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Backup SQLite discovery uses the current runtime, so affected Bun installations do not need a separate Node executable for this step. [#183476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183476) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Cancelling workspace preparation reports uncertain Git cleanup and retains temporary snapshot artifacts that may help recovery. [#184226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184226) Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
  - Retry temporary Windows package rollback failures and report retained recovery paths when the installed package root is missing ([#185160](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185160), [#187274](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187274)). Thanks to [@liuwqgit](https://github.com/liuwqgit), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and reporter [@syxscott](https://github.com/syxscott).
  - Preserve detected checkout changes during immediate Git rollback and provide manual recovery commands. The check excludes updater staging paths and `dist/control-ui/` ([#185201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185201)). Thanks to [@wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web) and reporter [@syxscott](https://github.com/syxscott).
  - Record accepted native supervised updates in history so earlier migration backups can become eligible for explicit offline cleanup ([#185392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185392)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stop retrying a verified-user refusal during update verification and allow guarded local HTTP readiness when its checks permit ([#185995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185995)). Thanks to [@Yun-0000](https://github.com/Yun-0000) and reporter [@lakemike](https://github.com/lakemike).
  - Retain the selected installed Node runtime and service authority through eligible managed package updates ([#187108](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187108)). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Retain failed Doctor details in update history and advise stopping the Gateway through its owner and running `openclaw update repair` when service inspection is unavailable ([#187574](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187574)). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@patrick-erichsen](https://github.com/patrick-erichsen).
  - Remove eligible current migration snapshots after verified package updates, including when a restoration refusal was recorded after capture ([#187581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187581)). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Repair configuration and state for a verified offline app-owned Gateway. Running app-owned services remain protected, and service management stays with the app. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b5176e33011942035ea43cbdcbd2cae0df944934)
  - Verify migration snapshots using exact filesystem identities during update recovery. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/28df9fe159fc9fb833d25e2c534d5e780f9e4c50)
  - Let offline maintenance and Doctor update flows finish cleanup after a maintenance policy is retired. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/019fdde9a9ba6d9dbf6d1f41733d3e685df67220)
  - Include inspection deadline details in update warnings and advise a verified backup and retry after resolving the failure when Doctor cannot capture the original state. Delegated maintenance has bounded termination after parent loss. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0b5fb78e3a2db38bf1b915a1bb7a8b2797d4867b), thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Recheck native autostart policy after a Gateway service repair attempt. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/215cadc147444a22bf7f2d20963a8c56c961e1c9), thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Let a healthy Gateway finish update restart checks despite a retained RPC authentication warning. Failed health checks and version or build mismatches still receive their respective outcomes. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b391dee01d810309067c18c8c681e2d984758724), thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Integration merge carrying changes to database backup and updates, agent steering, Synology Chat pacing, sessions and the interface. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f800194242f45f162d6dac441782d4bd69a1c3ce)

  </details>

- Update results distinguish a completed core installation from outstanding channel, plugin or restart work. A past success receipt is not proof that the current Gateway is healthy.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Immutable update status shows recorded failure and recovery information and the last activation receipt. The receipt records past verification rather than current Gateway health. [Update status and recovery](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168203). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Status and dry-run reports identify stores and schema differences for a prepared immutable candidate. An unprepared target remains unknown, and matching versions do not establish readiness to activate. [Immutable update inspection](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168295). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Candidate checks, cleanup, activation and Doctor section timings appear in update output and history. Most new parent-owned timings appear on the update after installation. [Update phase timings](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168290). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Results retain runtime preparation, service reconciliation and driver-handoff timings. The handoff can appear during the installing update, while the other timings need the updated driver. [Updater and service timings](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168409). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - A separate helper can inspect and archive a completed receipt blocking an older updater while preserving the receipt. [Completed receipt recovery](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168388), [reported macOS blocker](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168092), [related Linux report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166135). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@k94118](https://github.com/k94118), [@kimjinsuhh](https://github.com/kimjinsuhh), [@GoldenrodScript](https://github.com/GoldenrodScript) and [@neilofneils404](https://github.com/neilofneils404).
  - Remove obsolete warnings for completed plugin migrations and keep package-verification time limits steady when the system clock changes. [#169004](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169004), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@adenzhou1350](https://github.com/adenzhou1350), and issue reporter [@WG-Mojo](https://github.com/WG-Mojo).
  - Settle certain aborted 2026.9.8 recovery records using a newer CLI, with a warning that legacy contents could not be re-verified. Repair reports failure when an older record cannot be settled. [#169027](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169027), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@chedong](https://github.com/chedong) for the report and investigation.
  - Preserve a confirmed successful update result if publishing the result fails, and warn about configured channel accounts still stopped after repair. [#169037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169037), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@kvnloo](https://github.com/kvnloo), and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Settle eligible stale package recovery records and archive completed receipts before the next update. Dry runs report the planned action without changing the retained files. [#169038](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169038), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@mkdelta221](https://github.com/mkdelta221), [@waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault), [@clawdgawd](https://github.com/clawdgawd), [@yoyo837](https://github.com/yoyo837), [@lishewen](https://github.com/lishewen), [@nukk-pain](https://github.com/nukk-pain), [@jullianatolentino](https://github.com/jullianatolentino), [@joshpetras](https://github.com/joshpetras), and [@creanet-64](https://github.com/creanet-64) for the reports and recovery follow-ups.
  - Update failures explain dependency links that reach OpenClaw-owned files and show the observed Gateway service status when readiness cannot be verified. See the [runtime retention guidance](/cli/update#runtime-retention-failures). [#169280](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169280). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Optional candidate configuration-read warnings include the reason for failure while preserving the existing service definition. [#169284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169284), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169261). Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Doctor avoids misleading backup warnings for several bundled migrations and gives guidance when a plugin's migration coverage is unknown. See [plugin migration recovery coverage](/cli/doctor/state-migrations#plugin-migration-recovery-coverage). [#169295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169295). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Complete an update with channel warnings only after service, version, build and readiness checks pass. The warning does not repair the channel connection. [#172494](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172494), reported in [#172391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172391). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@sbc2016](https://github.com/sbc2016).
  - An optional shell completion refresh that times out can leave an otherwise successful update successful once its work and cleanup have settled. The warning remains, and `openclaw completion --write-state` refreshes completions manually. [#172502](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172502), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor preserves deferred legacy channel settings while saving core repairs during an update. [#172791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172791), thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Eligible older Linux Gateway service files can be regenerated with private permissions during an upgrade. Doctor repair still requires approval. [#172790](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172790), thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@wei887-os](https://github.com/wei887-os) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/152533).
  - Update warnings identify a missing pacman or FreeBSD pkg executable instead of suggesting a database repair for a package manager that is unavailable. [#173293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173293), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), and [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
  - A verified core update can continue with a warning when follow-up plugin checks cannot finish. Run `openclaw update repair` to retry them, stopping the Gateway first if repair requests exclusive access. Concrete readiness failures and uncertain process cleanup still block completion. [#173341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173341), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@yoyo837](https://github.com/yoyo837) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171096).
  - An update to the same installation can move past an unhealthy running Gateway without spending the replacement's startup time waiting for it. The replacement still has to pass its checks. [#173358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173358), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Successful update reports remove settled plugin migration warnings and repeated advisories, and give clearer Doctor guidance. [#173395](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173395), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi), and [@bhedanikhilkumar-code](https://github.com/bhedanikhilkumar-code).
  - Global package updates with `--no-restart` can report a successful installation with startup advisories when the Gateway restarts itself after package replacement. The flag skips the updater's managed restart but does not prevent that automatic restart. Check update or Gateway status after startup and keep recovery backups until verification completes. [#173409](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173409), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@mwbroxson](https://github.com/mwbroxson) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171311).

  </details>

- Supported update recovery handles more temporary contention, abandoned work and interrupted attempts without blindly repeating uncertain changes. Older broken updaters may still need manual recovery.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Slow database checks no longer falsely exhaust systemd service-inspection time during Linux Doctor or update repair. The checks can still make the overall command slow. [Linux maintenance inspection](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168161), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168031). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@Tosko4](https://github.com/Tosko4).
  - Temporary progress-output congestion no longer aborts database metadata inventory in an updater containing the fix. An older updater that fails before activation cannot acquire this fix during that attempt. [Update progress output](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168493), [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161042). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@creanet-64](https://github.com/creanet-64).
  - Immutable update readiness checks use the intended restart deadline. [Update readiness deadline](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168496). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Immutable Gateway activation is verified only after a valid session listing succeeds. An empty list is valid. [Activation session check](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168561). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Interim Team managed updates can verify migration after Doctor retires the optional Workshop proposal table. [Managed update verification](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168577). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update-history writes move their initial approval outside the database writer lock, reducing one source of contention with scheduled tasks and cleanup. Final approval still holds the lock. [#169283](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169283), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169260). Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
  - Windows short-name paths and differently cased paths on case-insensitive volumes retain the updater's original worker code during replacement and allow abandoned runtime copies to be cleaned up. [#169292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169292). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Windows SQLite recovery helpers can pass their own temporary-directory checks. This does not expand the platform support of individual updater commands. [#169397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169397). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A separately installed helper can settle an eligible unfinished publication after device numbers change, keeping the installed package and recovery evidence. Follow the [remount recovery guide](/install/updating#recover-an-unfinished-publication-after-a-remount) for the required checks and command. [#169410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169410), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169050). Thanks [@patrickschiller](https://github.com/patrickschiller) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Git updates clean up their temporary worktree when progress reporting fails and retain the original update error alongside cleanup diagnostics. [#169454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169454), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169446). Thanks [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).
  - Update and Doctor checks accept an explicitly empty agent roster and reject unsafe Canvas folder links before collecting migration files. [#169516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169516). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - On resolved npm-global installations, the updater avoids retaining unrelated global packages while keeping the dependencies it reaches. The first upgrade from an older updater still uses that updater's behavior. [#169551](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169551), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169373), [related investigation](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169521). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen) and [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant).
  - Doctor can continue an update after a later process-inspection failure when it has already verified the updater. An initially unverified relationship still stops continuation with a clearer reason. [#169563](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169563). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  Several of these update fixes depend on the updater already installed when an update starts. Installing a corrected candidate does not change an older updater's earlier steps.

  - Restart a revalidated, preserved Gateway service after a service refresh refuses before changing its definition, while reporting the refusal. [#169658](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169658). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Allow authorized update-time Doctor to continue under a verified updater when higher supervisors are unreadable, and retain a spawned command's identity if binding fails. Existing anonymous reservations remain unresolved. [#169665](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169665). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use the candidate release's native Doctor migration for supported managed Team schema upgrades. Unknown future schemas and downgrades remain blocked. [#169752](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169752). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Import deferred ACP session metadata after the legacy index has been archived and its plugin becomes available, without restoring metadata into closed or replaced sessions. [#169759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169759). Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).
  - Let Doctor attempt Gateway recovery after a transient resource-close failure succeeds on retry, while reporting the cleanup error. Unsettled cleanup still blocks restart. [#169783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169783). Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).
  - Preserve a verified healthy migrated Gateway and its newer writes during managed update recovery. [#169841](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169841). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep supported older Node updaters from falsely losing ownership across the heartbeat clock transition and rolling back after activation. [#169915](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169915). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Clear a settled Doctor command reservation when recording the command fails before it starts, preventing a false outstanding-process record from blocking later recovery. [#169936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169936). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Complete deferred Doctor inspections after Gateway readiness using the updated installation, and report incomplete checks. [#169940](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169940), building on [#168350](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168350). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - New or repaired pnpm Gateway services use a stable project path, and nested pnpm installations can be recognized for updates. Existing version-pinned service commands may need repair. See [#170003](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170003) and [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169059). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@xilopaint](https://github.com/xilopaint) and [@k-sekula](https://github.com/k-sekula).
  - Doctor and update recovery pause scheduled database checkpoints during offline maintenance to avoid unstable recovery captures in [#170022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170022). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update repair can settle eligible journals after filesystem device numbers change, including a verified newer manual installation. Older updaters may need the documented recovery helper first. See [#170046](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170046), [#169050](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169050) and [#169352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169352). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@patrickschiller](https://github.com/patrickschiller) and [@SergeiAug](https://github.com/SergeiAug).
  - Doctor checks for unsupported retired files before changing the database, preserving the intermediate upgrade route when it refuses repair. See [#170051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170051) and [#169918](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169918). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@bautrey](https://github.com/bautrey).
  - Native updates using an updater with the fix keep pnpm 12 launchers pointed at the activated package after cleanup. An already broken launcher needs manual repair. See [#170131](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170131) and [#169244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169244). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant) and [@k-sekula](https://github.com/k-sekula).
  - Deferred standalone update repair exits with code 2 and gives a profile-aware managed Gateway stop command. An inherited update finalizer continues to exit 0 with a warning. See [#170139](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170139) and [#169462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169462). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@jjgao](https://github.com/jjgao).
  - Doctor can find eligible abandoned updater runtimes that leave an extra recovery-database link and block npm updates. See [#170170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170170) and [#169463](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169463). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sunnyshu0925](https://github.com/sunnyshu0925) and [@warhangel](https://github.com/warhangel).
  - Reclaim abandoned updater runtimes in Linux systemd user sessions when the standard login processes have protected working directories. Live holders and other unresolved processes still prevent cleanup. [#171488](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171488) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Thanks to [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for [reporting the issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171410).
  - Release the shared database writer when updater commit admission is delayed, giving scheduled work and cleanup a chance to proceed. The installed updater and its matching worker need this fix. [#171493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171493) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Thanks to [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for the reports and investigation in [#169260](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169260) and [#171411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171411), and for the earlier partial repair.
  - Restore the previous package and databases when candidate Doctor fails before database writes. Actual unattributed writes still prevent automatic restoration. [#172151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172151). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Avoid the SQLite inspection refusal caused by active agent writes during an update. Existing installed drivers may still warn during original-state capture. [#172196](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172196). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Keep a verified update installed when Doctor cannot inspect process ancestry, with repair guidance for the pending maintenance. [#172247](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172247), reported in [#172213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172213). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@homeworld520](https://github.com/homeworld520).
  - Repair the agent database migration for updates from 2026.10.5 beta.2 and beta.3 to a main-based build. [#172249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172249). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Fetch the selected upstream for dev-channel Git updates and stop safely when it is excluded or invalid. [#172199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172199). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Document a manual recovery path for candidate snapshot cleanup failures and the sanitized nested cause to include in a report. [#172171](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172171), related to [#172104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172104). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@warezio](https://github.com/warezio).

  </details>

- Doctor avoids more repeated inspections and false warnings while keeping real faults and unfinished repairs visible.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Avoid repeated full database scans during admitted read-only Doctor maintenance without a cached writer. [#169745](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169745). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid repeated state scans during managed Doctor repair continuation checks. [#169905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169905). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Routine configuration reads no longer check the deferred plugin migration ledger. Doctor and managed updates retain pending repair information. [PR #170230](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170230). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor and session decoding avoid loading provider transports just to clean up transcript checkpoints. [#170585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170585). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Common `openclaw config get`, `set` and `validate` paths avoid unnecessary synchronous plugin-state snapshots and schema preparation. [#170685](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170685). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce long Doctor repair delays during plugin backup capture by reusing checks of an unchanged database. [#170934](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170934), [report #170773](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170773), related [#169745](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169745). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@rozenpl](https://github.com/rozenpl), and [@jb510](https://github.com/jb510).
  - Reuse database admission checks across workers and reopened connections for the same SQLite file. [#167739](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167739). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce repeated SQLite freshness checks and transaction work during Gateway chat turns. [#167965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167965). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce repeated chat-turn and idle pairing-notification database work, and require offline custody for external-supervisor ownership claims. [#168002](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168002). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The related recurring crash report remains open. [SQLite lock protection](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168274), [related crash report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167671). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@3n8](https://github.com/3n8).
  - Gateway startup and later worker opens avoid repeated full database scans after index repair. [Database checks after index repair](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168517), [related startup investigation](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168468). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@TommyLei666](https://github.com/TommyLei666).
  - Long-running Gateways release completed-run history retained by retired model generations. [Model generation memory retention](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168584). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Node's default compiler settings are restored. Some macOS watcher and certificate stalls remain. [Process shutdown](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168587). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Backup outcome recording remains best-effort and does not replay an uncertain Gateway write locally. Offline device approval requires exclusive state ownership. [Backup records and device approval](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168466). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor no longer shows node-hosting warnings for local-only Gateways that have not configured node hosting. The related Windows connection report concerns a separate issue. [Node-hosting diagnostics](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168218), [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164396). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@absol89](https://github.com/absol89).
  - Vault setup gives the specific correction for invalid secret IDs, including the required path and field format. [Vault setup errors](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168272). Thanks [@nocodet888-arch](https://github.com/nocodet888-arch) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Adds a filesystem fallback for SQLite snapshot publication on Termux. [#168629](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168629) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [@yxshee](https://github.com/yxshee) for reporting the issue.
  - Speeds up Doctor configuration checks for Telegram and WhatsApp by reading their defaults without loading the full channel runtimes. [#168869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168869) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stop unchanged one-shot cron timestamps from triggering redundant Doctor recovery copies. Real repairs still keep their protected backups. [#168938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168938), thanks [@harshitgupta31415](https://github.com/harshitgupta31415) and issue reporter [@wg-mojo](https://github.com/wg-mojo).
  - Avoid an unrelated system Node warning when a different selected Gateway Node passes its check, and identify the system executable when its SQLite check fails. [#169007](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169007), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Yun-0000](https://github.com/Yun-0000), and issue reporter [@amueller80](https://github.com/amueller80).
  - Release Doctor's private plugin metadata captures after inspection ([01ead786](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/01ead786dc19b1cf9921dfcf90917206d876dfc1)).
  - Keep Doctor plugin repair and diagnostic callbacks available through later checks and finalization ([310bce6a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/310bce6a751791a9f61ce7a3bfcb3fd79653642b)).

  </details>

- Supported package and source updates retain their launcher and runtime through more cleanup and handoff failures. Several fixes must already be in the installed updater to protect the update attempt.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Older updaters can validate a candidate without rejecting it solely for Doctor bookkeeping changes in [#170195](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170195). The separate startup hang in [#169953](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169953) remains unresolved. Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@maestrokling](https://github.com/maestrokling) and [@frxiaobei](https://github.com/frxiaobei).
  - Managed updates and Doctor retry when another writer releases its lock during the reclaim check in [#170196](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170196). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update repair prepares the target configuration before selecting and running a repair model, while keeping temporary repair overrides local to that run. [PR #170276](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170276). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Managed updates record a refusal and restore the Gateway when a requester's access is revoked while its channel is disabled. [PR #170365](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170365). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Large configuration responses no longer truncate the candidate read that refreshes eligible Gateway service definitions. If that optional read still fails, wait for the update to finish and run `openclaw gateway install --force` with the same `--profile` or `--dev` option. [PR #170373](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170373), fixing [#170331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170331). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for reporting and investigation, and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for advice.
  - Run eligible Linux managed update handoffs in a separate user service, outside the invoking agent and Gateway lifetime and the Gateway unit's memory limit. [#187215](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187215). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and reporter [@masterdevill](https://github.com/masterdevill).
  - Bun updates release completed SQLite snapshot locks before the managed update handoff. [#170519](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170519). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Package repair can settle an untouched preparation after a remount once it verifies the installed package and launchers. It refuses changed live content or unfinished publication. [#170676](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170676). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the fix and [git-larry-roots](https://github.com/git-larry-roots) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170443).
  - Successful snapshot-backed state reads no longer fail solely because best-effort temporary cleanup is busy. The warning remains and cleanup can happen later. [#170605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170605). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Allow responsive, busy Gateways to settle during managed updates and rollback, while retaining readiness and identity checks ([#170696](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170696)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Removing a Linux Gateway or node service reloads systemd so it recognizes the removed unit. If that reload fails after removal, run `systemctl --user daemon-reload`; repeating uninstall will not retry it. [#173048](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173048), thanks [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127359).
  - Prepared source updaters retain database backup helpers before replacing the runtime, avoiding a missing-module failure when creating rollback snapshots. [#173587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173587), thanks [@vishnusenthil-16](https://github.com/vishnusenthil-16) and [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/173754).
  - Update restart preparation waits for a verified new Gateway to publish ownership before treating its startup as a failure. [#173588](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173588), thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Managed Gateway and node service changes check runtime pins without repeatedly copying the full state database. [#172500](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172500), thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Source updates retain the running updater's modules after activation, avoiding missing-module errors during later update steps. [#174867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174867). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@vishnusenthil-16](https://github.com/vishnusenthil-16).
  - After an authentication rejection, update verification can use local readiness and runtime identity checks and record a warning. [#175735](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175735). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@sesgoe](https://github.com/sesgoe).
  - Update repair reports available Gateway ownership details and the stalled inspection phase when a service check times out. [#177560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177560). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@sdamian](https://github.com/sdamian).
  - A fresh Doctor process uses the update caller's finite step allowance when no explicit timeout is supplied. [#173862](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173862). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@jsf9789](https://github.com/jsf9789).
  - Package updates with `--no-restart` check the expected running build as well as the version before reporting the Gateway as verified. [#174649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174649). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Setup reports a failed installed Gateway restart and shows recovery commands instead of reporting success. [#174767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174767). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223).
  - Gateway service installation no longer lets the retired `CLAWDBOT_GATEWAY_PASSWORD` variable suppress token setup. Operators relying on it need a supported password or token. [#175416](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175416). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh provider setup retains the selected model's metadata without inadvertently restricting the model picker to that model. Existing explicit restrictions still apply. [#175945](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175945). Thanks [@matthematics1137](https://github.com/matthematics1137), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@jb-dev1](https://github.com/jb-dev1).
  - Interrupted update recovery accepts matching symlink launchers with different recorded modes while retaining the other integrity checks. An installation already blocked on an older updater needs a recovery helper containing this fix. [Recovery guidance](/install/updating#recover-an-unfinished-publication-after-a-remount) · [#177059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177059). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@BodegaClaw](https://github.com/BodegaClaw).
  - Backport runtime and release-tooling repairs ([#178169](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178169)). The source changes are [#172661](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172661), [#170297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170297), [#172689](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172689), [#172745](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172745), [#172668](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172668), [#172705](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172705), [#173615](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173615), [#173656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173656), [#173465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173465), [#173781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173781), [#173813](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173813), [#174718](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174718) and [#174812](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174812). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Supported backups and rollback can restore more older archives and retain eligible recovery copies. Restoring older code alone does not roll back migrated data; use the matching backup when needed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - An updater containing the snapshot fix avoids falsely reporting an unchanged agent database as changed when an exited writer left SQLite sidecars. Later database changes can still prevent restoration. See [rollback and recovery](/install/updating/rollback-and-recovery). [PR #170351](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170351), reported in [#170335](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170335). Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Supported older global and agent snapshots can be verified and restored. Newer, inconsistent or wrong-owner snapshots remain refused. [#170492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170492). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the fix and [davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170244).
  - Package updates retain unsealed database snapshots for manual recovery after successful activation. The updater performing the update must contain this fix. [#170523](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170523). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the fix and [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170335).
  - Identify unsafe recovery permissions and changed handoff identity in newly generated update reports from an updated installed updater ([#170787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170787), [#170076](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170076)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@zrzavywa](https://github.com/zrzavywa), and [@niriakot](https://github.com/niriakot).
  - Preserve verified Gateway state ownership after sleep or backward clock changes, and permit an authorized recovery restart when state access is fenced ([#170790](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170790), [#169829](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169829), [#170525](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170525), [#170597](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170597)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen), [@AnvinaLI](https://github.com/AnvinaLI), [@sgh6688](https://github.com/sgh6688), [@waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault), [@andersgreg](https://github.com/andersgreg), and [@shawnholt](https://github.com/shawnholt).
  - Private update checks help preserve database compatibility if an upgrade from 2026.9.2 with native plugins is refused. Databases already migrated by an earlier failed update still need a matching pre-update backup or a compatible newer package. [#171015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171015), [report #170939](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170939), related [#153657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153657), [#158414](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158414) and [#168340](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168340). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@bamorem](https://github.com/bamorem).
  - Update status checks current files and session history before showing old migration warnings, so resolved findings can disappear while uncertain ones remain. [#177096](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177096). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ScientificProgrammer](https://github.com/ScientificProgrammer), [@jj5638521](https://github.com/jj5638521) and [@ourcoms2018](https://github.com/ourcoms2018).
  - Update helpers with an absolute `TMPDIR` can use that volume for disposable plugin captures and SQLite inspection snapshots. Durable files and backups still need space on their own volumes. [#178009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178009). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
  - Update rehearsal omits the false loopback-only onboarding warning caused by its temporary configuration, while keeping genuine Doctor warnings. [#178155](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178155). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
  - Check the Gateway's Node version before staging an incompatible registry package and explain how to update a pinned service runtime ([#180769](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180769)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sglion777](https://github.com/sglion777), [@Almost-Certified-ops](https://github.com/Almost-Certified-ops), [@billpang1-sketch](https://github.com/billpang1-sketch), [@rayluo88](https://github.com/rayluo88) and [@jcheng510](https://github.com/jcheng510).
  - Repair changed artifacts in managed source installations and respect a later native autostart disable during restoration ([#180356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180356)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Keep snapshots marked for manual database recovery when Doctor refuses automatic restoration after capture. The installed updater must contain the fix to protect its own cleanup ([#180010](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180010), [#170335](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170335), [#170523](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170523)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
  - Allow update snapshots with verified stale owned SQLite search indexes so Doctor or Gateway startup can rebuild them. Activation backup tolerance requires an installed updater with the fix ([#181206](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181206)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@ndakota79](https://github.com/ndakota79).
  - Use the installation's own pnpm 10 shim and global bin path during update preflight. The installed updater must already contain the repair ([#181456](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181456)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@rayluo88](https://github.com/rayluo88).
  - Shut down a delegated update or Doctor worker after its executor dies, allowing a later command or Gateway start to reclaim the abandoned maintenance state. Live maintenance refusals also identify the holder and point to update status ([#181695](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181695)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@PonySlayer28](https://github.com/PonySlayer28).
  - Bound Doctor media-repair scans and roll back a rewrite when maintenance access is lost. [#171655](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171655) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Backup interruption also reaches Doctor's pre-migration recovery backup. [#171947](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171947) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Eligible Doctor migrations checkpoint their declared tables after checking source integrity. Other repairs and unsupported migration paths retain full-backup fallbacks. [#172671](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172671), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Backup verification and restore accept valid older hardlink targets when they resolve unambiguously. Missing or ambiguous targets remain rejected. [#173070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173070), thanks [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@mgonto](https://github.com/mgonto) for the [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/89257).
  - SQLite backup listing recognizes Git worktrees and points operators to Git backup history. [#176656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176656). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Repeated starts of the same official Docker container reuse a successful recovery capture while Doctor continues its repairs. A recreated container, new activation or missing capture or marker needs another initial capture. [#173711](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173711). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@8bitsforge](https://github.com/8bitsforge).
  - Reject non-decimal external backup byte counts instead of silently converting them ([#178585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178585), [#178491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/178491)). Thanks [@YangManBOBO](https://github.com/YangManBOBO) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Backport backup approval-policy preservation, persistent Linux Gateway pairing, database refusal recovery and macOS Gateway status and repair ([#181464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181464)). The related source changes are [#164297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164297), [#158908](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158908), [#181389](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181389), [#175987](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175987), [#163258](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163258) and [#179320](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179320). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@dor-sr](https://github.com/dor-sr), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@shanselman](https://github.com/shanselman).

  </details>

- Update checks identify more unsafe replacement and permission cases before changes begin. Avoid changing installation files during an update; some compound maintenance-and-start commands remain outside live-artifact protection.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Source builds avoid a missing-file race between Control UI publication and runtime compatibility scanning. [#170455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170455). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Compiled entries in source checkouts use the existing module compile cache on repeated starts. [#170514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170514). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the fix and [LouisGameDev](https://github.com/LouisGameDev) for the [earlier report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/73037).
  - Keep core update and Doctor recovery moving when optional dreaming schedule inspection fails. Existing jobs are retained and a warning directs users to retry the pending repair with `openclaw doctor --fix`. Successful cron reads also survive failure to remove a temporary snapshot ([#170754](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170754), [#147956](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/147956)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80), and [@anhnt3](https://github.com/anhnt3).
  - Allow standalone Doctor to repair the matching account's state when an app-owned Gateway is verified offline. Restart and update remain with OpenClaw.app ([#170768](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170768), [#170724](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170724)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@shanselman](https://github.com/shanselman).
  - Give force-killed helper processes more time to exit before update repair or Doctor reports uncertain cleanup ([#170779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170779)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid misleading checkpoint failure warnings while another process holds expected offline maintenance ownership ([#170783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170783)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Explain recovery after a failed Gateway restart preparation or failed embeddings cleanup. The related reports provide context for the broader cleanup ([#170826](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170826), [#40226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/40226), [#58789](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/58789), [#82712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/82712), [#103736](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/103736), [#112336](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/112336), [#137417](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137417)). A failed restart preparation can require reapplying configuration or a manual restart. Failed embeddings cleanup blocks the affected agent and provider scope until restart. Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Continue update state inspection when a progress message temporarily cannot be written. The affected progress message may be omitted. [#170955](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170955), related [#168493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168493) and [#170978](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170978) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let migrated update processes exit normally after finalization. [#170978](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170978) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prepare valid configuration before Doctor registers context-engine plugins that read it. [#170984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170984), [report #170715](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170715) and [#170689](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170689). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@sasan1200](https://github.com/sasan1200).
  - Use one explicit `openclaw update --timeout` budget across update and recovery phases. Recovery may remain pending while writers settle. [#171077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171077), [report #170666](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170666). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini).
  - Refuse a package replacement when service inspection is unavailable and a live Gateway uses the target installation. On Windows, a live Gateway with an unidentified package also prevents replacement. Report a failed maintenance resume. [#171101](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171101), [reports #170774](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170774) and [#171036](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171036). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), reporter [@homeworld520](https://github.com/homeworld520) and source-audit reporter [@Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot).
  - Avoid false plaintext warnings for environment-backed channel tokens after Doctor repairs. Literal tokens continue to receive the warning. [#171116](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171116), [report #171022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171022). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@exGeni](https://github.com/exGeni).
  - Gateway discovery excludes recognized single-command maintenance operations. A compound shell command that runs maintenance and then starts a Gateway may also be excluded, which can weaken live-artifact protection. [#170462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170462). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- Optional maintenance can finish after an updated Gateway is ready, with pending repairs kept in the report. Unattended startup failures do not automatically start a repair agent.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Skip a second npm install when the updated updater receives an unsafe-schema refusal, preserving the original recovery guidance. Older running updaters still need the [manual Windows recovery path](/platforms/windows#updating-from-202694) for the reported 2026.9.4 upgrade. [#171164](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171164), [report #171004](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171004). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@TommyLei666](https://github.com/TommyLei666).
  - Failed post-update verification records available child failure details, and repair and cleanup explain when the Gateway must be stopped first. Cleanup preview remains available while it is running. [#171252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171252), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [yoyo837](https://github.com/yoyo837) for [reporting](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171096).
  - SQLite backups use the captured image for engine detection, avoiding a separate live probe during ongoing writes. [#171295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171295), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Adopted update candidates defer eligible optional Doctor checks until after readiness. Required repairs remain before startup, and unusable selectors fall back to inline lint. [#171374](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171374), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Bundled Control UI assets build consistently across checkout locations, removing a known cause of updater preflight rejection. [#171398](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171398), thanks to [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Update reports retain optional Doctor information and warnings, and deferred-check advice includes `openclaw doctor --lint --all --severity-min info`. Readiness errors remain errors. [#171420](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171420), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Require an explicit action to start repair after unattended update or foreground Gateway startup failures. [#171677](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171677) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks to [mwbroxson](https://github.com/mwbroxson) for [reporting the issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171191).
  - Resume update progress after temporary Doctor maintenance. [#171454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171454) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Thanks to [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for [reporting the issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171409).
  - Name a failed candidate snapshot check in the update headline and retry guidance. [#171457](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171457) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Thanks to [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for [reporting the issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171408).
  - Keep the `openclaw gateway restart` reminder when an update with `--no-restart` changes both core and plugins. [#171687](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171687) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let managed updates wait through temporary database maintenance and retain recovery permission if that wait expires. Actual data-risk refusals still block recovery. [#171699](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171699) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks to [fdovarela](https://github.com/fdovarela) for [reporting the issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171376).
  - Online schema checks avoid a full staging copy for eligible databases. Recovery and incomplete WAL cases can still need a snapshot. [#171984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171984) by [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Unsupported SQLite NOCOW repair stops early with a specific reason, leaving the original store in place. The [reported host issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171933) is separate from the [Docker image prerequisites](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171930). [#171991](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171991) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to reporter [kitiyeung](https://github.com/kitiyeung) and [skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223) for the preflight suggestion.
  - Update repair workers send the updater a bounded reason when a worker exception stops the repair. [#171781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171781), addressing [#169999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169999). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Doctor can finish checks and admitted repairs when a child process closes its input early. If Doctor's own output closes, it exits with status 141 after admitted repairs and Gateway restoration. [#171780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171780) follows the narrower [Codex probe fix](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171248). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [dtump](https://github.com/dtump) for the [original report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171169), [joshpetras](https://github.com/joshpetras) for ARM64 corroboration, and [cbhawthorne84](https://github.com/cbhawthorne84) for the [earlier maintenance report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156712).
  - Setup assistant diagnosis uses read-only Doctor inspection. Run `openclaw doctor --fix` on the Gateway host when you want repairs. [#171960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171960), with thanks to co-author [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - A verified completed installation can settle its unfinished update record and allow the next update to proceed. Other unfinished states can still require `openclaw update repair`. [#171993](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171993), reported in [#171955](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171955). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [teetee2tone](https://github.com/teetee2tone).
  - Native updates can continue when a short Doctor command has already completed successfully instead of failing on the observed process-tracking race. [#172132](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172132) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Installation and Doctor errors give more relevant next steps. Review single-file repairs carefully when configuration includes other files, because an affected repair can revert included settings.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Recognize the Dutch Scheduled Task access-denied message and show the existing Windows elevation or manual-update guidance. An older installed updater still needs the elevated or manual first update ([#178219](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178219), [#170956](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170956)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@Rene-F](https://github.com/Rene-F) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Return a structured JSON error for an invalid update repair channel without changing the configured channel ([#180822](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180822)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@leon-sk668](https://github.com/leon-sk668).
  - Escape invalid update-channel values in human and JSON diagnostics so control characters cannot create misleading terminal lines ([#181298](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181298)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@TUARAN](https://github.com/TUARAN).
  - Preserve explicitly empty arguments and their positions when inspecting Windows scheduled tasks and systemd services ([#181213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181213)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@notlakshitha](https://github.com/notlakshitha) and [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80).
  - Blocked update messages distinguish retained installation custody from an updater that is still running and suggest status and repair commands. [#182774](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182774) Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Source updates on Node and the pinned Bun runtime retain the imports needed during replacement and cleanup. [#183429](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183429) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Queued service maintenance commands keep their elapsed-time budgets when the system clock changes. [#183104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183104) Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Candidate update admission and Doctor recovery workers keep JSON output readable with debug logging enabled. Candidate admission requires a compatible updater. [#182850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182850) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor no longer suggests an ineffective Skill Workshop grant for an agent that deliberately denies the tool. It still offers a hint for a profile-only exclusion. [#171314](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171314), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini) for [reporting](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171274).
  - Repair missing session columns before opening affected agents. If a cold read-only access reports that repair is needed, restart the Gateway or run `openclaw doctor --fix`. [#171634](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171634) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor advises retrying after SQLite snapshot contention instead of suggesting a backup restore for that condition. It still refuses repair when readiness cannot be verified. [#176779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176779). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@WG-Mojo](https://github.com/WG-Mojo).
  - In the demonstrated single-file configuration, Doctor can back up and remove empty retired plugin install metadata. If your configuration uses includes, this recovery may revert unrelated settings. [#173851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173851). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@fengfan201314-eng](https://github.com/fengfan201314-eng).
  - Start cancelling standalone Doctor's original-state capture after ten minutes and show Gateway status and start guidance if restoration fails. Settlement after cancellation can take longer ([#181941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181941)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@kenrohde](https://github.com/kenrohde) and [@kqtxmaster-boop](https://github.com/kqtxmaster-boop).
  - Provider registration keeps a default OAuth profile repair when explicit repairs are also declared. [#183183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183183) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - If a terminal hangup interrupts a repair, Doctor attempts to restore the managed Gateway it stopped. [#182901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182901) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Null-Bug-Null](https://github.com/Null-Bug-Null) for the related report.
  - Doctor can normalize older transcript content after reopening a database in [#170126](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170126). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Git backup refresh and pruning preserve unrelated files and staged changes. Opt-in secret exclusions cover identified bundled-plugin credentials, not every possible secret or existing Git history; restoring a redacted backup may require signing in again.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Apply `--exclude-secrets` to PostgreSQL Git exports while retaining the schema needed for restore. [#174698](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174698). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Omit identified bundled-plugin credentials from redacted SQLite and PostgreSQL Git backups while preserving unrelated plugin state. Readable Discord thread bindings retain routing fields but omit their webhook token. [#186953](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186953). Thanks [@art22s](https://github.com/art22s), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna).
  - Recognize SQLite databases by header for managed snapshots and unmanaged-copy warnings. [#148857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148857), [reported in #148796](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/148796). Thanks to [LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [migelizaga-rgb](https://github.com/migelizaga-rgb).
  - Preserve unrelated files and staging in Git backups, and support verification and restoration of historical refs. Declared artifact collisions and corrupt backups still stop the operation. [#162706](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162706). Thanks to [oshaughnessy-junior](https://github.com/oshaughnessy-junior), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - When SQLite backup listing is pointed at a Git backup repository, explain the mismatch and suggest the Git history command. [#167935](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167935), [reported in #167934](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167934). Thanks to [PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Recovery refuses an outdated session-data location after its store is replaced, rather than continuing against the wrong data. Check uncertain writes before retrying.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Refresh participant lists and membership facts immediately after session repair. [#171481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171481) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Replay committed pending approvals without waiting for unrelated writes when opening an approval-enabled session. [#171605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171605) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Allow state reads to resume after a resource cleanup failure while a safe cleanup retry remains possible. Native retirement failures and protected replacement cases continue to block reads. [#171709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171709) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Thanks to [residentb2b](https://github.com/residentb2b) for the [report and diagnosis](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162085), [homeworld520](https://github.com/homeworld520) for the detailed reproduction, and [sunny251008-debug](https://github.com/sunny251008-debug) for the affected-version report.
  - An interrupted main-session task remains eligible for another startup recovery if shutdown stops its accepted continuation before execution. The existing three-attempt limit and exhaustion notice still apply. [#172023](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172023), reported in [#172000](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172000). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jeanmonet](https://github.com/jeanmonet).
  - Keep a returned stale SQLite iterator from resetting a newer execution of the same statement ([697e5bc1](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/697e5bc126a32cbd2ab704938ade75e5d34ebc6d)).
  - Allow the existing bounded wait to handle temporary schema contention during initial primary state database creation ([fa23864b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/fa23864b643566823648ef8060ed5ba5e2442a40)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Coordinate transcript reconciliation with concurrent state writes when session metadata is read. [Merge commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/c0a235bbbb25df3938ffd2c1d13984e231de4557)
  - Accept session context through an alias of the same physical database while continuing to reject a different copied store. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/cba73231243d85ff0e9e34d7b3c35cd869b0430b), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prepare runtime settings before resolving where a cold transcript writer saves a conversation turn. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/accded4b85c7075b196475cfb3db95505115a1f4)
  - Preserve profile access when an unrelated feature initializes its database schema. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/c445da4029e17aca9bf1288c08389dc35698d634), thanks to [joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
  - Prevent settled session deletions from being invalidated when their SQLite worker exits cleanly. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3d9e3fb92051d8e5dc248b226599432aad8ccc26)
  - Publish copied participant data when repairing a session view. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4aa62334122ed29f605f9820c4604539b3ccee77)
  - An admitted database operation can finish on its open handle if the path is replaced. Later admission refuses the replacement, and permission drift is repaired on the next open or explicit maintenance. [#169310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169310). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep a refused stale database creation from disrupting another agent's healthy reader during session and subagent requests. [#169895](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169895). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Queued SQLite work waits briefly for temporary schema ownership while retaining its original order in [#170135](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170135). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The listener opens for liveness checks before session initialization finishes in [#170151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170151). Readiness continues to wait for initialization. Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject first-send session creation if the prepared session database was replaced before confirmation ([#170716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170716)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An existing session-store handle stays with its admitted database if an alias changes, while a fresh lookup resolves the new target. [#171375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171375), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Memory-backed session callers receive publication errors after committed writes instead of false success. The data remains saved, and committed trajectory batches are not appended again on a later flush. [#171419](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171419), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject stale session ownership when a native session row changes during agent setup ([e338bef1](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e338bef1348d7c9842242be657b3f595169d9127)).
  - Avoid false changed-during-read errors when session generation tracking initializes during an ordered read ([3a9aef66](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3a9aef6652a496192c3a98e046ec5bf961128559)).
  - Accept turns for the canonical session owner in shared stores and preserve pending-input failures ([f1fbf170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f1fbf170dea3c0c631031e477dc61dc06627fba1)).
  - Retry state lock creation once if its directory disappears during a concurrent handoff ([5f925029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5f925029e0800504fc04dd41c0cd297d37636c0f)).

  </details>

- Affected saved conversations retain more attachments and preferences through restore and refresh. Previously lost attachment identity or deleted choices cannot be reconstructed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Reduce writer memory used by large transcript appends and include payload size in slow transaction warnings. [#171540](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171540) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep new internal runtime continuations out of visible user history while retaining them for model replay. Existing stored markers are not rewritten. [#137273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137273), [related report #124244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/124244). Thanks to [LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [jpazvd](https://github.com/jpazvd).
  - Sessions using a symlinked state folder remain usable after a Gateway restart without changing stored paths. [Symlinked session state](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168361). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Activity refreshes blank previews after archived conversation history is restored. [Restored conversation previews](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168397). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Completed transcript searches no longer show an indexing warning just because an unchanged database writer initialized. [Transcript search readiness](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168550). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Accept valid worker transcript bytes from another JavaScript realm so affected saved-session history can load. [#168915](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168915), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce repeated session database reads during turns and retain managed-image attachments if an exact session read fails. [#169053](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169053), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Limit database and foreground transcript worker pools to reduce memory pressure on high-core hosts, and expose worker counts by script. [#169110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169110), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore cached-row invalidation after a failed database writer discovery followed by a successful write. [#169113](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169113), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recover session reads overtaken by committed writes so a completed agent reply can return normally. [#169174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169174), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A session read retries once after a transient invalidation, allowing the turn or final reply to continue. A second invalidation still returns the error. [#169199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169199), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169181).
