Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@beru-ant-king](https://github.com/beru-ant-king).
  - Transcript rebuilding can reach affected sessions after compaction when a database revision is unknown. [#169467](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169467). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reduce acknowledgement work for messages sent to ready existing sessions, while accepting input durably before acknowledging it. [#169596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169596). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve attachment identity in transcript mirrors and accept completed-turn receipts across equivalent database paths. Older filename-only history cannot recover missing attachment identity. [#169710](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169710). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session creation avoids waiting on some unrelated database writes in [#170092](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170092). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Native transcript reads restore history when a read overlaps an archive in [#170096](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170096). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session repair and deletion skip archive work for empty transcripts while protecting later additions in [#170122](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170122). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Eligible active-session writes reuse resident transcript context to avoid redundant reads in [#170199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170199). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Eligible Gateway connections reuse resident profile facts and avoid an extra database worker read in [#170228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170228). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session observation saves digests and activity recaps with fewer writer requests. [PR #170271](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170271). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Resident session history avoids keeping duplicate transcript bodies and reduces append work. [PR #170304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170304). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Activity summaries can reuse a complete resident transcript across ordinary metadata entries, reducing worker reads in eligible warm sessions. [PR #170340](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170340). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Operators can enable SQLite worker timing at startup with `OPENCLAW_DIAGNOSTICS=sqlite.worker-timing`. Empty admission updates also avoid unnecessary bookkeeping. [PR #170235](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170235). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Connected `openclaw sessions cleanup --all-agents` waits for the Gateway result, and routine cleanup avoids redundant work. [#170618](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170618). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the fix and [personaltrainerkoichan](https://github.com/personaltrainerkoichan) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170474).
  - Idle maintenance uses fewer wakeups and database operations. While the healthy-idle delay histogram is paused, idle snapshots do not include a delay percentile, but callback stalls are still tracked. [#170531](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170531). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve already streamed text when the transcript writer is available, without saving unfinished tool calls ([#170697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170697)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retry configured session history cleanup after a blocked checkpoint recovers, while keeping history until a checkpoint completes ([#170913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170913)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce database reads during chat preparation ([#170745](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170745)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve an existing Gateway startup tracing setting across service reinstall or repair ([#170725](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170725), [#169808](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169808)). Thanks [@wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@hbgangcn1](https://github.com/hbgangcn1).
  - Park idle maintenance until database writes, authentication failures, or pairing activity require it ([#170755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170755)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid an unnecessary database statistics refresh after large history-only cleanups, reducing interruptions to session writes. [#170977](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170977), related [#170913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170913) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid copying existing string payloads on the Gateway event loop when rewriting a loaded transcript. [#171019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171019) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Speed up pending approval replay when many unrelated approvals are waiting. [#171047](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171047) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Release stale SQLite receipt and admission metadata when sessions are deleted or workers retire. [#171088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171088), related [#170855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170855) and [#171012](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171012) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let automatic session cleanup proceed during unrelated activity while rechecking the sessions selected for cleanup and their protections. [#171154](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171154) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retire idle transcript workers after five minutes to release memory after bursts of activity. A later read can incur a cold start. [#171155](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171155) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session cleanup makes fewer unproductive passes over protected entries and resumes when relevant protection ends. [#171211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171211), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Surplus shared-state readers retire after 30 idle seconds while one stays warm. [#171231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171231), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Compatible scoped SQLite reads reuse a bounded worker pool, reducing process churn for repeated reads. [#171299](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171299), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Idle maintenance uses fewer wakeups. Healthy non-authority directory watches recover missed events on a five-minute interval, while authority-sensitive and unhealthy watches retain their 30-second recovery interval. [#171318](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171318), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Empty maintenance inventories wait for relevant changes or deadlines instead of repeatedly querying the database. [#171320](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171320), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Larger installations avoid repeated full placement scans and share plugin inventory reads during skill discovery. [#171323](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171323), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Surplus transcript history and search workers retire after 30 idle seconds and restart when needed. [#171354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171354), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Repeated inference can reuse published authentication rows, avoiding redundant credential worker reads after session writes. [#171726](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171726) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway SQLite exchanges avoid repeatedly publishing unchanged admission facts. [#171788](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171788) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session settlement avoids repeatedly inspecting long conversation data. [#171850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171850) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Loading bounded transcript context avoids copying message data twice. [#171862](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171862) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Agent database workers no longer run large automatic checkpoints during transcript commits. Passive maintenance handles checkpointing. [#172009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172009) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Warm transcript writes avoid a transaction-held metadata wait when another session registers concurrently. [#172059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172059) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Full transcript reads decode once per event, reducing temporary JavaScript work on large histories. [#172112](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172112) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce the data serialized and transferred when appending to long durable sessions. [#172462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172462). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse a broker for isolated Gateway agent file reads while retaining their separate-process boundary. [#172476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172476). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Following logs avoids repeated scans of old log content on subsequent polls while retaining intended secret masking. [#172885](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172885), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@najef1979-code](https://github.com/najef1979-code) for the [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172507).
  - Closing or replacing a transcript database releases its cached history pages. [#172521](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172521), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Closing a history reader also clears cached reset-window references to its database. [#172541](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172541), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor preserves supported explicit session runtime choices, including in archived sessions. Choices already deleted cannot be reconstructed, and unavailable or invalid choices can still be cleared. [#173498](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173498), thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@s-salamatov](https://github.com/s-salamatov) for the [earlier report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/83098).
  - Session snapshot construction reuses validation of unchanged frozen session facts. [#172533](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172533), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Allow session resets to pass completed, inactive subagent history while still cleaning up unfinished work ([#179731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179731)). Thanks [@hidaba](https://github.com/hidaba) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce unnecessary provider dependencies retained by transcript history workers ([#181936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181936)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session-fork snapshots account for retained data more completely. Snapshots near the existing budget may be evicted sooner. [#182544](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182544) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Defer the remaining expired subagent cleanup backlog during shutdown and resume it after restart ([#185859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185859)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Chat recovery and copy-error controls remain usable when a conversation fails to load.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep keyboard focus on the copy button when draft recovery shows its copied confirmation. [#171591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171591) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show login recovery and its copy control while the chat route is still loading ([06afa0d9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/06afa0d926363318e1aa252ac9c019e75989aac2)).
  - Simplify the failed-reply card and make retry and error copying easier to find. [#172349](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172349). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Affected startup and first-message paths avoid more stalls and preparation races when large agent or session inventories are present.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Limits startup read bursts and retries temporary capacity rejections so healthy agents are less likely to remain degraded after a fleet starts. [#168638](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168638) by [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT), [@wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web), and [@karaaslanz](https://github.com/karaaslanz).
  - Move cold session and Memory preparation reads off the Gateway main thread. Before a Memory database exists, deep Memory status may incorrectly report an installed vector extension as unavailable. [#169164](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169164), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore unmanaged in-process restarts after configuration reload recovery and show the cause of lock reacquisition failures. [#169167](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169167), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session catalog loading can finish when a Unix login shell ignores termination during executable-path discovery. [#169507](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169507). Thanks [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Catalog refreshes reuse unchanged installed native ESM plugin modules, avoiding repeated retained copies. Replacing installed source requires a worker restart. [#169272](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169272), [earlier work](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162080), [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159662). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@mcooks-cmd](https://github.com/mcooks-cmd), [@svdinu-jpg](https://github.com/svdinu-jpg) and [@eduherranz](https://github.com/eduherranz).
  - Independent Gateway startup preparations run concurrently. [#169328](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169328). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Direct Gateway entries use build-scoped module compilation caches. [#169365](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169365). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway startup and reload yield between plugin registrations so queued requests can run. A single synchronous plugin can still block during its own registration. [#169519](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169519). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Overlap first-message model preparation with workspace baseline capture. [#169592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169592). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse accepted plugin migration facts on warm startup. [#169613](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169613). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse eligible SQLite validation after a clean shutdown, with full checks when the saved validation is ineligible. [#169649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169649). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prewarm the Gateway module graph for immutable updates before cutover. If ordinary prewarming fails, startup continues cold. [#169656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169656). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prevent slow native service inspection from consuming the network probe limit during Gateway readiness checks. [#169787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169787). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stop session preparation from looping on pinned harness sessions and blocking readiness or session listing. [#169821](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169821). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Conversation-saving failures show useful recovery guidance, including complete session-list summaries, in [#170048](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170048). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Usage totals can refresh after compaction, expected shutdown cancellations log more quietly, and memory indexing avoids loading session storage unnecessarily during shutdown. [PR #170239](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170239). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Control UI session requests under a probe-prefixed custom base path wait for startup instead of receiving a premature 403. Root probes and authentication retain their usual behavior. [PR #170316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170316). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Deferred agent-database preparation no longer deadlocks Gateway startup with worker recovery. [PR #170348](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170348). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway startup overlaps independent preparation and loads WebSocket connection modules before reporting readiness. [PR #170401](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170401). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A first chat turn can create a missing configured session store and continue to agent and child execution. [#170464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170464). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A retired Usage refresh executor no longer causes the affected new-session first turn to be refused. [#170572](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170572). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The default Gateway handshake deadline is 30 seconds, giving busy hosts more time to connect. Repeated connections can also avoid unnecessary owner-profile work. [#170621](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170621). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the replacement runtime available for agent runs and inter-session requests after overlapping startup refreshes ([#170877](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170877), [#170762](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170762)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
  - Keep usage inventory and placement reconciliation running, retain media for sessions with oversized history messages, and report protected external installations as host-managed. Native updates still require adoption ([#170905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170905)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Move the initial fleet skills scan after readiness while keeping on-demand workspace skill preparation available. [#170933](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170933) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Run independent session inspections together during startup. [#171006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171006), related [#170849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170849) and [#170933](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170933) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Start warming session projections while the Gateway transport starts, keeping the existing readiness checks. [#171028](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171028) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse a valid clean-close database seal across compatible builds after the first validation with the updated seal. [#171038](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171038) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Skip unnecessary child-session recovery work and limit startup task data when many unrelated sessions are retained. [#171046](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171046) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recover agent sessions when an overlapping config or secrets refresh replaces startup preparation. [#171054](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171054), [report #171018](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171018), related [#150755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150755) and [#165107](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165107). Thanks to [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@homeworld520](https://github.com/homeworld520).
  - Return a readiness failure while a required agent database is still preparing, with a diagnostic for the affected agent. [#171063](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171063) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Worker compile caches are persisted during activity to reduce cold-cache shutdown work. Synchronous writes can delay early work on slow storage. [#171298](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171298), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Foreground Gateway startup avoids synchronous main-thread compile-cache flush stalls while workers retain caching. [#171358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171358), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Standalone agent turns using workspace skills prepare their runtime configuration before reading it. [#171209](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171209), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recover a Gateway startup that has not bound its listener for over five minutes with `openclaw gateway stop`, then retry startup. [#171678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171678) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks to [TommyLei666](https://github.com/TommyLei666) for [reporting the issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171513).
  - Large fleets avoid repeated directory preparation and scheduling work. [#171805](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171805) addresses the [fleet report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171700), incorporating work from [#171778](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171778). The separate [SQLite store-capacity issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162497) remains. Thanks to [Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [chelsealong](https://github.com/chelsealong), and [609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT).
  - Bun Gateway startup handles SQLite admission timeouts without treating a worker's clock deadline as already expired. [#172756](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172756), thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Docker healthchecks honor `OPENCLAW_GATEWAY_PORT` when configuration loading fails and no live lock supplies the port. [#172765](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172765), thanks [@xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Gateway startup follows replacement model preparation when an overlapping refresh cancels older work. Actual replacement failures still surface. [#173366](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173366), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Jackten](https://github.com/Jackten) for the [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/173357).
  - Reduce filesystem work during plugin source capture, which can ease startup and first-turn delays depending on the workload ([#180895](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180895)). Thanks [@Roddmason](https://github.com/Roddmason), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@n572770](https://github.com/n572770).
  - Keep first-session creation working while a usage refresh waits for its lock ([6d479f5c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6d479f5c893a687e92683b7e896be26e4c8bedd3)).

  </details>

- Stop and shutdown settle more owned worker and reader cleanup without waiting on processes that have already exited. Unconfirmed cleanup still needs inspection.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Lets Gateway shutdown drain retained worker tunnels and transfers when the worker inventory has already retired. [#168862](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168862) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [@Captain69G](https://github.com/Captain69G).
  - Idle Gateways stop periodic plugin-state expiry sweeps. Expired values remain unreadable, while inactive namespaces can retain expired rows on disk until a later write. [#169524](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169524). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stall diagnostics add callback labels and a bounded trailing CPU sample on supported Node 26 processes. The sample describes recent activity rather than identifying one callback as the cause. [#169535](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169535). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid retaining empty request activity records and length-specific cumulative tool metric series. Exact parameter lengths remain on tool spans. [#169786](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169786). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let an admitted wake callback finish under its captured handler, and start listeners added during an event with the next event. [#169648](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169648). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway shutdown and restart finish their lifecycle records before shared database workers close in [#169983](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169983). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Idle Gateways defer unobserved health, GitHub discovery, and upstream checks until needed. [PR #170389](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170389). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway shutdown waits for accepted session updates to finish preparation before closing connected clients. Existing client delivery checks still apply. [#170555](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170555). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Completed and failed operations release owned resources more consistently, including the OpenRouter sign-in listener and Tlon HTTP resources. [#170439](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170439). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor and startup inspections release temporary plugin captures, and rejected file-lock handoffs release the acquired lock. [#170530](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170530). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stalled transcript indexing receives a five-second grace during Gateway shutdown before cancellation. Saved transcripts and required cleanup remain protected, and unfinished indexing can be rediscovered after restart. The grace is not a total shutdown deadline. See the [restart recovery guide](/gateway/restart-recovery). [#170493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170493). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169777), and [O96a](https://github.com/O96a) for shutdown advice.
  - Ordinary shutdown gives execution cleanup time to release worktrees, and a warning-only Codex profile check no longer holds up startup. [#171308](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171308), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - After a validated external drain, supervised restarts skip repeated waits and settle queued work while preserving restart recovery and final transcript writes. [#171399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171399), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Drain status reports blockers promptly, and the deployment controller can retry transient transport failures within its original deadline. The changed controller executable and library need to be adopted together, and a failed poll does not authorize interruption. Gateway hello also no longer waits for watched-session membership preparation. [#171442](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171442), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Finish shutdown sooner when attached command trees have exited. The plugin SDK process runtime also exposes a latched `exited()` check on the process-tree handle. [#171725](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171725) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Clean up streaming observation when a client disconnects while authorization is pending. [#171713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171713) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prevent an early Gateway exit when an explicit Node heap budget is combined with `--exit-on-contradictory-flags`. Workers inherit the configured ceiling in this diagnostic mode. [#171490](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171490) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Busy shutdown cancels in-flight worker preparation while preserving recovery state. [#171843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171843) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway stall monitoring removes process-wide asynchronous hook overhead. Task labels now cover synchronous scopes, and some stalls during healthy idle periods may go unreported. [#172531](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172531), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Foreground transcript readers release database resources during cleanup, addressing a Windows cleanup hang. [#172506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172506), thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
  - Inspect macOS processes natively for supervisor cleanup and Codex containment, including a bounded recheck when an exiting process is briefly unavailable. Migrate the process-runtime SDK from `DarwinProcess.uid` to `owner`. [#182215](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182215), combining [#174147](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174147), [#174284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174284), [#175368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175368), [#176576](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176576) and [#176612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176612). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve file-backed standard input when a service-child supervision anchor retires. [#187804](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187804). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Keep orphaned-process cleanup on elapsed time so clock changes do not shorten or extend its shutdown grace period ([#181335](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181335)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Keep spawn-broker child cleanup and confirmation deadlines stable across system-clock changes ([#181360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181360)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Honor a stop signal during restart drain after the existing cleanup completes ([#186055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186055)). Thanks to [@freeqaz](https://github.com/freeqaz).
  - Deliver confirmed service-child cancellation exits to existing and late exit listeners ([7fdc3dcd](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7fdc3dcd45be661e4e032281af7371a5dd415cbb)).
  - Avoid duplicate recurring maintenance runs under clock drift, including the exact half-period boundary. [Scheduler fix](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/344b71032447521feaff383f619667801dff6d83) and [boundary follow-up](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/741a6d2cf6e791c26b194d500fdf4708ec0abce4), thanks to [Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
  - Avoid transient Skills worker filesystem watches that could delay shutdown, observed on macOS. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a6491a1b37e138700d9db52e8ee9bafcebe9466a)
  - Close retained agent directory watchers during shared-state shutdown. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7e4c0f7378ea285e405bdb9cfca3207d2529d30f)
  - Close idle SQLite readers before broker shutdown to avoid an extra wait during Linux Gateway shutdown and restart. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/075dc2f6777726c52c808228f8dd43ee746d81e4)
  - Reduce potential memory retention when a scheduler scope closes after its parent has stopped. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d8af435b5ba1495f35f5df6a48b740dd2029d77a)
  - End pending device scope-upgrade waits as interruptions when the Gateway scheduler shuts down. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0dc75157e1b1f9abfafd661b1e16276990c0f649)
  - Preserve empty arguments and the positions of later arguments in generated systemd service commands. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/82600d8d4828223dcad244612312d30d69d9763a), thanks to [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) and [gideonpraise](https://github.com/gideonpraise).
  - Restore structured cleanup duration, escalation timing and signal failure fields for tools inspecting service-child cleanup errors. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/edd07a352505e6443c853f835b78a2ab6a57e9c9), thanks to [romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).

  </details>

- Helpers can return valid nested results without being rejected as invalid, and exhausted retries are reported instead of treating later calls as completed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Accept schema-valid objects or arrays encoded as nested JSON strings in collector results. [#150209](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150209), [reported in #150167](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/150167). Thanks to [chelsealong](https://github.com/chelsealong), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [residentb2b](https://github.com/residentb2b).
  - Report when a collector has exhausted its structured-output retries instead of presenting the previous schema error as a new validation result. Later calls are not evaluated after the budget is spent ([#181515](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181515), [#181442](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181442)). Thanks [@sukhdeepjohar](https://github.com/sukhdeepjohar), [@oldrich333](https://github.com/oldrich333) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Affected Bun, browser and container setups give clearer supported requirements. The proposed release cutoff requires Bun 1.4.3 or newer; existing read-only Kubernetes deployments need a writable cache with the image update.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Include the missing workspace manifest in Docker production dependency builds ([a2d159ca](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a2d159cadcaf384fc6f7f4d84a7dc785e0fa401d)).
  - The documented Control UI baseline is Safari 26.2 or later, Chrome 147 or later, and Firefox 150 or later. The Node CLI and Gateway requirements are unchanged. [Platform requirements](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168595), [platform plan](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168114). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Package-only create, validate, build, dev and inspect commands remain available while the Gateway runs. Offline removal of adopted Claw ownership keeps the original agent and workspace. [Installed Claw state ownership](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168586). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Bun startup and package installation require version 1.4.3 or newer. The pinned OpenClaw Bun fork remains unchanged. [#171627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171627) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Add a writable cache to the deployment recipe to address startup failures on a read-only root filesystem. [#172377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172377), reported in [#172355](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172355). Thanks to [@saftall](https://github.com/saftall) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Add a Bun-based standalone artifact builder and packaging manifest. [#172384](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172384). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Local source-checkout tests and development use a checksum-verified pinned Bun fork by default on supported platforms. Set `OPENCLAW_VITEST_RUNTIME=node` to force Node. CI retains its separate runtime selection. [#172625](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172625), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Baseline setup routes supported changes through the running Gateway and seeds workspace files on the agent's first run. [#185109](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185109) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Covered secret and workspace-restricted tool paths enforce their intended boundaries. Raw local logs can still contain credentials even when console output and diagnostic exports are masked.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Protect the active environment variable used by a whole-reference hook token from Gateway-hosted exec children, including across hook reloads ([#179199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179199)). Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc). The broader credential-inheritance report came from [@nierob-cmd](https://github.com/nierob-cmd) ([#178368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/178368)).
  - Closing MCP Relay's Connected apps view releases its rendered grant rows and prevents detached controls or pending pairing replies from acting on the closed view. [#184213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184213) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Workspace-restricted Gateway tools require approval for persistent delegated changes and reject local media reads outside the allowed roots when filesystem policy was omitted. [#185094](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185094) Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - Security audit follows linked workspace skills roots when checking for nested symlink escapes. [#182384](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182384) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@dineshsuthar123](https://github.com/dineshsuthar123) for the report.
  - Matching Google OAuth client secrets are masked in console and log-tail output. Raw local file logs may still contain them. [#183193](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183193) Thanks [@leizhenzhang](https://github.com/leizhenzhang) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks [@osher86](https://github.com/osher86) for the report.
  - Preserve workspace policy for Gateway-created delegate and media tools ([#187307](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187307)). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - Local file logging and WebSocket summaries change their handling of sensitive content in [#170023](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170023). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Authorized operators can inspect and revoke standing grants when approval history is unavailable, provided the grants service is working.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Clarify expired and cancelled approvals and unconfirmed application of an approved setup change. [#172270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172270). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The Approvals page loads standing grants independently of approval history, so authorized operators can still see and revoke them. [#183769](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183769) Thanks [@AmelioMansour](https://github.com/AmelioMansour) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Routine logs avoid repeated warnings about already-recorded history problems. The quieter warning does not restore previously blocked conversation turns.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Previously blocked durable context turns are not recovered by this fix. [Steered conversation context](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168394), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168014). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@robertoronderosjr](https://github.com/robertoronderosjr).
  - Stop repeating warnings for historical blocked context-engine turns on subsequent session runs. The blocked records remain in place. [#167595](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167595), [related reports #163566](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/163566) and [#135628](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/135628). Thanks to [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman), [Clode2026](https://github.com/Clode2026), and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Legacy subagent authority warnings appear once per run per process instead of repeating on every recovery sweep in [#170120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170120). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Warn once per unchanged oversized bootstrap file, revision and limit while keeping truncation details available in `/context`. [#171074](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171074) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Log routine per-connection identity scope grants at info and include the authentication method. Warn-only filters no longer retain these records. [#171144](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171144) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Move successful Gateway RPC responses under one second to debug, keeping failures and slower calls visible at info. [#171150](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171150) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Diagnostics exports keep the chosen destination, and patch views preserve meaningful whitespace.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserve whitespace in nonblank, non-home destinations for Gateway diagnostics and stability exports ([#181775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181775)). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve trailing whitespace and the final newline in Diffs patch viewers ([#181674](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181674)). Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Local model verification rejects pipe-backed artifacts without hanging, while continuing to support ordinary files and links.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - ONNX verification rejects pipe-backed manifests and model artifacts without hanging. Regular files and links to them remain supported. [#183621](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183621) Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Selected reasoning visibility and model controls stay consistent through more active turns and saved-history views.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Show incoming reasoning during generation and hand it off to the saved transcript without a duplicate when the relevant reasoning view is enabled. [#117721](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/117721), [#88079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/88079), [#163567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/163567). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - With View → Reasoning enabled, each tool step can retain its preceding reasoning. Saved reasoning replaces previews in `on` mode, while `stream` previews disappear when the run ends. [PR #168898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168898). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@leapdragon](https://github.com/leapdragon).
  - Hide an unsupported context percentage when the selected model's capacity is unknown, while retaining available provider plan usage. [#169341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169341) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Chat shows fallback and return dividers and hides recovered same-run diagnostic errors. A named fallback candidate may have been skipped. [#169773](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169773), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
  - Compact context ring beside the model picker, with its usage details retained ([#170370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170370)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Respect the configured reasoning policy when showing session thinking options for runtime-routed models. [#170589](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170589), [issue #170528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170528). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for the fix and [@EmeraldPi](https://github.com/EmeraldPi) for the report.
  - Retain session thinking labels and options when a later catalog row omits the model. [#170659](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170659) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the effort slider available during a model catalog retry when the retained selected model is enabled ([commit 66c4b4a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/66c4b4a524dfcf571f1c529919aa84d668636266)).
  - Show the applicable agent verbosity in Control UI and local TUI history, with session overrides taking precedence ([#182059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182059)). Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Fixes formatting loss in affected YAML edits and failures handling large patches or files on unusual storage.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Bun workspace helpers can run inline code without a false path-length error, while other workspace paths remain checked. [#171970](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171970) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - OC Path can edit YAML entries with numeric, boolean, or null keys without changing neighboring entries. The accompanying plugin consolidation removes safeguards associated with the earlier Diffs reports [#83914](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/83914) and [#83917](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/83917). Unexpected card hydration errors now stop later cards, and packaged viewer asset changes require a plugin reload. [#171957](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171957) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update filesystem handling for affected Windows 9p mounts and Android/Termux temporary workspaces. [#172306](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172306), related to [#145589](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/145589) and [#171406](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171406). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Hawk5150](https://github.com/Hawk5150), [@iiAnthony](https://github.com/iiAnthony), [@jaisummagithub](https://github.com/jaisummagithub) and [@kukirin-ui](https://github.com/kukirin-ui).
  - Agents can apply very large file patch hunks without hitting the JavaScript argument-limit crash. [#174477](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174477). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Paired-computer setup keeps the selected profile and approved device through more retries, with clearer choices for enabling session hosting.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserve the selected profile or container in QR setup's device-list and approval commands. [#178878](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178878). Thanks to [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Enrollment suggests an explicitly chosen background session-host service, keeps a command-only option, and explains how existing paired devices can enable hosting ([#168623](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168623), [session hosting guide](/nodes/session-hosting)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Match generated machine join commands to the Gateway release and offer an installed-command alternative, with matching-build guidance for development or unpublished builds ([#168680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168680)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve paired-device credentials on reconnect and support explicit environment authentication ([#168653](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168653)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Accept copied node pairing request IDs with surrounding whitespace for approval or rejection ([#141286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141286)). Thanks to [hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen).
  - Reuse an approved pairing during node-only and cloud-worker bootstrap retries. [#168795](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168795) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Align device setup controls with the administrator requirement and retain eligible pairing approvals. [#170988](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170988) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Paired-computer services installed through npx use a durable package path and handle custom state folders more consistently.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep `npx`-installed node services outside npm's disposable cache and allow same-profile reinstall to repair or update them ([#168644](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168644)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep Linux node service PATH entries available after login-shell startup, ahead of profile entries ([#136677](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/136677), [report #134354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134354)). Thanks to [harjothkhara](https://github.com/harjothkhara), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [abacha](https://github.com/abacha).
  - Node service lifecycle commands work with custom state directories without a mistaken Gateway identity refusal. [#170403](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170403) by [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Node service status exposes runtime intent, and guarded install options can restore a retained Node or Bun CLI. [#170441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170441) by [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- A valid Incognito device choice survives an unrelated database-discovery cleanup failure.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserve a valid incognito placement result when database reader cleanup encounters an unreadable discovery candidate ([83579fdb](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/83579fdb473aafd4274b1677a10a77679de1b389)).

  </details>

- Muse Wonder and Spark expose their supported Standard and maximum-thinking choices.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Muse Wonder 1.0 Standard selection and max thinking for Wonder and Spark 1.3 Standard ([#170232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170232)). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Commands naming a paired device run there when no host is supplied; conflicting host and device targets are rejected.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Node-only exec routing and rejection of conflicting host and node arguments. [PR #172327](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172327), [issue #172260](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172260). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), reporter [yozakura-ava](https://github.com/yozakura-ava), and [jayswu](https://github.com/jayswu) for tracing the routing and advising on coverage.

  </details>

- Fixes false approval denials and tool-start failures in affected concurrent CLI-backed requests.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Authorized concurrent host commands avoid false approval denials when another command only records allowlist usage. [PR #171143](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171143). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - CLI-backed agents avoid a local connection reuse race that could fail a tool call before it started. Long-running responses remain supported. [PR #172980](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172980), [issue #172871](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172871). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
  - Gateway-owned session ID lookup and creation for agent requests. [PR #172110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172110). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Publish PR tells you whether credentials, verification or the workspace is blocking publication.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Distinguish credentials, verification and workspace problems in Publish PR, [#168223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168223). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Eligible existing workspaces and delegated work retain permitted GitHub publication through more handoffs, without granting new publication authority.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Publishing an existing workspace no longer separately requires authorization to run an agent, while session and caller checks still apply, [#168951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168951). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Own-session PR publishing after a delayed verified profile attachment in [#169400](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169400), addressing [#169377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169377). Thanks [@Gigaxel](https://github.com/Gigaxel) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - New managed children retain permitted GitHub publication for eligible worktrees, subject to inherited restrictions and Gateway checks. [#171842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171842), addressing [#171782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171782). Thanks to [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
  - Avoid unnecessary publication refusals during overlapping requests. [#172351](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172351). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Watched session PR refreshes and GitHub publication avoid redundant Git calls. [#172498](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172498), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - ClickClack discussion lookup and shared GitHub publication receipt discovery avoid duplicate local reads. [#172537](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172537), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Accepted PR publication can wait through a temporary session-access interruption instead of requiring a duplicate request.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep accepted publication requests pending during temporary session authority outages ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7f98077b2e30dd5b0c2aa271b9934f6414a106dc)). Thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
